File: src/lib/backup/crypto.ts

import crypto from 'crypto';

// マジックヘッダー: 14バイト ASCII
export const BACKUP_MAGIC_HEADER = 'WELFARE_ENC_V1';
const HEADER_BUFFER = Buffer.from(BACKUP_MAGIC_HEADER, 'utf-8'); // 14 bytes
const SALT_LENGTH = 16;
const IV_LENGTH = 12; // GCM推奨 96-bit
const TAG_LENGTH = 16; // GCM推奨 128-bit
const PBKDF2_ITERATIONS = 100000;
const KEY_LENGTH = 32; // 256 bits

// SQLite3のマジックヘッダー: "SQLite format 3\0" (16 bytes)
export const SQLITE_HEADER = Buffer.from('SQLite format 3\0', 'utf-8');

/**
 * パスフレーズを用いてバッファをAES-256-GCMで暗号化します。
 * 出力フォーマット:
 * [14B HEADER][16B SALT][12B IV][16B AUTH_TAG][CIPHERTEXT...]
 */
export function encryptBuffer(buffer: Buffer, passphrase: string): Buffer {
  if (!passphrase || passphrase.length < 8) {
    throw new Error('暗号化パスフレーズは8文字以上である必要があります。');
  }

  // ランダムなSaltとIVを生成
  const salt = crypto.randomBytes(SALT_LENGTH);
  const iv = crypto.randomBytes(IV_LENGTH);

  // PBKDF2による256ビット鍵の導出 (SHA-256, 100,000回ストレッチング)
  const key = crypto.pbkdf2Sync(passphrase, salt, PBKDF2_ITERATIONS, KEY_LENGTH, 'sha256');

  // AES-256-GCM 暗号化初期化
  const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv('aes-256-gcm', key, iv);
  
  // AAD (Additional Authenticated Data) としてヘッダーをバインド
  cipher.setAAD(HEADER_BUFFER);

  const encrypted = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(buffer), cipher.final()]);
  const authTag = cipher.getAuthTag();

  // ヘッダー + Salt + IV + AuthTag + 暗号化データを結合
  return Buffer.concat([
    HEADER_BUFFER,
    salt,
    iv,
    authTag,
    encrypted
  ]);
}

/**
 * 暗号化されたバッファのヘッダーが正当か検証します。
 */
export function validateBackupHeader(buffer: Buffer): boolean {
  const minLength = HEADER_BUFFER.length + SALT_LENGTH + IV_LENGTH + TAG_LENGTH;
  if (!buffer || buffer.length < minLength) {
    return false;
  }
  const fileHeader = buffer.subarray(0, HEADER_BUFFER.length);
  return fileHeader.equals(HEADER_BUFFER);
}

/**
 * バッファがSQLite3データベースファイルであるか検証します。
 */
export function isSqliteBuffer(buffer: Buffer): boolean {
  if (!buffer || buffer.length < SQLITE_HEADER.length) {
    return false;
  }
  const fileHeader = buffer.subarray(0, SQLITE_HEADER.length);
  return fileHeader.equals(SQLITE_HEADER);
}

/**
 * AES-256-GCMで暗号化されたバッファを復号します。
 * パスフレーズの誤り、改ざん、ファイル破損がある場合は例外をスローします。
 */
export function decryptBuffer(encryptedBuffer: Buffer, passphrase: string): Buffer {
  if (!passphrase) {
    throw new Error('復号用のパスフレーズを入力してください。');
  }

  if (!validateBackupHeader(encryptedBuffer)) {
    throw new Error('無効なバックアップファイルです。当システムの暗号化バックアップ形式ではありません。');
  }

  let offset = 0;
  // ヘッダーをスキップ
  offset += HEADER_BUFFER.length;

  // Salt
  const salt = encryptedBuffer.subarray(offset, offset + SALT_LENGTH);
  offset += SALT_LENGTH;

  // IV
  const iv = encryptedBuffer.subarray(offset, offset + IV_LENGTH);
  offset += IV_LENGTH;

  // AuthTag
  const authTag = encryptedBuffer.subarray(offset, offset + TAG_LENGTH);
  offset += TAG_LENGTH;

  // 暗号文本体
  const ciphertext = encryptedBuffer.subarray(offset);

  // 鍵導出
  const key = crypto.pbkdf2Sync(passphrase, salt, PBKDF2_ITERATIONS, KEY_LENGTH, 'sha256');

  // 復号
  try {
    const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv('aes-256-gcm', key, iv);
    decipher.setAAD(HEADER_BUFFER);
    decipher.setAuthTag(authTag);

    const decrypted = Buffer.concat([decipher.update(ciphertext), decipher.final()]);
    return decrypted;
  } catch (err: any) {
    throw new Error('復号に失敗しました。パスフレーズが間違っているか、ファイルが破損・改ざんされている可能性があります。');
  }
}

File: src/lib/backup/s3Client.ts

import crypto from 'crypto';

export interface S3UploadOptions {
  endpoint: string; // 例: https://s3.ap-northeast-1.amazonaws.com または https://<account_id>.r2.cloudflarestorage.com
  bucket: string;
  region?: string;
  accessKeyId: string;
  secretAccessKey: string;
  key: string; // オブジェクトキー（ファイル名など）
  body: Buffer;
  contentType?: string;
}

function hmac(key: Buffer | string, data: string): Buffer {
  return crypto.createHmac('sha256', key).update(data, 'utf-8').digest();
}

function hash(data: Buffer | string): string {
  return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(data).digest('hex');
}

/**
 * AWS Signature Version 4 (SigV4) を使用して、
 * S3互換ストレージ (AWS S3, Cloudflare R2, MinIO, Wasabi等) にオブジェクトをPUTアップロードします。
 * 追加ライブラリ不要で、Node.js標準のcryptoとfetchのみで動作します。
 */
export async function uploadToS3(options: S3UploadOptions): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string; httpStatus?: number }> {
  const {
    endpoint,
    bucket,
    region = 'auto',
    accessKeyId,
    secretAccessKey,
    key,
    body,
    contentType = 'application/octet-stream'
  } = options;

  if (!endpoint || !bucket || !accessKeyId || !secretAccessKey) {
    throw new Error('S3接続設定（エンドポイント、バケット名、アクセスキー、シークレットキー）が不足しています。');
  }

  // URLとホスト名の解析
  let cleanEndpoint = endpoint.trim().replace(/\/+$/, '');
  if (!cleanEndpoint.startsWith('http://') && !cleanEndpoint.startsWith('https://')) {
    cleanEndpoint = 'https://' + cleanEndpoint;
  }

  const urlObj = new URL(cleanEndpoint);
  // パス形式か仮想ホスト形式か（汎用的にパス形式またはURLにバケットを付与）
  // Cloudflare R2やAWS S3等に対応: https://endpoint/bucket/key
  const fullUrl = `${cleanEndpoint}/${bucket}/${encodeURIComponent(key).replace(/%2F/g, '/')}`;
  const requestUrl = new URL(fullUrl);

  const host = requestUrl.host;
  const path = requestUrl.pathname;

  const now = new Date();
  const amzDate = now.toISOString().replace(/[:-]|\.\d{3}/g, ''); // YYYYMMDDTHHmmssZ
  const dateStamp = amzDate.substring(0, 8); // YYYYMMDD

  const payloadHash = hash(body);

  // Canonical Headers (アルファベット昇順、小文字)
  const canonicalHeaders = 
    `content-type:${contentType}\n` +
    `host:${host}\n` +
    `x-amz-content-sha256:${payloadHash}\n` +
    `x-amz-date:${amzDate}\n`;

  const signedHeaders = 'content-type;host;x-amz-content-sha256;x-amz-date';

  // Canonical Request
  const canonicalRequest = [
    'PUT',
    path,
    '', // query string
    canonicalHeaders,
    signedHeaders,
    payloadHash
  ].join('\n');

  // String to Sign
  const service = 's3';
  const credentialScope = `${dateStamp}/${region}/${service}/aws4_request`;
  const stringToSign = [
    'AWS4-HMAC-SHA256',
    amzDate,
    credentialScope,
    hash(canonicalRequest)
  ].join('\n');

  // 署名鍵の導出
  const kDate = hmac('AWS4' + secretAccessKey, dateStamp);
  const kRegion = hmac(kDate, region);
  const kService = hmac(kRegion, service);
  const kSigning = hmac(kService, 'aws4_request');

  // 署名計算
  const signature = crypto.createHmac('sha256', kSigning).update(stringToSign, 'utf-8').digest('hex');

  // Authorization ヘッダー
  const authorizationHeader = `AWS4-HMAC-SHA256 Credential=${accessKeyId}/${credentialScope}, SignedHeaders=${signedHeaders}, Signature=${signature}`;

  try {
    const res = await fetch(requestUrl.toString(), {
      method: 'PUT',
      headers: {
        'Host': host,
        'Content-Type': contentType,
        'x-amz-date': amzDate,
        'x-amz-content-sha256': payloadHash,
        'Authorization': authorizationHeader
      },
      body: new Uint8Array(body)
    });

    if (res.ok) {
      return { success: true, message: 'S3へのアップロードに成功しました。', httpStatus: res.status };
    } else {
      const errorText = await res.text();
      return {
        success: false,
        message: `S3エラー (${res.status} ${res.statusText}): ${errorText.slice(0, 200)}`,
        httpStatus: res.status
      };
    }
  } catch (err: any) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: `S3通信エラー: ${err.message || '接続に失敗しました'}`
    };
  }
}

File: src/lib/billing/burdenCalculator.ts
================================================================================

export const COPAY_RATE = 0.1; // 原則1割負担（障害福祉サービス法定自己負担率）

export interface BurdenInput {
  payment_limit: number;
  our_total_cost: number; // 自事業所の総費用（10割分）
  other_total_cost: number; // 他事業所の総費用（10割分）
  is_manager?: boolean; // 自事業所が上限額管理事業所であるか（デフォルト: true）
}

export interface BurdenResult {
  total_cost: number;
  total_burden: number; // 1割負担の本来の合計額
  our_burden: number; // 当事業所の最終的な請求額（自己負担分）
  other_burden: number; // 他事業所の負担額（通知用）
  is_over_limit: boolean; // 上限管理が発生したか
}

/**
 * 複数事業所利用時の利用者負担額を按分計算するロジック
 * 
 * 国保連の上限額管理仕様:
 * - 1割負担額が上限月額を超過する場合、各事業所の総費用に応じて按分
 * - 端数（1円単位）が発生した場合、指定された「上限管理事業所」が端数を引き受ける
 */
export function calculateBurden(input: BurdenInput): BurdenResult {
  const { payment_limit, our_total_cost, other_total_cost } = input;
  
  const total_cost = our_total_cost + other_total_cost;
  // 原則1割負担
  const total_burden = Math.floor(total_cost * COPAY_RATE);
  
  let our_burden = 0;
  let other_burden = 0;
  let is_over_limit = false;

  if (total_burden <= payment_limit) {
    // 上限到達していない場合、自事業所の総費用の1割をそのまま請求
    our_burden = Math.floor(our_total_cost * COPAY_RATE);
    other_burden = Math.floor(other_total_cost * COPAY_RATE);
  } else {
    // 上限を超過した場合、按分計算（比例配分）
    is_over_limit = true;
    if (total_cost > 0) {
      // 各事業所の按分額 = 上限月額 × (各事業所の総費用 / 全体の総費用)
      our_burden = Math.floor((payment_limit * our_total_cost) / total_cost);
      other_burden = Math.floor((payment_limit * other_total_cost) / total_cost);
      
      // 端数調整: 切り捨てにより合計が上限月額に満たない場合、
      // 上限管理事業所（is_manager）が端数を引き受ける（未指定時は費用比較）
      const remainder = payment_limit - (our_burden + other_burden);
      if (remainder > 0) {
        const isManager = input.is_manager ?? (our_total_cost >= other_total_cost);
        if (isManager) {
          our_burden += remainder;
        } else {
          other_burden += remainder;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // バリデーション: 計算結果が上限額を上回っていないか安全チェック
  if (our_burden > payment_limit) {
    console.error("バリデーションエラー: 按分結果が上限月額を超過しています", { our_burden, payment_limit });
    our_burden = payment_limit; // 最終防衛線
  }

  return {
    total_cost,
    total_burden,
    our_burden,
    other_burden,
    is_over_limit
  };
}

File: src/lib/billing/kokuhoCsvParser.ts
================================================================================

export interface KokuhoErrorRecord {
  errorCode: string;
  targetName: string;
  errorMessage: string;
  lineNo: number;
}

/**
 * 国保連から返戻されるエラーCSV（75100000.CSV等）を解析する
 * 今回はプロトタイプのため、単純なカンマ区切り（エラーコード, 対象者, メッセージ）を想定
 */
export function parseKokuhoErrorCsv(csvText: string): KokuhoErrorRecord[] {
  // CR, LF, CRLF のすべてに対応して行に分割し、空行を除外
  const lines = csvText.split(/\r?\n/).filter(line => line.trim() !== '');
  const records: KokuhoErrorRecord[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    const cols: string[] = [];
    let currentField = '';
    let inQuotes = false;
    
    for (let char of lines[i]) {
      if (char === '"') {
        inQuotes = !inQuotes;
      } else if (char === ',' && !inQuotes) {
        cols.push(currentField.trim());
        currentField = '';
      } else {
        currentField += char;
      }
    }
    cols.push(currentField.trim());
    
    // プロトタイプ想定のフォーマット: エラーコード, 対象者名, エラーメッセージ
    if (cols.length >= 3) {
      records.push({
        lineNo: i + 1,
        errorCode: cols[0],
        targetName: cols[1],
        errorMessage: cols[2]
      });
    } else {
      // 想定外のフォーマットへのフォールバック
      records.push({
        lineNo: i + 1,
        errorCode: 'UNKNOWN',
        targetName: '-',
        errorMessage: cols.join(',')
      });
    }
  }

  return records;
}

File: src/lib/billing/kokurenCsvService.ts

/**
 * 国民健康保険中央会（国保連）電子請求システム インターフェース仕様書 準拠
 * 障害者総合支援法（A型:43, B型:44, GH:33）および 児童福祉法（放課後等デイサービス:63）
 * 国保連請求伝送用 CSV 生成サービス
 */

import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { getDbConnection } from '@/lib/db-client';
import { getAreaUnitCost } from '@/lib/constants/areaUnitCostMaster';
import iconv from 'iconv-lite';
import { 
  isAdditionEnabled, 
  getAdditionValue, 
  checkAutomaticReductions,
  ServiceTypeKey as ComplianceServiceKey
} from '@/lib/compliance/facilityAdditionsManager';

export type ServiceTypeKey = 'a' | 'b' | 'gh' | 'houday';

export interface KokurenCsvResult {
  serviceKey: ServiceTypeKey;
  serviceCode: string;
  facilityNumber: string;
  facilityName: string;
  targetMonth: string;
  recordCount: number;
  totalUnits: number;
  totalBillingAmount: number;
  totalBurdenAmount: number;
  csvText: string;
  shiftJisBuffer: Buffer;
}

/**
 * 安全なCSVセル値のエスケープ（ダブルクォーテーション囲み、カンマ・改行対応）
 */
export function formatCsvField(val: any, isString = false): string {
  if (val === null || val === undefined) {
    return isString ? '""' : '0';
  }
  const str = String(val).trim();
  if (isString) {
    return `"${str.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
  }
  return str;
}

/**
 * 文字列を Shift-JIS (CP932) Buffer に変換
 */
export function encodeToShiftJisBuffer(text: string): Buffer {
  return iconv.encode(text, 'CP932');
}

/**
 * =========================================================================
 * 1. 就労継続支援A型 (サービス種類コード: 43) 国保連伝送CSV生成
 * =========================================================================
 */
export function generateKokurenCsvForTypeA(targetMonth: string): KokurenCsvResult {
  const db = getDbConnection('type_a');
  return generateWorkServiceCsv(db, 'a', '43', targetMonth);
}

/**
 * =========================================================================
 * 2. 就労継続支援B型 (サービス種類コード: 44) 国保連伝送CSV生成
 * =========================================================================
 */
export function generateKokurenCsvForTypeB(targetMonth: string): KokurenCsvResult {
  const db = getDbConnection('type_b');
  return generateWorkServiceCsv(db, 'b', '44', targetMonth);
}

/**
 * 就労A型・B型 共通CSV生成エンジン
 */
function generateWorkServiceCsv(
  db: Database.Database,
  serviceKey: 'a' | 'b',
  serviceCode: '43' | '44',
  targetMonth: string
): KokurenCsvResult {
  const facility = (db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM facility_settings WHERE id = 'default'`).get() as any) || {};
  const facilityNumber = String(facility.facility_number || '2810099999').padStart(10, '0');
  const facilityName = facility.facility_name || (serviceKey === 'a' ? '就労継続支援A型事業所' : '就労継続支援B型事業所');
  const defaultCityCode = String(facility.city_code || '28100').padStart(6, '0');
  const areaUnitCost = getAreaUnitCost(facility.region_class);

  const monthStr = targetMonth.replace('-', ''); // YYYYMM
  const now = new Date();
  const createdDateStr = `${now.getFullYear()}${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;

  // 利用者ごとの勤怠集計
  const attendanceDays = db.prepare(`
    SELECT client_id, 
           SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'present' AND COALESCE(is_offsite, 0) = 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as present_days,
           SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'out_facility' OR (status = 'present' AND is_offsite = 1) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as out_facility_days,
           SUM(CASE WHEN is_absence_addition = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as absence_addition_days,
           SUM(CASE WHEN meal_provided = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as meal_days,
           SUM(CASE 
             WHEN (COALESCE(pickup_used, 0) + COALESCE(dropoff_used, 0)) > 0 
               THEN COALESCE(pickup_used, 0) + COALESCE(dropoff_used, 0)
             WHEN transportation_used = 1 THEN 1 
             ELSE 0 
           END) as transportation_trips
    FROM attendance_records
    WHERE record_date LIKE ? AND (status IN ('present', 'out_facility') OR is_absence_addition = 1) AND deleted_at IS NULL
    GROUP BY client_id
  `).all(`${targetMonth}%`) as any[];

  // 上限管理
  const limits = db.prepare(`
    SELECT * FROM monthly_burden_limits WHERE target_month = ?
  `).all(targetMonth) as any[];

  // 基本報酬単位数
  const standardBaseUnit = serviceKey === 'a' 
    ? (facility.base_unit_score || 584) 
    : (facility.type_b_base_unit || 610);

  let totalClientCount = 0;
  let totalFacilityUnits = 0;
  let totalBillingAmount = 0;
  let totalUserBurdenAmount = 0;

  const detailLines: string[] = [];
  const provisionLines: string[] = [];
  const burdenResultLines: string[] = [];

  for (const record of attendanceDays) {
    const client = (db.prepare('SELECT * FROM clients WHERE id = ?').get(record.client_id) as any) || {};
    const recipientNumber = String(client.recipient_number || '9999999999').padStart(10, '0');
    const cityCode = String(client.city_code || defaultCityCode).padStart(6, '0');

    const serviceTypeKey: ComplianceServiceKey = serviceKey === 'a' ? 'type_a' : 'type_b';
    const canAbsence = isAdditionEnabled(serviceTypeKey, 'absence_support', db);
    const canTransport = isAdditionEnabled(serviceTypeKey, 'transportation', db);
    const transportType = getAdditionValue(serviceTypeKey, 'transportation', db);
    const canMeal = isAdditionEnabled(serviceTypeKey, 'meal_provision', db);

    const actualDays = record.present_days + record.out_facility_days;
    if (actualDays === 0 && (!canAbsence || record.absence_addition_days === 0)) continue;

    let clientTotalUnits = 0;
    const itemDetails: string[] = [];

    // 基本報酬
    if (actualDays > 0) {
      const baseUnitsTotal = actualDays * standardBaseUnit;
      clientTotalUnits += baseUnitsTotal;
      const baseCode = serviceKey === 'a' ? '1111' : '1121';
      itemDetails.push(`22,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},${baseCode},${standardBaseUnit},${actualDays},${baseUnitsTotal}`);
    }

    // 欠席時対応加算（設定でONの場合のみ算定・未届出時は誤請求防止ガードにより除外）
    if (canAbsence && record.absence_addition_days > 0) {
      const absenceDays = Math.min(4, record.absence_addition_days);
      const absenceUnit = 94;
      const absenceTotal = absenceDays * absenceUnit;
      clientTotalUnits += absenceTotal;
      itemDetails.push(`22,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},5871,${absenceUnit},${absenceDays},${absenceTotal}`);
    }

    // 送迎加算（設定でONの場合のみ算定・未届出時は誤請求防止ガードにより除外）
    if (canTransport && record.transportation_trips > 0) {
      const transUnit = transportType === 'same_site' ? 15 : 21;
      const transTotal = record.transportation_trips * transUnit;
      clientTotalUnits += transTotal;
      itemDetails.push(`22,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},6500,${transUnit},${record.transportation_trips},${transTotal}`);
    }

    // 食事提供体制加算（設定でONの場合のみ算定・未届出時は誤請求防止ガードにより除外）
    if (canMeal && record.meal_days > 0) {
      const mealUnit = 30;
      const mealTotal = record.meal_days * mealUnit;
      clientTotalUnits += mealTotal;
      itemDetails.push(`22,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},5441,${mealUnit},${record.meal_days},${mealTotal}`);
    }


    const totalCost = Math.floor(clientTotalUnits * areaUnitCost);
    const clientLimit = limits.find(l => l.client_id === record.client_id);
    const userBurden = clientLimit?.calculated_our_burden ?? 0;
    const billingAmount = totalCost - userBurden;

    totalClientCount++;
    totalFacilityUnits += clientTotalUnits;
    totalBillingAmount += billingAmount;
    totalUserBurdenAmount += userBurden;

    // 明細基本情報レコード (21)
    detailLines.push(`21,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},${cityCode},${monthStr},${actualDays},${clientTotalUnits},${clientTotalUnits},${totalCost},${billingAmount},${userBurden}`);
    detailLines.push(...itemDetails);

    // サービス提供実績記録票・基本 (71)
    provisionLines.push(`71,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},${monthStr},${actualDays}`);

    // 日別明細レコード (72: 様式第4)
    const dailyRecords = db.prepare(`
      SELECT record_date, status, is_absence_addition, is_offsite, meal_provided, 
             pickup_used, dropoff_used, transportation_used
      FROM attendance_records
      WHERE client_id = ? AND record_date LIKE ? AND deleted_at IS NULL
      ORDER BY record_date ASC
    `).all(record.client_id, `${targetMonth}-%`) as any[];

    for (const d of dailyRecords) {
      const dayNum = parseInt(d.record_date.slice(8, 10), 10);
      const formattedDay = String(dayNum).padStart(2, '0');
      let serviceStatus = 0;
      if (d.is_offsite === 1 || d.status === 'out_facility') {
        serviceStatus = 2; // 施設外就労
      } else if (d.status === 'present') {
        serviceStatus = 1; // 通所
      } else if (d.is_absence_addition === 1) {
        serviceStatus = 4; // 欠席時対応
      }

      if (serviceStatus > 0) {
        const hasPickup = d.pickup_used === 1;
        const hasDropoff = d.dropoff_used === 1;
        const hasTransport = d.transportation_used === 1;
        let transportCode = 0;
        if (hasPickup && hasDropoff) transportCode = 1;
        else if (hasPickup) transportCode = 2;
        else if (hasDropoff) transportCode = 3;
        else if (hasTransport) transportCode = 1;

        const mealFlag = d.meal_provided ? 1 : 0;
        // 72,事業所番号,受給者証番号,サービス種類コード,対象年月,日,提供状況,送迎,食事
        provisionLines.push(`72,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},${monthStr},${formattedDay},${serviceStatus},${transportCode},${mealFlag}`);
      }
    }

    // 上限額管理結果票 (73)
    if (clientLimit && clientLimit.is_manager === 1) {
      burdenResultLines.push(`73,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${monthStr},${clientLimit.our_total_cost ?? totalCost},${clientLimit.other_total_cost ?? 0},${clientLimit.calculated_our_burden ?? userBurden},1`);
    }
  }

  // 01: 交換情報レコード
  const headerLine = `01,01,${facilityNumber},${monthStr},${createdDateStr},${formatCsvField(facilityName, true)}`;
  // 11: 請求書レコード
  const totalCostAll = totalBillingAmount + totalUserBurdenAmount;
  const invoiceLine = `11,${facilityNumber},${serviceCode},${monthStr},${totalClientCount},${totalFacilityUnits},${totalCostAll},${totalUserBurdenAmount},${totalBillingAmount}`;

  const totalLinesCount = detailLines.length + provisionLines.length + burdenResultLines.length + 3;
  const trailerLine = `99,${totalLinesCount}`;

  const lines = [
    headerLine,
    invoiceLine,
    ...detailLines,
    ...provisionLines,
    ...burdenResultLines,
    trailerLine,
  ];

  const csvText = lines.join('\r\n') + '\r\n';
  const shiftJisBuffer = encodeToShiftJisBuffer(csvText);

  return {
    serviceKey,
    serviceCode,
    facilityNumber,
    facilityName,
    targetMonth,
    recordCount: totalLinesCount,
    totalUnits: totalFacilityUnits,
    totalBillingAmount,
    totalBurdenAmount: totalUserBurdenAmount,
    csvText,
    shiftJisBuffer,
  };
}

/**
 * =========================================================================
 * 3. グループホーム（共同生活援助 / サービス種類コード: 33）国保連伝送CSV生成
 * =========================================================================
 */
export function generateKokurenCsvForGh(targetMonth: string): KokurenCsvResult {
  const db = getDbConnection('gh');
  const serviceCode = '33';
  const settings = (db.prepare('SELECT * FROM gh_settings LIMIT 1').get() as any) || {};
  const facilityNumber = String(settings.facility_number || '2810088888').padStart(10, '0');
  const facilityName = settings.facility_name || 'グループホーム（共同生活援助）事業所';
  const defaultCityCode = '281001';
  const areaUnitCost = 10.0; // GH基本（自治体基準）

  const monthStr = targetMonth.replace('-', '');
  const now = new Date();
  const createdDateStr = `${now.getFullYear()}${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;

  const residents = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM gh_residents WHERE status = 'active'").all() as any[];
  const billings = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM gh_monthly_billings WHERE target_month = ?").all(targetMonth) as any[];

  // GH介護サービス包括型 区分別単位数（定員8人規模、1日あたり）
  const GH_UNITS: Record<number, number> = { 6: 624, 5: 567, 4: 489, 3: 418, 2: 362, 1: 308, 0: 254 };

  let totalClientCount = 0;
  let totalFacilityUnits = 0;
  let totalBillingAmount = 0;
  let totalUserBurdenAmount = 0;

  const detailLines: string[] = [];
  const provisionLines: string[] = [];

  const [yearStr, mStr] = targetMonth.split('-');
  const daysInMonth = new Date(parseInt(yearStr), parseInt(mStr), 0).getDate();

  for (const res of residents) {
    const recipientNumber = String(res.recipient_number || '1234567890').padStart(10, '0');
    const bill = billings.find(b => b.resident_id === res.id);
    const stayDays = bill?.stay_days ?? daysInMonth;

    const baseUnit = GH_UNITS[res.support_level] || 308;
    const clientBaseUnits = stayDays * baseUnit;

    const canNightSupport = isAdditionEnabled('gh', 'night_support', db);
    const nightSupportVal = getAdditionValue('gh', 'night_support', db);
    const nightAdditionUnit = nightSupportVal === 'II' ? 8 : (nightSupportVal === 'III' ? 3 : 13);
    const clientNightUnits = canNightSupport ? stayDays * nightAdditionUnit : 0;

    const clientTotalUnits = clientBaseUnits + clientNightUnits;
    const totalCost = Math.floor(clientTotalUnits * areaUnitCost);
    const userBurden = 0; // 生活保護・低所得世帯の自己負担0円想定
    const billingAmount = totalCost;

    totalClientCount++;
    totalFacilityUnits += clientTotalUnits;
    totalBillingAmount += billingAmount;
    totalUserBurdenAmount += userBurden;

    // 明細基本情報レコード (21)
    detailLines.push(`21,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},${defaultCityCode},${monthStr},${stayDays},${clientTotalUnits},${clientTotalUnits},${totalCost},${billingAmount},${userBurden}`);
    // サービス明細 (22: 基本報酬 1111)
    detailLines.push(`22,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},1111,${baseUnit},${stayDays},${clientBaseUnits}`);
    // サービス明細 (22: 夜間支援体制加算 6110 - 設定でONの場合のみ算定・未届出時は誤請求防止ガードにより除外)
    if (canNightSupport && clientNightUnits > 0) {
      detailLines.push(`22,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},6110,${nightAdditionUnit},${stayDays},${clientNightUnits}`);
    }


    // 実績記録票・基本 (71: 別表2)
    provisionLines.push(`71,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},${monthStr},${stayDays}`);

    // 外泊情報取得
    const stays = db.prepare(`
      SELECT * FROM gh_overnight_stays 
      WHERE resident_id = ? AND start_date <= ? AND (end_date IS NULL OR end_date >= ?)
    `).all(res.id, `${targetMonth}-${daysInMonth}`, `${targetMonth}-01`) as any[];

    // 日別明細 (72: 共同生活援助 別表2)
    // 72,事業所番号,受給者証番号,33,対象年月,日,入居状況(1:在館,2:外泊,3:入院),帰宅時加算(0/1),朝食(0/1),夕食(0/1)
    for (let day = 1; day <= daysInMonth; day++) {
      const dayFormatted = String(day).padStart(2, '0');
      const dateStr = `${targetMonth}-${dayFormatted}`;
      const isStay = stays.some(s => s.start_date <= dateStr && (!s.end_date || s.end_date >= dateStr));

      const status = isStay ? 2 : 1; // 1:在館, 2:外泊
      const returnAddition = isStay ? 1 : 0; // 帰宅時加算（外泊時算定）
      const breakfast = isStay ? 0 : 1;
      const dinner = isStay ? 0 : 1;

      provisionLines.push(`72,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},${monthStr},${dayFormatted},${status},${returnAddition},${breakfast},${dinner}`);
    }
  }

  const headerLine = `01,01,${facilityNumber},${monthStr},${createdDateStr},${formatCsvField(facilityName, true)}`;
  const invoiceLine = `11,${facilityNumber},${serviceCode},${monthStr},${totalClientCount},${totalFacilityUnits},${totalBillingAmount},${totalUserBurdenAmount},${totalBillingAmount}`;
  const totalLinesCount = detailLines.length + provisionLines.length + 3;
  const trailerLine = `99,${totalLinesCount}`;

  const lines = [
    headerLine,
    invoiceLine,
    ...detailLines,
    ...provisionLines,
    trailerLine,
  ];

  const csvText = lines.join('\r\n') + '\r\n';
  const shiftJisBuffer = encodeToShiftJisBuffer(csvText);

  return {
    serviceKey: 'gh',
    serviceCode,
    facilityNumber,
    facilityName,
    targetMonth,
    recordCount: totalLinesCount,
    totalUnits: totalFacilityUnits,
    totalBillingAmount,
    totalBurdenAmount: totalUserBurdenAmount,
    csvText,
    shiftJisBuffer,
  };
}

/**
 * =========================================================================
 * 4. 放課後等デイサービス (児童福祉法 / サービス種類コード: 63) 国保連伝送CSV生成
 *    【障害児通所給付費 電子請求仕様書・様式第7準拠】
 * =========================================================================
 */
export function generateKokurenCsvForHouday(targetMonth: string): KokurenCsvResult {
  const db = getDbConnection('houday');
  const serviceCode = '63'; // 児童福祉法 障害児通所給付費（放課後等デイサービス）
  const settings = (db.prepare('SELECT * FROM houday_settings WHERE id = ?').get('houday_default') as any) || {};
  const facilityNumber = String(settings.facility_number || '2850077777').padStart(10, '0');
  const facilityName = settings.facility_name || '放課後等デイサービス';
  const defaultCityCode = '281001';
  const areaUnitCost = 10.0; // 障害児通所地域区分

  const monthStr = targetMonth.replace('-', '');
  const now = new Date();
  const createdDateStr = `${now.getFullYear()}${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;

  const children = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM houday_children WHERE status = 'active'").all() as any[];

  let totalClientCount = 0;
  let totalFacilityUnits = 0;
  let totalBillingAmount = 0;
  let totalUserBurdenAmount = 0;

  const detailLines: string[] = [];
  const provisionLines: string[] = [];

  for (const child of children) {
    const recipientNumber = String(child.recipient_number || '2800000001').padStart(10, '0');

    // 通所実績
    const atts = db.prepare(`
      SELECT * FROM houday_attendance
      WHERE child_id = ? AND target_date LIKE ? AND status = 'present'
      ORDER BY target_date ASC
    `).all(child.id, `${targetMonth}%`) as any[];

    if (atts.length === 0) continue;

    const schoolDayCount = atts.filter(a => a.service_type === 'school_day').length;
    const vacationDayCount = atts.filter(a => a.service_type === 'vacation').length;
    const totalDays = atts.length;

    // 送迎回数
    let transportCount = 0;
    for (const a of atts) {
      if (a.pickup_school) transportCount++;
      if (a.dropoff_home) transportCount++;
    }

    const canTransport = isAdditionEnabled('houday', 'transportation', db);

    // 単位数計算（平日放課後: 610単位, 休業日: 714単位, 送迎加算: 54単位/片道）
    const schoolUnits = schoolDayCount * 610;
    const vacationUnits = vacationDayCount * 714;
    const transportUnits = (canTransport && transportCount > 0) ? transportCount * 54 : 0;
    const clientTotalUnits = schoolUnits + vacationUnits + transportUnits;

    const totalCost = Math.floor(clientTotalUnits * areaUnitCost);
    const userBurden = 0; // 児童通所受給自己負担上限管理（無償化または上限0円）
    const billingAmount = totalCost;

    totalClientCount++;
    totalFacilityUnits += clientTotalUnits;
    totalBillingAmount += billingAmount;
    totalUserBurdenAmount += userBurden;

    // 明細基本レコード (21)
    detailLines.push(`21,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},${defaultCityCode},${monthStr},${totalDays},${clientTotalUnits},${clientTotalUnits},${totalCost},${billingAmount},${userBurden}`);

    // 明細行 (22: 平日放課後基本単位 6101)
    if (schoolDayCount > 0) {
      detailLines.push(`22,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},6101,610,${schoolDayCount},${schoolUnits}`);
    }
    // 明細行 (22: 学校休業日基本単位 6102)
    if (vacationDayCount > 0) {
      detailLines.push(`22,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},6102,714,${vacationDayCount},${vacationUnits}`);
    }
    // 明細行 (22: 送迎加算 6200 - 設定でONの場合のみ算定・未届出時は誤請求防止ガードにより除外)
    if (canTransport && transportCount > 0) {
      detailLines.push(`22,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},6200,54,${transportCount},${transportUnits}`);
    }


    // 障害児通所給付費実績記録票・基本レコード (71: 様式第7)
    provisionLines.push(`71,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},${monthStr},${totalDays}`);

    // 日別明細レコード (72: 児童福祉法 様式第7)
    // 72,事業所番号,受給者証番号,63,対象年月,日,開学区分(1:平日放課後,2:休業日),提供状況(1:利用,2:欠席加算),送迎区分(0:なし,1:往復,2:片道迎,3:片道送),短時間通常区分(1:通常),医療的ケア(0/1)
    for (const a of atts) {
      const dayNum = parseInt(a.target_date.slice(8, 10), 10);
      const dayFormatted = String(dayNum).padStart(2, '0');
      const schoolType = a.service_type === 'school_day' ? 1 : 2; // 1: 平日, 2: 休業日
      const serviceStatus = 1; // 利用

      let transportCode = 0;
      if (a.pickup_school && a.dropoff_home) transportCode = 1; // 往復
      else if (a.pickup_school) transportCode = 2; // 片道（迎え）
      else if (a.dropoff_home) transportCode = 3; // 片道（送り）

      const medCare = child.has_medical_care ? 1 : 0;

      provisionLines.push(`72,${facilityNumber},${recipientNumber},${serviceCode},${monthStr},${dayFormatted},${schoolType},${serviceStatus},${transportCode},1,${medCare}`);
    }
  }

  const headerLine = `01,01,${facilityNumber},${monthStr},${createdDateStr},${formatCsvField(facilityName, true)}`;
  const invoiceLine = `11,${facilityNumber},${serviceCode},${monthStr},${totalClientCount},${totalFacilityUnits},${totalBillingAmount},${totalUserBurdenAmount},${totalBillingAmount}`;
  const totalLinesCount = detailLines.length + provisionLines.length + 3;
  const trailerLine = `99,${totalLinesCount}`;

  const lines = [
    headerLine,
    invoiceLine,
    ...detailLines,
    ...provisionLines,
    trailerLine,
  ];

  const csvText = lines.join('\r\n') + '\r\n';
  const shiftJisBuffer = encodeToShiftJisBuffer(csvText);

  return {
    serviceKey: 'houday',
    serviceCode,
    facilityNumber,
    facilityName,
    targetMonth,
    recordCount: totalLinesCount,
    totalUnits: totalFacilityUnits,
    totalBillingAmount,
    totalBurdenAmount: totalUserBurdenAmount,
    csvText,
    shiftJisBuffer,
  };
}


================================================================================
File: src/lib/billing/serviceCodeResolver.ts
================================================================================

import { db, getBillingRules } from "@/lib/db";

export type FacilitySettings = {
  capacity_class?: string;
  staffing_class?: string;
  treatment_addition_class?: string;
  welfare_specialist_addition?: string;
  wage_improvement_addition?: number;
  visual_auditory_addition?: string;
  higher_brain_addition?: number;
  severe_disability_addition?: string;
  meal_provision_addition?: number;
  transportation_addition?: string;
};

export interface ResolvedServiceCode {
  service_item_code: string;
  service_name: string;
  unit_score: number;
}

// =========================================================================
// WAM NET名称マッピング生成
// =========================================================================

function getCapacitySuffix(capacityClass?: string): string {
  switch (capacityClass) {
    case '20人以下': return '（１）';
    case '21人以上40人以下': return '（２）';
    case '41人以上60人以下': return '（３）';
    case '61人以上80人以下': return '（４）';
    case '81人以上': return '（５）';
    default: return '（１）';
  }
}

function getStaffingSuffix(staffingClass?: string): string {
  switch (staffingClass) {
    case '7.5:1': return 'Ⅰ';
    case '10:1': return 'Ⅱ';
    default: return 'Ⅰ';
  }
}

/**
 * 施設設定から基本報酬のサービス名称（WAM NET略称）を推測
 * 例: "就労Ａ型基本報酬Ⅰ（１）"
 */
export function getExpectedBasicRewardName(settings: FacilitySettings): string {
  const staff = getStaffingSuffix(settings.staffing_class);
  const cap = getCapacitySuffix(settings.capacity_class);
  return `就労Ａ型基本報酬${staff}${cap}`;
}

/**
 * マスタから部分一致または完全一致で一番近いコードを引く
 */
export function findCodeByPattern(pattern: string): ResolvedServiceCode | null {
  try {
    const row = db.prepare(`
      SELECT service_item_code, service_name, unit_score 
      FROM service_code_master 
      WHERE service_type_code = '43' AND (service_name LIKE ? OR service_item_code = ?)
      ORDER BY service_item_code ASC
      LIMIT 1
    `).get(`%${pattern}%`, pattern) as ResolvedServiceCode | undefined;

    return row || null;
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}

// =========================================================================
// 公開リゾルバ
// =========================================================================

/** 基本報酬 (S-08: A型スコア方式の基本報酬対応) */
export function resolveBasicRewardCode(settings: FacilitySettings, targetMonth?: string): ResolvedServiceCode {
  if (targetMonth) {
    try {
      const monthlyUnit = db.prepare(`
        SELECT base_unit, total_score 
        FROM monthly_facility_units 
        WHERE target_month = ?
      `).get(targetMonth) as any;

      if (monthlyUnit && monthlyUnit.base_unit > 0) {
        return {
          service_item_code: '1111',
          service_name: `就労Ａ型基本報酬（スコア${monthlyUnit.total_score || 0}点算定）`,
          unit_score: monthlyUnit.base_unit
        };
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  const expectedName = getExpectedBasicRewardName(settings);
  const found = findCodeByPattern(expectedName);
  const rules = getBillingRules();
  const defaultBaseUnit = rules['base_unit_standard'] ?? 584;
  return found || { service_item_code: '1111', service_name: '就労Ａ型基本報酬Ⅰ（１）', unit_score: defaultBaseUnit };
}

/** 処遇改善新加算 (Ⅰ〜Ⅴ) */
export function resolveTreatmentAdditionCode(settings: FacilitySettings): ResolvedServiceCode | null {
  if (!settings.treatment_addition_class || settings.treatment_addition_class === 'なし') return null;

  let roman = '';
  if (settings.treatment_addition_class.includes('Ⅰ')) roman = 'Ⅰ';
  else if (settings.treatment_addition_class.includes('Ⅱ')) roman = 'Ⅱ';
  else if (settings.treatment_addition_class.includes('Ⅲ')) roman = 'Ⅲ';
  else if (settings.treatment_addition_class.includes('Ⅳ')) roman = 'Ⅳ';
  else if (settings.treatment_addition_class.includes('Ⅴ')) roman = 'Ⅴ';

  if (!roman) return null;

  const pattern = `就労Ａ型処遇改善加算${roman}`;
  const found = findCodeByPattern(pattern);
  const codeSuffix = (['Ⅰ','Ⅱ','Ⅲ','Ⅳ','Ⅴ'].indexOf(roman) + 1).toString();
  return found || { service_item_code: `611${codeSuffix}`, service_name: `就労Ａ型処遇改善加算${roman}`, unit_score: 0 };
}

/** 欠席時対応加算 */
export function resolveAbsenceAdditionCode(): ResolvedServiceCode {
  const found = findCodeByPattern("就労Ａ型欠席時対応加算");
  const rules = getBillingRules();
  return found || { service_item_code: '5871', service_name: '就労Ａ型欠席時対応加算', unit_score: rules['absence_addition'] ?? 94 };
}

/** 食事提供体制加算 */
export function resolveMealProvisionAdditionCode(): ResolvedServiceCode {
  const found = findCodeByPattern("就労Ａ型食事提供体制加算");
  const rules = getBillingRules();
  return found || { service_item_code: '5441', service_name: '就労Ａ型食事提供体制加算', unit_score: rules['meal_addition'] ?? 30 };
}

/** 初期加算 */
export function resolveInitialAdditionCode(): ResolvedServiceCode {
  const found = findCodeByPattern("就労Ａ型初期加算");
  const rules = getBillingRules();
  return found || { service_item_code: '6101', service_name: '就労Ａ型初期加算', unit_score: rules['initial_addition'] ?? 30 };
}

/** 送迎加算 */
export function resolveTransportationCode(): ResolvedServiceCode {
  const found = findCodeByPattern("就労Ａ型送迎加算");
  const rules = getBillingRules();
  return found || { service_item_code: '6500', service_name: '就労Ａ型送迎加算', unit_score: rules['transportation_addition'] ?? 21 };
}

/** 福祉専門職員配置等加算 (Ⅰ〜Ⅲ) */
export function resolveWelfareSpecialistCode(rank: string): ResolvedServiceCode {
  const rules = getBillingRules();
  let itemCode = '6200';
  let unit = rules['welfare_specialist_1'] ?? 15;
  let name = '就労Ａ型福祉専門職員配置等加算Ⅰ';
  if (rank === 'II' || rank === 'Ⅱ') {
    itemCode = '6201'; unit = rules['welfare_specialist_2'] ?? 10; name = '就労Ａ型福祉専門職員配置等加算Ⅱ';
  } else if (rank === 'III' || rank === 'Ⅲ') {
    itemCode = '6202'; unit = rules['welfare_specialist_3'] ?? 6; name = '就労Ａ型福祉専門職員配置等加算Ⅲ';
  }
  const found = findCodeByPattern(name);
  return found || { service_item_code: itemCode, service_name: name, unit_score: unit };
}

/** 賃金向上達成指導員配置加算（令和6年度報酬改定により基本報酬包括化に伴い廃止。後方互換・請求防止のため0単位） */
export function resolveWageImprovementCode(): ResolvedServiceCode {
  const found = findCodeByPattern("就労Ａ型賃金向上達成指導員配置加算");
  const rules = getBillingRules();
  return found || { service_item_code: '6300', service_name: '就労Ａ型賃金向上達成指導員配置加算', unit_score: rules['wage_improvement'] ?? 0 };
}

/** 視覚・聴覚言語障害者支援体制加算 (WAM NET標準コード: 6600) */
export function resolveVisualAuditoryCode(): ResolvedServiceCode {
  const found = findCodeByPattern("就労Ａ型視覚・聴覚言語障害者支援体制加算");
  const rules = getBillingRules();
  return found || { service_item_code: '6600', service_name: '就労Ａ型視覚・聴覚言語障害者支援体制加算', unit_score: rules['visual_auditory_addition'] ?? 41 };
}

/** 高次脳機能障害者支援体制加算 (WAM NET標準コード: 6601) */
export function resolveHigherBrainCode(): ResolvedServiceCode {
  const found = findCodeByPattern("就労Ａ型高次脳機能障害者支援体制加算");
  const rules = getBillingRules();
  return found || { service_item_code: '6601', service_name: '就労Ａ型高次脳機能障害者支援体制加算', unit_score: rules['higher_brain_addition'] ?? 41 };
}

/** 重度障害者支援加算 (Ⅰ・Ⅱ) */
export function resolveSevereDisabilityCode(rank: string): ResolvedServiceCode {
  const rules = getBillingRules();
  let itemCode = '6800';
  let unit = rules['severe_disability_1'] ?? 47;
  let name = '就労Ａ型重度障害者支援加算Ⅰ';
  if (rank === 'II' || rank === 'Ⅱ') {
    itemCode = '6801'; unit = rules['severe_disability_2'] ?? 23; name = '就労Ａ型重度障害者支援加算Ⅱ';
  }
  const found = findCodeByPattern(name);
  return found || { service_item_code: itemCode, service_name: name, unit_score: unit };
}
/** 施設外支援体制加算（施設外就労） */
export function resolveOffsiteWorkAdditionCode(cityCode?: string): ResolvedServiceCode {
  // ① 標準仕様としてのマスタ検索
  const defaultCode = { service_item_code: '6700', service_name: '就労Ａ型施設外支援体制加算', unit_score: 100 };
  const found = findCodeByPattern("就労Ａ型施設外支援体制加算");
  const baseCode = found || defaultCode;

  // ② 自治体独自のローカルルール上書き適用（要件対応）
  if (cityCode) {
    try {
      const jurisdiction = db.prepare(`SELECT local_rules_json FROM jurisdictions WHERE city_code = ?`).get(cityCode) as any;
      if (jurisdiction && jurisdiction.local_rules_json) {
        const rules = JSON.parse(jurisdiction.local_rules_json);
        if (rules.offsite_work_addition && typeof rules.offsite_work_addition === 'number') {
          // 自治体のローカルルールで単位数が上書きされている場合
          return {
            ...baseCode,
            unit_score: rules.offsite_work_addition,
            service_name: baseCode.service_name + '（独自要件）'
          };
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("ローカルルールの適用に失敗しました:", e);
    }
  }

  return baseCode;
}



================================================================================
File: src/lib/compliance/capacityChecker.ts
================================================================================

import { db, getBillingRules } from "@/lib/db";
import { toLocalISODate } from "@/lib/utils";

export interface DepartmentCapacityClient {
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  workType: 'in_facility' | 'out_facility' | 'both';
  isOffsite: boolean;
  isHoliday: boolean;
  scheduledIn?: string;
  scheduledOut?: string;
}

export interface DepartmentCapacityStatus {
  departmentId: string;
  departmentName: string;
  facilityType: 'main' | 'satellite';
  facilityTypeLabel: string;
  capacity: number; // 利用定員
  dailyLimit: number; // 1日の定員超過基準（定員50以下:150%, 51以上:定員+25）
  inFacilityCount: number; // 内勤予定人数
  offsiteCount: number; // 施設外就労予定人数
  holidayCount: number; // 休日人数
  totalPresentCount: number; // 当日利用予定人数(内勤+施設外)
  totalAssignedClients: number; // 所属登録人数
  overflowCount: number; // 1日基準超過人数 (Math.max(0, totalPresentCount - dailyLimit))
  isOverCapacity: boolean; // 超過フラグ
  inFacilityClients: DepartmentCapacityClient[];
  offsiteClients: DepartmentCapacityClient[];
}

export interface DailyCapacityAlert {
  dateStr: string; // YYYY-MM-DD
  dayOfWeek: string; // (月), (火) など
  isHoliday: boolean;
  hasOverflow: boolean;
  totalInFacility: number;
  totalOffsite: number;
  totalOverflow: number;
  departments: DepartmentCapacityStatus[];
}

const DAY_NAMES = ['日', '月', '火', '水', '木', '金', '土'];

/**
 * 定員区分文字列（例: "20人以下", "21人以上40人以下" 等）から実定員数値を安全に取得する
 * 正規表現で数字だけを結合して "2140" になるバグを防止
 */
export function parseCapacityFromClass(capClass?: string | null): number {
  if (!capClass) return 20;
  const trimmed = capClass.trim();
  if (trimmed.includes('20人以下')) return 20;
  if (trimmed.includes('40人以下') || trimmed.includes('21人以上40人以下')) return 40;
  if (trimmed.includes('60人以下') || trimmed.includes('41人以上60人以下')) return 60;
  if (trimmed.includes('80人以下') || trimmed.includes('61人以上80人以下')) return 80;
  if (trimmed.includes('81人以上')) return 81;

  // "○○人以下" 形式の正規表現パッチ (末尾の上限人数を取得)
  const underMatch = trimmed.match(/(\d+)人以下/);
  if (underMatch) return Number(underMatch[1]);

  const overMatch = trimmed.match(/(\d+)人以上/);
  if (overMatch) return Number(overMatch[1]);

  const directNum = Number(trimmed);
  if (!isNaN(directNum) && directNum > 0) return directNum;

  return 20;
}

/**
 * 特定の日付における各拠点（本所・サテライト）の内勤・施設外状況と受入上限超過を診断
 */
export function getDailyCapacityStatus(targetDate: string): DailyCapacityAlert {
  const dObj = new Date(targetDate);
  const dayOfWeekIndex = dObj.getDay();
  const dayOfWeek = `(${DAY_NAMES[dayOfWeekIndex]})`;

  // 施設全体の休業日か確認
  const facilityHoliday = db.prepare(`SELECT 1 FROM facility_holidays WHERE target_date = ?`).get(targetDate);
  const isFacilityHoliday = !!facilityHoliday;

  // 登録されている有効な拠点（部署）を取得
  const departments = db.prepare(`
    SELECT id, name, facility_type, COALESCE(capacity, 10) as capacity
    FROM departments
    WHERE deleted_at IS NULL
    ORDER BY sort_order ASC, created_at ASC
  `).all() as any[];

  // 部署が一つもない場合のフォールバック（仮想的に本所とサテライトを作成）
  const deptList: { id: string; name: string; facility_type: 'main' | 'satellite'; capacity: number }[] = 
    departments.length > 0 
      ? departments.map(d => ({
          id: d.id,
          name: d.name,
          facility_type: (d.facility_type === 'satellite' ? 'satellite' : 'main') as 'main' | 'satellite',
          capacity: Number(d.capacity) || 10
        }))
      : [
          { id: 'main_dept', name: '本所', facility_type: 'main', capacity: 10 },
          { id: 'satellite_dept', name: 'サテライト', facility_type: 'satellite', capacity: 10 }
        ];

  // 有効な利用者を全件取得
  const activeClients = db.prepare(`
    SELECT id, full_name, department_id, regular_holidays, weekly_schedule_pattern
    FROM clients
    WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND (status = 'active' OR status IS NULL)
  `).all() as any[];

  // 当日の勤怠実績（attendance_records）があるか確認
  const attendances = db.prepare(`
    SELECT client_id, status, work_type, is_offsite, clock_in, clock_out
    FROM attendance_records
    WHERE record_date = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL
  `).all(targetDate) as any[];

  const attendanceMap = new Map<string, any>();
  for (const a of attendances) {
    attendanceMap.set(a.client_id, a);
  }

  // 当日のシフト予定（client_shifts）を取得
  const shifts = db.prepare(`
    SELECT client_id, is_holiday, work_type, is_offsite, scheduled_in, scheduled_out
    FROM client_shifts
    WHERE target_date = ?
  `).all(targetDate) as any[];

  const shiftMap = new Map<string, any>();
  for (const s of shifts) {
    shiftMap.set(s.client_id, s);
  }

  const deptStatusMap = new Map<string, DepartmentCapacityStatus>();
  const rules = getBillingRules();
  const dailyRatioLimit = rules['capacity_daily_ratio_limit'] ?? 1.5;

  for (const dep of deptList) {
    // 厚労省告示基準: 定員11人以下は「定員+5人」、12〜50人は「定員×150%」、51人以上は「定員+25人」
    const dailyLimit = dep.capacity <= 11 
      ? dep.capacity + 5 
      : (dep.capacity <= 50 ? Math.floor(dep.capacity * dailyRatioLimit) : dep.capacity + 25);
    deptStatusMap.set(dep.id, {
      departmentId: dep.id,
      departmentName: dep.name,
      facilityType: dep.facility_type,
      facilityTypeLabel: dep.facility_type === 'satellite' ? 'サテライト' : '本所',
      capacity: dep.capacity,
      dailyLimit,
      inFacilityCount: 0,
      offsiteCount: 0,
      holidayCount: 0,
      totalPresentCount: 0,
      totalAssignedClients: 0,
      overflowCount: 0,
      isOverCapacity: false,
      inFacilityClients: [],
      offsiteClients: []
    });
  }

  // 未分類の利用者用のフォールバック（最初の部署、なければ本所）
  const defaultDeptId = deptList[0]?.id;

  for (const client of activeClients) {
    // 所属部署の特定
    let targetDeptId = client.department_id;
    if (!targetDeptId || !deptStatusMap.has(targetDeptId)) {
      targetDeptId = defaultDeptId;
    }
    const deptStatus = deptStatusMap.get(targetDeptId);
    if (!deptStatus) continue;

    deptStatus.totalAssignedClients++;

    const att = attendanceMap.get(client.id);
    const shift = shiftMap.get(client.id);

    let isHoliday = false;
    let isOffsite = false;
    let isInFacility = false;
    let scheduledIn: string | undefined = undefined;
    let scheduledOut: string | undefined = undefined;
    let workType: 'in_facility' | 'out_facility' | 'both' = 'in_facility';

    // 1. 実績がある場合（当日または過去）
    if (att) {
      if (att.status === 'absent' || att.status === 'holiday' || att.status === 'paid_leave' || att.status === 'pto') {
        isHoliday = true;
      } else {
        if (att.work_type === 'both') {
          isOffsite = true;
          isInFacility = true;
          workType = 'both';
        } else if (att.work_type === 'out_facility' || att.is_offsite === 1) {
          isOffsite = true;
          workType = 'out_facility';
        } else {
          isInFacility = true;
          workType = 'in_facility';
        }
        scheduledIn = att.clock_in;
        scheduledOut = att.clock_out;
      }
    } 
    // 2. シフト予定がある場合
    else if (shift) {
      if (shift.is_holiday === 1) {
        isHoliday = true;
      } else {
        if (shift.work_type === 'both') {
          isOffsite = true;
          isInFacility = true;
          workType = 'both';
        } else if (shift.work_type === 'out_facility' || shift.is_offsite === 1) {
          isOffsite = true;
          workType = 'out_facility';
        } else {
          isInFacility = true;
          workType = 'in_facility';
        }
        scheduledIn = shift.scheduled_in;
        scheduledOut = shift.scheduled_out;
      }
    } 
    // 3. シフト未設定の場合（定休日またはデフォルト通所予定）
    else {
      let isRegularHoliday = false;
      let matchedPattern: any = null;

      if (client.weekly_schedule_pattern) {
        try {
          const parsed = typeof client.weekly_schedule_pattern === 'string'
            ? JSON.parse(client.weekly_schedule_pattern)
            : client.weekly_schedule_pattern;
          matchedPattern = parsed[dayOfWeekIndex.toString()];
        } catch (e) {}
      }

      if (matchedPattern) {
        if (matchedPattern.is_holiday) {
          isRegularHoliday = true;
        } else {
          if (matchedPattern.work_type === 'both') {
            isOffsite = true;
            isInFacility = true;
            workType = 'both';
          } else if (matchedPattern.work_type === 'out_facility') {
            isOffsite = true;
            workType = 'out_facility';
          } else {
            isInFacility = true;
            workType = 'in_facility';
          }
          scheduledIn = matchedPattern.start_time;
          scheduledOut = matchedPattern.end_time;
        }
      } else {
        try {
          const hArr = JSON.parse(client.regular_holidays || '[]');
          if (hArr.includes(dayOfWeekIndex.toString())) {
            isRegularHoliday = true;
          }
        } catch (e) {}

        if (!isRegularHoliday) {
          isInFacility = true;
          workType = 'in_facility';
        }
      }

      if (isFacilityHoliday || isRegularHoliday) {
        isHoliday = true;
      }
    }

    const clientInfo: DepartmentCapacityClient = {
      id: client.id,
      fullName: client.full_name,
      workType,
      isOffsite,
      isHoliday,
      scheduledIn,
      scheduledOut
    };

    if (isHoliday) {
      deptStatus.holidayCount++;
    } else {
      if (isInFacility) {
        deptStatus.inFacilityCount++;
        deptStatus.inFacilityClients.push(clientInfo);
      }
      if (isOffsite) {
        deptStatus.offsiteCount++;
        deptStatus.offsiteClients.push(clientInfo);
      }
    }
  }

  let totalInFacility = 0;
  let totalOffsite = 0;
  let totalOverflow = 0;
  let hasOverflow = false;

  const resultDepts: DepartmentCapacityStatus[] = [];
  for (const dep of deptList) {
    const status = deptStatusMap.get(dep.id)!;
    
    // 厚労省指定基準: 施設外就労者も事業所の利用定員枠に含まれるため、総利用人数（内勤＋施設外）で超過判定する
    // 重複（work_type === 'both'）を排除した一意の当日利用人数を算出
    const uniqueClientIds = new Set([
      ...status.inFacilityClients.map(c => c.id),
      ...status.offsiteClients.map(c => c.id)
    ]);
    const totalPresent = uniqueClientIds.size;
    status.totalPresentCount = totalPresent;
    const overflow = Math.max(0, totalPresent - status.dailyLimit);
    status.overflowCount = overflow;
    status.isOverCapacity = overflow > 0;

    totalInFacility += status.inFacilityCount;
    totalOffsite += status.offsiteCount;
    totalOverflow += overflow;
    if (status.isOverCapacity) {
      hasOverflow = true;
    }
    resultDepts.push(status);
  }

  return {
    dateStr: targetDate,
    dayOfWeek,
    isHoliday: isFacilityHoliday,
    hasOverflow,
    totalInFacility,
    totalOffsite,
    totalOverflow,
    departments: resultDepts
  };
}

/**
 * 直近（今日〜指定日数先、デフォルト30日間）の内勤上限超過アラート一覧を取得
 */
export function getUpcomingCapacityAlerts(startDateStr?: string, daysAhead: number = 30): DailyCapacityAlert[] {
  const start = startDateStr ? new Date(startDateStr) : new Date();
  const alerts: DailyCapacityAlert[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < daysAhead; i++) {
    const curr = new Date(start);
    curr.setDate(start.getDate() + i);
    const dateStr = toLocalISODate(curr);
    const dailyStatus = getDailyCapacityStatus(dateStr);

    // 内勤超過がある日のみを抽出
    if (dailyStatus.hasOverflow) {
      alerts.push(dailyStatus);
    }
  }

  return alerts;
}

/**
 * 指定した月（YYYY-MM）の全日について拠点別内勤状況カレンダーデータを取得
 */
export function getMonthlyCapacityCalendar(targetMonth: string): DailyCapacityAlert[] {
  const [yearStr, monthStr] = targetMonth.split('-');
  const year = parseInt(yearStr, 10);
  const month = parseInt(monthStr, 10);
  const lastDay = new Date(year, month, 0).getDate();

  const results: DailyCapacityAlert[] = [];
  for (let day = 1; day <= lastDay; day++) {
    const dateStr = `${targetMonth}-${String(day).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    results.push(getDailyCapacityStatus(dateStr));
  }

  return results;
}

export interface MonthlyCapacityAlert {
  departmentId: string;
  departmentName: string;
  capacity: number;
  businessDays: number;
  totalPresentCount: number;
  monthlyLimit: number; // capacity * businessDays * 1.25 (小規模特例などは簡易的に一律1.25計算とする)
  averageRate: number; // %
  isOverCapacity: boolean;
}

/**
 * 過去3ヶ月（または当月）の月間平均定員超過（125%）を判定する
 * @param targetMonth YYYY-MM
 */
export function checkMonthlyCapacity(targetMonth: string): MonthlyCapacityAlert[] {
  const [yearStr, monthStr] = targetMonth.split('-');
  const year = parseInt(yearStr, 10);
  const month = parseInt(monthStr, 10);
  const lastDay = new Date(year, month, 0).getDate();

  // 当月の各日の状況を取得
  const dailyAlerts = getMonthlyCapacityCalendar(targetMonth);

  // 部署別に集計
  const summary = new Map<string, { capacity: number; departmentName: string; businessDays: number; presentCount: number; }>();

  for (const daily of dailyAlerts) {
    for (const dep of daily.departments) {
      if (!summary.has(dep.departmentId)) {
        summary.set(dep.departmentId, {
          capacity: dep.capacity,
          departmentName: dep.departmentName,
          businessDays: 0,
          presentCount: 0
        });
      }
      const s = summary.get(dep.departmentId)!;
      // 休業日以外を開所日としてカウント（あるいは利用者がいる日）
      if (!daily.isHoliday || dep.totalPresentCount > 0) {
        s.businessDays++;
      }
      s.presentCount += dep.totalPresentCount;
    }
  }

  const alerts: MonthlyCapacityAlert[] = [];
  const rules = getBillingRules();
  const monthlyRatioLimit = rules['capacity_monthly_ratio_limit'] ?? 1.25;

  for (const [deptId, s] of summary.entries()) {
    // 11名以下の特例（定員+3）等あるが、基本125%（ルール設定値）とする
    const monthlyLimit = Math.floor(s.capacity * s.businessDays * monthlyRatioLimit);
    const isOverCapacity = s.presentCount > monthlyLimit;
    const averageRate = s.businessDays > 0 ? (s.presentCount / (s.capacity * s.businessDays)) * 100 : 0;

    alerts.push({
      departmentId: deptId,
      departmentName: s.departmentName,
      capacity: s.capacity,
      businessDays: s.businessDays,
      totalPresentCount: s.presentCount,
      monthlyLimit,
      averageRate: Math.round(averageRate * 10) / 10,
      isOverCapacity
    });
  }

  return alerts;
}

/**
 * サービス別専用 加算・減算マスター定数 ＆ 型定義（クライアント・サーバー共通）
 * 
 * ※本ファイルは better-sqlite3 などのサーバー専用ネイティブモジュールに依存せず、
 * クライアントコンポーネントから安全にインポートできます。
 */

  defaultEnabled: boolean;
  defaultValue?: string;
  description: string;
  options?: Array<{ label: string; value: string; units?: number; rate?: number }>;
}

  isEnabled: boolean;
  value: string;
  notes?: string;
}

/**
 * サービス種類に応じた加算・減算マスタを取得
 */
export function getAdditionMasterForService(serviceType: ServiceTypeKey): AdditionMasterItem[] {
  switch (serviceType) {
    case 'type_a':
      return TYPE_A_ADDITIONS_MASTER;
    case 'type_b':
      return TYPE_B_ADDITIONS_MASTER;
    case 'gh':
      return GH_ADDITIONS_MASTER;
    case 'houday':
      return HOUDAY_ADDITIONS_MASTER;
    default:
      return TYPE_A_ADDITIONS_MASTER;
  }
}


================================================================================
File: src/lib/compliance/facilityAdditionsManager.ts

/**
 * サービス別専用 加算・減算マスタ ＆ 体制届出動的制御マネージャー
 * 
 * 障害者総合支援法（A型:43, B型:44, GH:33）および 児童福祉法（放課後等デイサービス:63）
 * 厚生労働省告示・こども家庭庁通知・令和6年度報酬改定に完全準拠
 */

import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { getDbConnection } from '@/lib/db-client';

export * from './facilityAdditionsConstants';
import { 
  ServiceTypeKey, 
  AdditionMasterItem, 
  FacilityAdditionRecord,
  getAdditionMasterForService 
} from './facilityAdditionsConstants';


/**
 * テーブル `facility_addition_settings` を確保する
 */
export function ensureAdditionSettingsTable(db: Database.Database): void {
  db.exec(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS facility_addition_settings (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      service_type TEXT NOT NULL,
      addition_key TEXT NOT NULL,
      is_enabled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
      selected_value TEXT,
      notes TEXT,
      created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now', 'localtime')),
      updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now', 'localtime')),
      UNIQUE(service_type, addition_key)
    );
  `);
}

/**
 * 指定サービスの全加算・減算設定を取得（DBにない項目はマスターのデフォルト値でフォールバック）
 */
export function getFacilityAdditions(
  serviceType: ServiceTypeKey,
  dbConn?: Database.Database
): FacilityAdditionRecord[] {
  const db = dbConn || getDbConnection(serviceType);
  ensureAdditionSettingsTable(db);

  const master = getAdditionMasterForService(serviceType);
  const rows = db.prepare(`
    SELECT addition_key, is_enabled, selected_value, notes 
    FROM facility_addition_settings 
    WHERE service_type = ?
  `).all(serviceType) as any[];

  const dbMap = new Map<string, { is_enabled: number; selected_value: string; notes?: string }>();
  for (const r of rows) {
    dbMap.set(r.addition_key, {
      is_enabled: r.is_enabled,
      selected_value: r.selected_value,
      notes: r.notes,
    });
  }

  // レガシー設定テーブルからのフォールバック値マッピング
  const legacyFallbacks = getLegacySettingsFallback(serviceType, db);

  return master.map((item) => {
    const existing = dbMap.get(item.key);
    if (existing) {
      return {
        additionKey: item.key,
        isEnabled: existing.is_enabled === 1,
        value: existing.selected_value ?? item.defaultValue ?? '',
        notes: existing.notes,
      };
    }

    // レガシーからの引き継ぎ、またはマスターのデフォルト値
    const fallback = legacyFallbacks.get(item.key);
    if (fallback !== undefined) {
      return {
        additionKey: item.key,
        isEnabled: fallback.isEnabled,
        value: fallback.value,
      };
    }

    return {
      additionKey: item.key,
      isEnabled: item.defaultEnabled,
      value: item.defaultValue ?? '',
    };
  });
}

/**
 * 特定の加算が有効（ON）かどうか判定する
 */
export function isAdditionEnabled(
  serviceType: ServiceTypeKey,
  additionKey: string,
  dbConn?: Database.Database
): boolean {
  const db = dbConn || getDbConnection(serviceType);
  ensureAdditionSettingsTable(db);

  const row = db.prepare(`
    SELECT is_enabled, selected_value 
    FROM facility_addition_settings 
    WHERE service_type = ? AND addition_key = ?
  `).get(serviceType, additionKey) as any;

  if (row) {
    if (row.is_enabled === 0) return false;
    // select型で 'none' または 'なし' が選ばれている場合は無効
    if (row.selected_value === 'none' || row.selected_value === 'なし') return false;
    return true;
  }

  // DB未保存時はレガシーまたはマスターの初期値判定
  const master = getAdditionMasterForService(serviceType);
  const item = master.find(m => m.key === additionKey);
  if (!item) return false;

  const legacy = getLegacySettingsFallback(serviceType, db).get(additionKey);
  if (legacy !== undefined) {
    return legacy.isEnabled && legacy.value !== 'none' && legacy.value !== 'なし';
  }

  return item.defaultEnabled;
}

/**
 * 特定の加算の選択値（区分・単位数）を取得する
 */
export function getAdditionValue(
  serviceType: ServiceTypeKey,
  additionKey: string,
  dbConn?: Database.Database
): string {
  const db = dbConn || getDbConnection(serviceType);
  ensureAdditionSettingsTable(db);

  const row = db.prepare(`
    SELECT selected_value 
    FROM facility_addition_settings 
    WHERE service_type = ? AND addition_key = ?
  `).get(serviceType, additionKey) as any;

  if (row && row.selected_value !== null && row.selected_value !== undefined) {
    return row.selected_value;
  }

  const master = getAdditionMasterForService(serviceType);
  const item = master.find(m => m.key === additionKey);
  return item?.defaultValue ?? '';
}

/**
 * 加算・減算設定を一括保存する
 */
export function saveFacilityAdditions(
  serviceType: ServiceTypeKey,
  additions: Record<string, { isEnabled: boolean; value?: string; notes?: string }>,
  dbConn?: Database.Database
): void {
  const db = dbConn || getDbConnection(serviceType);
  ensureAdditionSettingsTable(db);

  const stmt = db.prepare(`
    INSERT INTO facility_addition_settings (id, service_type, addition_key, is_enabled, selected_value, notes, updated_at)
    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now', 'localtime'))
    ON CONFLICT(service_type, addition_key) DO UPDATE SET
      is_enabled = excluded.is_enabled,
      selected_value = excluded.selected_value,
      notes = excluded.notes,
      updated_at = excluded.updated_at
  `);

  const tx = db.transaction(() => {
    for (const [key, config] of Object.entries(additions)) {
      const id = `${serviceType}_${key}`;
      const isEnabledInt = config.isEnabled ? 1 : 0;
      stmt.run(id, serviceType, key, isEnabledInt, config.value ?? '', config.notes ?? null);
    }
    // レガシー設定テーブルにも相互同期
    syncToLegacySettings(serviceType, additions, db);
  });

  tx();
}

/**
 * 減算項目の二重安全自動判定
 * チェックボックス手動フラグ ＋ データベース必須記録・計画の不備検知
 */
export function checkAutomaticReductions(
  serviceType: ServiceTypeKey,
  dbConn?: Database.Database
): Record<string, { isReduced: boolean; reason?: string }> {
  const db = dbConn || getDbConnection(serviceType);
  const result: Record<string, { isReduced: boolean; reason?: string }> = {};

  // 1. 手動フラグ（チェックボックスで明示的にONになっているか）
  const manualRestraint = !isAdditionEnabled(serviceType, 'restraint_reduction', db); // ONなら減算
  const manualAbuse = !isAdditionEnabled(serviceType, 'abuse_prevention_reduction', db);
  const manualBcp = !isAdditionEnabled(serviceType, 'bcp_reduction', db);

  // 2. 自動不備検知（各DBの記録・計画テーブル監査）
  if (serviceType === 'type_a' || serviceType === 'type_b') {
    const facility = (db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM facility_settings WHERE id = 'default'`).get() as any) || {};

    // BCP未策定
    const hasBcp = facility.has_bcp === 1;
    if (!hasBcp) {
      result.bcp_reduction = { isReduced: true, reason: '業務継続計画（BCP）未策定設定が有効です（1%減算）' };
    }

    // 虐待防止未実施
    const hasAbuse = facility.has_abuse_prevention === 1;
    if (!hasAbuse) {
      result.abuse_prevention_reduction = { isReduced: true, reason: '虐待防止措置未実施設定が有効です（1%減算）' };
    }

    // 身体拘束廃止未実施
    const hasRestraint = facility.has_restraint_reduction === 1;
    if (!hasRestraint) {
      result.restraint_reduction = { isReduced: true, reason: '身体拘束適正化未実施設定が有効です（1%減算）' };
    }
  } else if (serviceType === 'gh') {
    const gh = (db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM gh_settings WHERE id = 'gh_default'`).get() as any) || {};
    // GH特有の地域連携推進会議
    if (gh.has_community_meeting === 0) {
      result.community_meeting_reduction = { isReduced: true, reason: '地域連携推進会議未開催減算が指定されています' };
    }
  } else if (serviceType === 'houday') {
    const houday = (db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM houday_settings WHERE id = 'houday_default'`).get() as any) || {};
    // 自己評価未公表
    if (!houday.evaluation_publish_date || !houday.evaluation_url) {
      result.evaluation_unannounced_reduction = { isReduced: true, reason: '事業者自己評価結果が未公表です（15%減算）' };
    }
    // 安全計画未策定
    if (!houday.safety_plan_date) {
      result.safety_plan_reduction = { isReduced: true, reason: '安全計画が未策定です（1%減算）' };
    }
  }

  return result;
}

/**
 * レガシーテーブルからのフォールバック値マップ
 */
function getLegacySettingsFallback(
  serviceType: ServiceTypeKey,
  db: Database.Database
): Map<string, { isEnabled: boolean; value: string }> {
  const map = new Map<string, { isEnabled: boolean; value: string }>();

  try {
    if (serviceType === 'type_a' || serviceType === 'type_b') {
      const row = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM facility_settings WHERE id = 'default'").get() as any;
      if (row) {
        if (row.transportation_addition) {
          map.set('transportation', {
            isEnabled: row.transportation_addition !== 'none',
            value: row.transportation_addition,
          });
        }
        if (row.meal_provision_addition !== undefined) {
          map.set('meal_provision', {
            isEnabled: row.meal_provision_addition === 1,
            value: '30',
          });
        }
        if (row.visit_support_addition !== undefined) {
          map.set('visit_support', {
            isEnabled: row.visit_support_addition === 1,
            value: 'enabled',
          });
        }
        if (row.treatment_addition_class) {
          map.set('treatment_improvement', {
            isEnabled: row.treatment_addition_class !== 'なし',
            value: row.treatment_addition_class,
          });
        }
        if (row.staffing_class) {
          map.set('staffing_class', {
            isEnabled: true,
            value: row.staffing_class,
          });
        }
      }
    } else if (serviceType === 'gh') {
      const row = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM gh_settings WHERE id = 'gh_default'").get() as any;
      if (row) {
        if (row.caregiver_ratio) {
          map.set('caregiver_ratio', { isEnabled: true, value: row.caregiver_ratio });
        }
        if (row.night_support_type) {
          const isEnabled = !row.night_support_type.includes('なし');
          const val = row.night_support_type.includes('加算I') ? 'I' : 'II';
          map.set('night_support', { isEnabled, value: val });
        }
      }
    } else if (serviceType === 'houday') {
      const row = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM houday_settings WHERE id = 'houday_default'").get() as any;
      if (row) {
        map.set('transportation', { isEnabled: true, value: 'standard' });
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    // テーブルが存在しないなどの場合は無視
  }

  return map;
}

/**
 * レガシー設定テーブル（facility_settings, gh_settings, houday_settings）への同期
 */
function syncToLegacySettings(
  serviceType: ServiceTypeKey,
  additions: Record<string, { isEnabled: boolean; value?: string }>,
  db: Database.Database
): void {
  try {
    if (serviceType === 'type_a' || serviceType === 'type_b') {
      const trans = additions.transportation;
      const meal = additions.meal_provision;
      const visit = additions.visit_support;
      const treat = additions.treatment_improvement;
      const staff = additions.staffing_class;

      const updates: string[] = [];
      const params: any[] = [];

      if (trans) {
        updates.push('transportation_addition = ?');
        params.push(trans.isEnabled ? (trans.value || 'standard') : 'none');
      }
      if (meal) {
        updates.push('meal_provision_addition = ?');
        params.push(meal.isEnabled ? 1 : 0);
      }
      if (visit) {
        updates.push('visit_support_addition = ?');
        params.push(visit.isEnabled ? 1 : 0);
      }
      if (treat) {
        updates.push('treatment_addition_class = ?');
        params.push(treat.isEnabled ? (treat.value || '新加算Ⅰ') : 'なし');
      }
      if (staff && staff.value) {
        updates.push('staffing_class = ?');
        params.push(staff.value);
      }

      if (updates.length > 0) {
        params.push('default');
        db.prepare(`UPDATE facility_settings SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ?`).run(...params);
      }
    } else if (serviceType === 'gh') {
      const tableInfo = db.prepare("PRAGMA table_info(gh_settings)").all() as any[];
      const colNames = new Set(tableInfo.map(c => c.name));

      const ratio = additions.caregiver_ratio;
      const night = additions.night_support;
      const updates: string[] = [];
      const params: any[] = [];

      if (ratio && ratio.value && colNames.has('caregiver_ratio')) {
        updates.push('caregiver_ratio = ?');
        params.push(ratio.value);
      }
      if (night && colNames.has('night_support_type')) {
        updates.push('night_support_type = ?');
        params.push(night.isEnabled ? (night.value === 'I' ? '加算I（夜勤職員常駐）' : '加算II（宿直職員配置）') : '算定なし');
      }
      if (updates.length > 0) {
        params.push('gh_default');
        db.prepare(`UPDATE gh_settings SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ?`).run(...params);
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    // レガシー同期エラーはログのみ
  }
}



================================================================================
File: src/lib/compliance/holidayUtils.ts
================================================================================

/**
 * 就労継続支援A型 公休日・労働時間 判定ユーティリティ
 * 
 * シフト表（client_shifts）、曜日別スケジュール（weekly_schedule_pattern）、
 * 定休日（regular_holidays）を統合判定し、出勤簿および利用実績表へ正確に反映します。
 */

export interface ClientHolidaySettings {
  id?: string;
  regular_holidays?: string | null;
  weekly_schedule_pattern?: string | null;
}

export interface ShiftRecord {
  client_id?: string;
  target_date?: string;
  is_holiday?: number;
  holiday_type?: string | null;
  scheduled_in?: string | null;
  scheduled_out?: string | null;
}

/**
 * 利用者と指定日において、公休日（または指定休日）かどうかを判定する
 * 
 * 優先順位:
 * 1. 個別シフト表（client_shifts）の該当日の登録
 * 2. 利用者台帳の曜日別定例スケジュール（weekly_schedule_pattern）
 * 3. 利用者台帳の定休日設定（regular_holidays）
 * 4. いずれも設定されていない場合のデフォルト（土日公休）
 */
export function getClientDayHolidayInfo(
  dateStr: string, // YYYY-MM-DD
  client: ClientHolidaySettings,
  shift?: ShiftRecord | null
): { isHoliday: boolean; holidayLabel: string } {
  // 1. 個別シフト表の登録
  if (shift) {
    if (shift.is_holiday === 1) {
      return { isHoliday: true, holidayLabel: shift.holiday_type || "公休" };
    }
    // シフト登録があり、かつ is_holiday !== 1 の場合は出勤予定日（公休ではない）
    return { isHoliday: false, holidayLabel: "" };
  }

  const dateObj = new Date(dateStr);
  const dayOfWeek = dateObj.getDay(); // 0: 日, 1: 月, ..., 6: 土
  const dayOfWeekStr = dayOfWeek.toString();

  // 2. 利用者台帳の曜日別定例スケジュール（weekly_schedule_pattern）
  if (client.weekly_schedule_pattern) {
    try {
      const pattern = typeof client.weekly_schedule_pattern === 'string'
        ? JSON.parse(client.weekly_schedule_pattern)
        : client.weekly_schedule_pattern;
      const dayPattern = pattern?.[dayOfWeekStr];
      if (dayPattern) {
        if (dayPattern.is_holiday === true || dayPattern.is_holiday === 1) {
          return { isHoliday: true, holidayLabel: dayPattern.holiday_type || "公休" };
        } else {
          // 曜日別パターンで出勤日として明示されている場合
          return { isHoliday: false, holidayLabel: "" };
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  // 3. 利用者台帳の定休日設定（regular_holidays）
  if (client.regular_holidays) {
    try {
      const arr = typeof client.regular_holidays === 'string'
        ? JSON.parse(client.regular_holidays)
        : client.regular_holidays;
      if (Array.isArray(arr) && arr.length > 0) {
        const kanjiDays = ["日", "月", "火", "水", "木", "金", "土"];
        const fullKanjiDays = ["日曜日", "月曜日", "火曜日", "水曜日", "木曜日", "金曜日", "土曜日"];
        const isMatch = arr.some(h => 
          h === dayOfWeek ||
          h === dayOfWeekStr ||
          h === kanjiDays[dayOfWeek] ||
          h === fullKanjiDays[dayOfWeek] ||
          (typeof h === 'string' && h.includes(kanjiDays[dayOfWeek]))
        );
        if (isMatch) {
          return { isHoliday: true, holidayLabel: "公休" };
        } else {
          // regular_holidays が設定されており、当曜日が含まれていなければ出勤日
          return { isHoliday: false, holidayLabel: "" };
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  // 4. いずれも設定されていない場合のデフォルト（土日は公休）
  if (dayOfWeek === 0 || dayOfWeek === 6) {
    return { isHoliday: true, holidayLabel: "公休" };
  }

  return { isHoliday: false, holidayLabel: "" };
}

export interface ClientScheduleSettings extends ClientHolidaySettings {
  work_start_time?: string | null;
  work_end_time?: string | null;
  break_time_minutes?: number | null;
}

export interface DayScheduleInfo {
  isHoliday: boolean;
  holidayLabel: string;
  isWorkDay: boolean;
  scheduledIn?: string;
  scheduledOut?: string;
  breakMinutes: number;
  workType: 'in_facility' | 'out_facility' | string;
  workTypeLabel: string;
}

/**
 * 利用者と指定日において、予定スケジュール（公休・出勤予定・予定時刻・作業区分）を取得する
 */
export function getClientDayScheduleInfo(
  dateStr: string,
  client: ClientScheduleSettings,
  shift?: ShiftRecord | null
): DayScheduleInfo {
  const holidayInfo = getClientDayHolidayInfo(dateStr, client, shift);
  const defaultBreak = client.break_time_minutes ?? 60;

  if (holidayInfo.isHoliday) {
    return {
      isHoliday: true,
      holidayLabel: holidayInfo.holidayLabel || "公休",
      isWorkDay: false,
      breakMinutes: 0,
      workType: 'holiday',
      workTypeLabel: holidayInfo.holidayLabel || "公休"
    };
  }

  // 1. シフトレコードがある場合
  if (shift && (shift.scheduled_in || shift.scheduled_out)) {
    return {
      isHoliday: false,
      holidayLabel: "",
      isWorkDay: true,
      scheduledIn: shift.scheduled_in || undefined,
      scheduledOut: shift.scheduled_out || undefined,
      breakMinutes: defaultBreak,
      workType: 'in_facility',
      workTypeLabel: '施設内就労'
    };
  }

  const dateObj = new Date(dateStr);
  const dayOfWeek = dateObj.getDay();
  const dayOfWeekStr = dayOfWeek.toString();

  // 2. 曜日別定例パターン（weekly_schedule_pattern）
  if (client.weekly_schedule_pattern) {
    try {
      const pattern = typeof client.weekly_schedule_pattern === 'string'
        ? JSON.parse(client.weekly_schedule_pattern)
        : client.weekly_schedule_pattern;
      const dayPattern = pattern?.[dayOfWeekStr];
      if (dayPattern && !dayPattern.is_holiday) {
        const isOut = dayPattern.work_type === 'out_facility';
        return {
          isHoliday: false,
          holidayLabel: "",
          isWorkDay: true,
          scheduledIn: dayPattern.start_time || client.work_start_time || "10:00",
          scheduledOut: dayPattern.end_time || client.work_end_time || "16:00",
          breakMinutes: defaultBreak,
          workType: dayPattern.work_type || 'in_facility',
          workTypeLabel: isOut ? '施設外就労' : '施設内就労'
        };
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  // 3. 基本所定労働時間
  return {
    isHoliday: false,
    holidayLabel: "",
    isWorkDay: true,
    scheduledIn: client.work_start_time || "10:00",
    scheduledOut: client.work_end_time || "16:00",
    breakMinutes: defaultBreak,
    workType: 'in_facility',
    workTypeLabel: '施設内就労'
  };
}

/**
 * 出勤・退勤時刻と休憩時間から実労働時間（分）を算出する
 */
export function calculateActualWorkMinutes(
  clockIn?: string | null,
  clockOut?: string | null,
  breakTimeMinutes: number = 0
): number {
  if (!clockIn || !clockOut) return 0;
  
  const [inH, inM] = clockIn.split(':').map(Number);
  const [outH, outM] = clockOut.split(':').map(Number);
  
  if (isNaN(inH) || isNaN(inM) || isNaN(outH) || isNaN(outM)) return 0;

  const inMinutes = inH * 60 + inM;
  let outMinutes = outH * 60 + outM;

  if (outMinutes < inMinutes) {
    // 日跨ぎ（翌日）の場合
    outMinutes += 24 * 60;
  }

  let totalMinutes = outMinutes - inMinutes - breakTimeMinutes;
  return totalMinutes > 0 ? totalMinutes : 0;
}

【問1】就労継続支援A型の「1日の平均労働時間」の算定方法はどのように行うか。
（答）前年度において雇用契約を締結していた利用者の労働時間の合計を、当該利用者の前年度の延べ労働日数（実際に就労した日数の合計）で除して算出する。年次有給休暇を取得した日については、所定労働時間労働したものとして算定に含めることができる。

【問3】欠席時対応加算（94単位）を算定するための記録要件は何か。
（答）利用予定日の前々日、前日又は当日に急病等により欠席の連絡があった場合に、電話等により当該利用者の状況を確認し、引き続き利用を促す等の相談援助を行うとともに、「連絡日時・連絡方法・連絡者・対応職員・欠席理由及び体調・相談援助の具体的内容・次回通所予定日」を漏れなく記録しておくことが必要である。`

第2 平均工賃月額の算定方法及び3,000円下限基準（指定基準第201条・厚労省課長通知）
1. 平均工賃月額の下限（指定基準第201条第2項）：
   - 指定就労継続支援B型事業者が利用者に支払う1月当たりの工賃の平均額（平均工賃月額）は、「3,000円を下回ってはならない」。
2. 令和6年度改定後の平均工賃月額の確定算定式（障害保健福祉部障害福祉課長通知 障発0329第29号）：
   - 算定式：【前年度の年間工賃支払総額】÷（【前年度の年間延べ利用人数】÷【前年度の年間開所日数】）÷ 12か月
   - 通勤手当等の「実費弁償」として支払われる金銭は、生産活動の対価である工賃には該当しないため、年間工賃支払総額から厳格に除外すること。

第3 就労継続支援B型固有の加算及び処遇改善新加算率
1. B型専用加算（定員20人以下の場合）：
   - 目標工賃達成指導員配置加算：89単位/日（コード 45-6310）。サービス費Ⅰ〜Ⅲを算定し、職業指導員・生活支援員に加えて目標工賃達成指導員を常勤換算方法で1以上配置し、手厚い人員配置（常勤換算で5:1以上）を満たす場合に算定。
   - 目標工賃達成加算：10単位/日（コード 45-6320）。前年度の平均工賃月額が目標工賃（地域の最低賃金の1/3以上等）を達成した場合に算定。
   - 地域協働加算：30単位/日（コード 45-6400）。地域住民や企業等と協働して生産活動等を実施し公表している場合に算定。
2. 福祉・介護職員等処遇改善加算（就労継続支援B型 法定率）：
   - 処遇改善新加算Ⅰ：11.2%（0.112）
   - 処遇改善新加算Ⅱ：11.0%（0.110）
   - 処遇改善新加算Ⅲ：9.2%（0.092）
   - 処遇改善新加算Ⅳ：7.4%（0.074）`

1. 非雇用契約の原則と工賃支払規程の整備
   - 就労継続支援B型は雇用契約を締結しない福祉サービスであるため、労働基準法及び最低賃金法は適用されない（雇用契約書ではなく「利用契約書」及び「工賃支払規程同意書」を締結・保管すること）。

2. 生産活動収支と工賃支払総額の原則（指定基準第201条第1項）
   - 生産活動に係る事業の収入から生産活動に係る必要な経費を控除した額に相当する金額を、利用者に工賃として支払わなければならない。
   - 自立支援給付費（訓練等給付費）を工賃の原資に充てること（給付費からの工賃補填）は指定基準違反であり、実地指導における重点確認事項である。

3. 年度別工賃実績報告及び工賃向上計画の策定・提出義務
   - 毎年4月〜5月に、前年度（4月〜翌3月）の月別「工賃支払総額」「延べ利用人数」「開所日数」及び確定算定式による「平均工賃月額」を集計した『年度別工賃実績報告書』を兵庫県・神戸市へ提出すること。
   - 平均工賃月額が1万円未満の事業所や工賃水準の向上が求められる事業所は、3か年の『工賃向上計画書』を策定し、目標工賃と具体的取組（販路開拓、新商品開発、作業工程の細分化等）を定めること。`

【資料名】指定就労継続支援B型事業所 工賃支払規程

第1条（目的）
本規程は、障害者の日常生活及び社会生活を総合的に支援するための法律に基づく指定障害福祉サービスの事業等の人員、設備及び運営に関する基準第201条に基づき、当事業所の就労継続支援B型を利用する者に支払う工賃の算定及び支給方法について定める。

第2条（工賃の原資）
利用者に支払う工賃の総額は、生産活動に係る事業の収入から生産活動に係る必要な経費を控除した額に相当する金額以上の額とする。訓練等給付費（自立支援給付費）から工賃を補填することは一切行わない。

第3条（工賃の算定体系）
利用者の作業形態及び能力に応じ、以下の算定方式を組み合わせて支給する。ただし、事業所の1月当たりの平均工賃月額は3,000円を下回らないものとする。
1. 時間給（時給制工賃）：基本工賃単価（1時間当たり300円〜）× 月間作業時間数（1円未満の端数は四捨五入）
2. 日額制工賃：1日当たり所定工賃額 × 月間通所作業日数
3. 出来高制工賃：作業品目別単価 × 完成検収個数
4. 能力給（役割・評価手当）：作業習熟度やリーダー業務等に応じ月額加算する工賃

第4条（実費弁償・通勤手当の区分）
通所に要する交通費等を補助する「通勤手当（実費弁償）」は、生産活動の対価である工賃とは明確に区分して支給し、厚生労働省へ報告する工賃実績額及び平均工賃月額の算定対象には含めない。

第5条（工賃からの控除）
昼食代、行事参加費等の実費を工賃から控除する場合は、あらかじめ利用者本人（及び必要に応じて家族・後見人）から書面による「工賃控除に関する同意書」を取得したものに限り控除を行う。`
