【資料名】令和6年度障害福祉サービス等報酬改定等に関するQ&A VOL.1〜VOL.3（就労継続支援B型抜粋）

【問1】令和6年度報酬改定により、就労継続支援B型の「平均工賃月額」の算定方式はどのように変更されたか。
（答）障害特性等により利用日数が少ない利用者を多く受け入れる事業所が不利にならないよう、従来の「外れ値除外方式」を廃止し、令和6年4月からは『年間工賃支払総額 ÷（年間延べ利用人数 ÷ 年間開所日数）÷ 12か月』により算定する方式（1日当たり平均利用者数を用いた算定式）に統一された。

【問2】目標工賃達成指導員配置加算（定員20人以下：89単位/日）を算定するための人員配置要件は何か。
（答）就労継続支援B型サービス費Ⅰ、Ⅱ又はⅢ（平均工賃月額連動型）を算定している事業所において、指定基準上の職業指導員及び生活支援員の員数（6:1等）に加えて、常勤換算方法で1以上の目標工賃達成指導員を配置し、かつ職業指導員・生活支援員・目標工賃達成指導員の合計が常勤換算方法で利用者の数を5で除した数以上（5:1以上）配置されている場合に算定できる。

【問3】能力給（作業評価手当等）は生産活動収支の工賃支払額及び平均工賃月額の算定に含まれるか。
（答）生産活動の対価として工賃支払規程に基づき支給される基本工賃（時給・日給・出来高）及び能力給は工賃支払総額に含まれる。一方、通勤手当等の実費弁償は生産活動の対価ではないため含まれない。`
  }
];

export function ensureKnowledgeDocumentsSeeded(db: Database.Database, mode: ServiceMode) {
  try {
    const seeds = mode === 'type_b' ? TYPE_B_KNOWLEDGE_SEEDS : TYPE_A_KNOWLEDGE_SEEDS;
    const storageDir = path.join(process.cwd(), 'storage', 'knowledge', mode);
    if (!fs.existsSync(storageDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(storageDir, { recursive: true });
    }

    const insertStmt = db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO knowledge_documents (id, title, category, file_name, file_path, extracted_text, is_active, uploaded_at, deleted_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 1, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, NULL)
      ON CONFLICT(id) DO NOTHING
    `);

    db.transaction(() => {
      for (const item of seeds) {
        const absFilePath = path.join(storageDir, item.file_name);
        const relFilePath = path.join('storage', 'knowledge', mode, item.file_name).replace(/\\/g, '/');
        if (!fs.existsSync(absFilePath)) {
          try {
            fs.writeFileSync(absFilePath, item.extracted_text, 'utf8');
          } catch (err) {
            console.warn('Could not write knowledge seed file:', err);
          }
        }
        insertStmt.run(item.id, item.title, item.category, item.file_name, relFilePath, item.extracted_text);
      }
    })();
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to seed knowledge_documents:', e);
  }
}


================================================================================
File: src/lib/compliance/minimumWageData.ts
================================================================================

import { getRegionalMinimumWage, DEFAULT_REGIONAL_MINIMUM_WAGES } from '@/lib/db';

export interface MinimumWageInfo {
  prefecture: string;
  minimumWage: number;
  effectiveDate: string;
  sourceUrl: string;
}

/**
 * 住所文字列から都道府県を判定し、最新の最低賃金情報を取得する（DB動的取得）
 */
export function getMinimumWageByAddress(address: string): MinimumWageInfo | null {
  if (!address) return null;

  // 1. DBから都道府県名照合
  const dbResult = getRegionalMinimumWage(address);
  if (dbResult) {
    return {
      prefecture: dbResult.prefName,
      minimumWage: dbResult.wage,
      effectiveDate: dbResult.effectiveDate,
      sourceUrl: "https://saiteichingin.mhlw.go.jp/"
    };
  }

  // 2. フォールバック
  for (const w of DEFAULT_REGIONAL_MINIMUM_WAGES) {
    const prefNameWithoutSuffix = w.pref_name.replace(/(都|府|県)$/, '');
    if (address.includes(w.pref_name) || address.includes(prefNameWithoutSuffix)) {
      return {
        prefecture: w.pref_name,
        minimumWage: w.minimum_wage,
        effectiveDate: w.effective_date,
        sourceUrl: "https://saiteichingin.mhlw.go.jp/"
      };
    }
  }

  return null;
}

/**
 * 就労継続支援A型 厚生労働省公式告示・確定ルールマスター
 * （令和6年度障害福祉サービス等報酬改定 こども家庭庁・厚生労働省告示第3号、および令和6年6月施行 処遇改善一本化通知準拠）
 * 
 * 生成AIの非決定性（モデル差・ハルシネーション・年代混同）による数値ブレを完全に防止するため、
 * 法令確定値をマスターとしてグラウンディングし、AIは差分検出・解説・法的根拠の要約に集中させます。
 */

export interface OfficialBillingRuleMaster {
  rule_key: string;
  rule_name: string;
  category: 'treatment_addition' | 'deduction' | 'base_addition';
  official_value: number; // DB保存用の正確な数値（小数は0.096等、単位数は94等）
  display_percent?: number; // %表示用の確定数値（9.6等）
  unit_label: string;
  effective_from: string;
  official_reason: string;
  exact_mhlw_quote: string; // 厚労省告示・通知の原文直接引用
  is_obsolete?: boolean; // 包括化・廃止フラグ
}

/**
 * 現在のシステムルール一覧と、厚生労働省公式確定マスターを比較照合し、
 * 改定が必要な差異項目を100%正確に抽出する関数
 */
export function detectAdditionRuleDiscrepancies(currentRules: { rule_key: string; rule_value: number }[]) {
  const discrepancies: Array<{
    master: OfficialBillingRuleMaster;
    current_value: number;
    proposed_value: number;
  }> = [];

  const currentMap = new Map(currentRules.map(r => [r.rule_key, r.rule_value]));

  for (const master of OFFICIAL_A_TYPE_BILLING_MASTER) {
    const currentVal = currentMap.get(master.rule_key);
    if (currentVal === undefined) continue;

    // 許容誤差 0.0001
    const diff = Math.abs(currentVal - master.official_value);
    if (diff > 0.0001) {
      discrepancies.push({
        master,
        current_value: currentVal,
        proposed_value: master.official_value
      });
    }
  }

  return discrepancies;
}

/**
 * 就労継続支援B型 厚生労働省公式告示・確定ルールマスター

 * 
 * 人員配置「6:1」「7.5:1」「10:1」
 * サービス費Ⅰ〜Ⅵ、平均工賃月額に応じた報酬区分、B型特有の加算（目標工賃達成指導員加算・地域協働加算等）
 * 厚労省最新算定式: 年間工賃支払総額 ÷（年間延べ利用人数 ÷ 年間開所日数）÷ 12ヶ月
 */

/**
 * 厚生労働省告示に定める「平均工賃月額」の法定算定式
 * 算定式: 年間工賃支払総額 ÷（年間延べ利用人数 ÷ 年間開所日数）÷ 12ヶ月
 */
export function calculateTypeBAverageWage(
  arg1: number | {
    totalWagePaid?: number;
    annualTotalWage?: number;
    totalClientAttendances?: number;
    annualTotalClients?: number;
    facilityOpenDays?: number;
    annualOpenDays?: number;
    operatingMonths?: number;
  },
  arg2?: number,
  arg3?: number
): {
  averageWageMonthly: number;
  averageMonthlyWage: number;
  averageClientsPerDay: number;
  averageUsersPerDay: number;
  isCompliantWithMinimum: boolean; // 3,000円以上達成フラグ
  isBelowMinimum3000: boolean; // 3,000円未満フラグ
  formulaExplanation: string;
} {
  let annualTotalWage = 0;
  let annualTotalClients = 0;
  let annualOpenDays = 0;

  if (typeof arg1 === 'object' && arg1 !== null) {
    annualTotalWage = Number(arg1.totalWagePaid ?? arg1.annualTotalWage ?? 0);
    annualTotalClients = Number(arg1.totalClientAttendances ?? arg1.annualTotalClients ?? 0);
    annualOpenDays = Number(arg1.facilityOpenDays ?? arg1.annualOpenDays ?? 0);
  } else {
    annualTotalWage = Number(arg1 || 0);
    annualTotalClients = Number(arg2 || 0);
    annualOpenDays = Number(arg3 || 0);
  }

  if (annualTotalWage <= 0 || annualTotalClients <= 0 || annualOpenDays <= 0) {
    return {
      averageWageMonthly: 0,
      averageMonthlyWage: 0,
      averageClientsPerDay: 0,
      averageUsersPerDay: 0,
      isCompliantWithMinimum: false,
      isBelowMinimum3000: true,
      formulaExplanation: '実績データが不足しているため算定できません'
    };
  }

  // 1日あたり平均利用人数 = 年間延べ利用人数 ÷ 年間開所日数
  const averageClientsPerDay = annualTotalClients / annualOpenDays;
  const roundedClientsPerDay = Math.round(averageClientsPerDay * 10) / 10;
  // 平均工賃月額 = 年間工賃総額 ÷ 1日あたり平均利用人数 ÷ 12
  const averageWageMonthly = Math.round(annualTotalWage / averageClientsPerDay / 12);
  const isCompliantWithMinimum = averageWageMonthly >= 3000;

  return {
    averageWageMonthly,
    averageMonthlyWage: averageWageMonthly,
    averageClientsPerDay: roundedClientsPerDay,
    averageUsersPerDay: roundedClientsPerDay,
    isCompliantWithMinimum,
    isBelowMinimum3000: !isCompliantWithMinimum,
    formulaExplanation: `年間工賃 ${annualTotalWage.toLocaleString()}円 ÷ (${annualTotalClients}人 ÷ ${annualOpenDays}日) ÷ 12ヶ月 = ${averageWageMonthly.toLocaleString()}円/月`
  };
}

/**
 * 平均工賃月額と人員配置区分から該当する基本報酬区分・単位数を判定する
 */
export function determineTypeBServiceFee(
  arg1?: string | {
    averageMonthlyWage?: number;
    averageWageMonthly?: number;
    staffRatio?: string;
    staffingClass?: string;
    capacity?: number;
    useGeneralScale?: boolean;
    isFixedSystem?: boolean;
  },
  arg2?: number,
  arg3?: boolean
): {
  serviceName: string;
  feeName: string;
  serviceItemCode: string;
  serviceCode: string;
  unitScore: number;
  units: number;
  wageRange: string;
  bracketLabel: string;
  feeType: string;
} {
  let staffingClass = '6:1';
  let averageWageMonthly = 0;
  let isFixedSystem = false;

  if (typeof arg1 === 'object' && arg1 !== null) {
    staffingClass = String(arg1.staffRatio ?? arg1.staffingClass ?? '6:1');
    averageWageMonthly = Number(arg1.averageMonthlyWage ?? arg1.averageWageMonthly ?? 0);
    isFixedSystem = Boolean(arg1.useGeneralScale ?? arg1.isFixedSystem ?? false);
  } else {
    staffingClass = String(arg1 || '6:1');
    averageWageMonthly = Number(arg2 || 0);
    isFixedSystem = Boolean(arg3 || false);
  }

  if (isFixedSystem) {
    const fixed = TYPE_B_FIXED_REWARDS[staffingClass as '6:1' | '7.5:1' | '10:1'] || TYPE_B_FIXED_REWARDS['6:1'];
    return {
      serviceName: fixed.feeName,
      feeName: fixed.feeName,
      serviceItemCode: fixed.serviceItemCode,
      serviceCode: fixed.serviceItemCode,
      unitScore: fixed.unitScore,
      units: fixed.unitScore,
      wageRange: '工賃実績不問（新規開設・前年度実績なし）',
      bracketLabel: '工賃実績不問（新規開設・前年度実績なし）',
      feeType: 'fixed'
    };
  }

  const bracket = TYPE_B_WAGE_BRACKETS.find(b => {
    if (b.maxWage === null) {
      return averageWageMonthly >= b.minWage;
    }
    return averageWageMonthly >= b.minWage && averageWageMonthly < b.maxWage;
  }) || TYPE_B_WAGE_BRACKETS[TYPE_B_WAGE_BRACKETS.length - 1];

  let unitScore = bracket.units6to1;
  let serviceItemCode = bracket.serviceCode6to1;
  let feeType = '就労Ｂ型サービス費Ⅰ（6:1）';

  if (staffingClass === '7.5:1') {
    unitScore = bracket.units75to1;
    serviceItemCode = bracket.serviceCode75to1;
    feeType = '就労Ｂ型サービス費Ⅱ（7.5:1）';
  } else if (staffingClass === '10:1') {
    unitScore = bracket.units10to1;
    serviceItemCode = bracket.serviceCode10to1;
    feeType = '就労Ｂ型サービス費Ⅲ（10:1）';
  }

  const serviceName = `${feeType}（${bracket.label}）`;

  return {
    serviceName,
    feeName: serviceName,
    serviceItemCode,
    serviceCode: serviceItemCode,
    unitScore,
    units: unitScore,
    wageRange: bracket.label,
    bracketLabel: bracket.label,
    feeType
  };
}

export const TYPE_B_ADDITIONS = OFFICIAL_B_TYPE_BILLING_MASTER;
export const TYPE_B_FIXED_FEES = TYPE_B_FIXED_REWARDS;

/**
 * 現在のシステムルール一覧と、B型用厚生労働省公式確定マスターを比較照合し、
 * 改定が必要な差異項目を100%正確に抽出する関数
 */
export function detectTypeBAdditionRuleDiscrepancies(currentRules: { rule_key: string; rule_value: number }[]) {
  const discrepancies: Array<{
    master: (typeof OFFICIAL_B_TYPE_BILLING_MASTER)[number];
    current_value: number;
    proposed_value: number;
  }> = [];

  const currentMap = new Map(currentRules.map(r => [r.rule_key, r.rule_value]));

  for (const master of OFFICIAL_B_TYPE_BILLING_MASTER) {
    const currentVal = currentMap.get(master.rule_key);
    if (currentVal === undefined) continue;

    const diff = Math.abs(currentVal - master.official_value);
    if (diff > 0.0001) {
      discrepancies.push({
        master,
        current_value: currentVal,
        proposed_value: master.official_value
      });
    }
  }

  return discrepancies;
}




================================================================================
File: src/lib/compliance/paidLeaveCalculator.ts
================================================================================

import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { toLocalISODate } from "@/lib/utils";
import { getClientDayHolidayInfo } from "@/lib/compliance/holidayUtils";

export interface ClientPaidLeaveStatus {
  clientId: string;
  clientName: string;
  hireDate: string;             // 雇入れ年月日
  currentPeriodStart: string;   // 当期基準日
  currentPeriodEnd: string;     // 当期終了日 (次回基準日前日)
  serviceYears: number;         // 算定時点の勤続年数
  weeklyHours: number;          // 週所定労働時間
  weeklyDays: number;           // 週所定労働日数
  grantedDays: number;          // 当期付与日数
  isMandatoryTarget: boolean;   // 年5日取得義務対象フラグ (年10日以上付与者)
  takenDays: number;            // 取得実績日数
  takenDates: string[];         // 取得日一覧
  remainingDaysToTarget: number;// 年5日達成までの不足日数 (0〜5)
  daysUntilPeriodEnd: number;   // 期間満了までの残日数
  complianceStatus: 'achieved' | 'in_progress' | 'warning' | 'critical' | 'exempt';
  statusLabel: string;
}

/**
 * 労働基準法第39条に基づく年次有給休暇付与日数テーブル
 * キー: 'standard' (週5日以上または週30時間以上) / 4 / 3 / 2 / 1 (週所定労働日数)
 * 配列インデックス: [0.5年, 1.5年, 2.5年, 3.5年, 4.5年, 5.5年, 6.5年以上]
 */
export const STATUTORY_PAID_LEAVE_TABLE: Record<string | number, number[]> = {
  standard: [10, 11, 12, 14, 16, 18, 20],
  4: [7, 8, 9, 10, 12, 13, 15],
  3: [5, 6, 6, 8, 9, 10, 11],
  2: [3, 4, 4, 5, 6, 6, 7],
  1: [1, 2, 2, 2, 3, 3, 3]
};

function getServiceYearIndex(yearsOfService: number): number {
  if (yearsOfService < 0.5) return -1;
  if (yearsOfService < 1.5) return 0;
  if (yearsOfService < 2.5) return 1;
  if (yearsOfService < 3.5) return 2;
  if (yearsOfService < 4.5) return 3;
  if (yearsOfService < 5.5) return 4;
  if (yearsOfService < 6.5) return 5;
  return 6;
}

export function calculateStatutoryDays(
  yearsOfService: number,
  weeklyDays: number,
  weeklyHours: number = 30
): number {
  const yearIdx = getServiceYearIndex(yearsOfService);
  if (yearIdx === -1) return 0;

  const isFullTimeOrStandard = weeklyHours >= 30 || weeklyDays >= 5;
  const key = isFullTimeOrStandard ? 'standard' : weeklyDays;
  const table = STATUTORY_PAID_LEAVE_TABLE[key];
  if (!table) return 0;

  return table[yearIdx] ?? 0;
}

export const calculateStatutoryGrantDays = (
  weeklyHours: number,
  weeklyDays: number,
  yearsOfService: number
): number => {
  return calculateStatutoryDays(yearsOfService, weeklyDays, weeklyHours);
};

/**
 * 全利用者の年次有給休暇管理簿（年5日取得義務進捗）を取得
 */
export function getAllClientsPaidLeaveStatus(targetDateStr?: string): ClientPaidLeaveStatus[] {
  const today = targetDateStr ? new Date(targetDateStr) : new Date();
  const clients = db.prepare(`
    SELECT c.id, c.full_name, c.service_start_date, c.created_at, 
           c.weekly_scheduled_hours, c.regular_holidays
    FROM clients c
    WHERE c.deleted_at IS NULL AND (c.status IS NULL OR c.status = 'active')
    ORDER BY c.full_name ASC
  `).all() as any[];

  const results: ClientPaidLeaveStatus[] = [];

  for (const client of clients) {
    // 雇入れ日（service_start_date優先、なければ登録日）
    const hireDateStr = client.service_start_date || (client.created_at ? client.created_at.slice(0, 10) : toLocalISODate(today));
    const hireDate = new Date(hireDateStr);

    // 初回基準日（雇入れから6ヶ月後、民法第143条準拠: 応当日がない月末は加算先月の末日）
    const firstPeriodStart = new Date(hireDate);
    const originalDay = hireDate.getDate();
    firstPeriodStart.setMonth(firstPeriodStart.getMonth() + 6);
    if (firstPeriodStart.getDate() !== originalDay) {
      firstPeriodStart.setDate(0); // 月末日補正（例: 8/31 -> 2/28または2/29）
    }

    // 週所定労働日数・時間
    let holidaysCount = 2; // デフォルト週休2日（週5日勤務）
    try {
      const holidays = JSON.parse(client.regular_holidays || '[]');
      if (Array.isArray(holidays)) holidaysCount = holidays.length;
    } catch (e) {}
    const weeklyDays = Math.max(1, 7 - holidaysCount);
    const weeklyHours = Number(client.weekly_scheduled_hours) > 0 
      ? Number(client.weekly_scheduled_hours) 
      : (weeklyDays * 4); // 未設定時は所定日数×1日4時間ベース

    // 現在の基準日（currentPeriodStart）と次回基準日（currentPeriodEnd）の決定
    let periodStart = new Date(firstPeriodStart);
    if (today < firstPeriodStart) {
      // 雇入れ後6ヶ月未満（まだ有給付与前）
      periodStart = new Date(hireDate);
    } else {
      while (true) {
        const nextPeriod = new Date(periodStart);
        nextPeriod.setFullYear(nextPeriod.getFullYear() + 1);
        if (today < nextPeriod) {
          break;
        }
        periodStart = nextPeriod;
      }
    }

    const nextPeriodStart = new Date(periodStart);
    nextPeriodStart.setFullYear(nextPeriodStart.getFullYear() + 1);

    const periodEnd = new Date(nextPeriodStart);
    periodEnd.setDate(periodEnd.getDate() - 1);

    const periodStartStr = toLocalISODate(periodStart);
    const periodEndStr = toLocalISODate(periodEnd);

    // 勤続年数の計算（基準日時点）
    const diffTime = periodStart.getTime() - hireDate.getTime();
    const serviceYears = Math.max(0, Math.round((diffTime / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24 * 365.25)) * 10) / 10);

    // 法定出勤率（8割）の判定（労基法第39条）
    let isAttendanceRate80Percent = true;
    let calcStartStr = "";
    let calcEndStr = "";

    if (serviceYears > 0 && periodStart > hireDate) {
      if (serviceYears === 0.5) {
        calcStartStr = hireDateStr;
        calcEndStr = toLocalISODate(new Date(periodStart.getTime() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
      } else {
        const prev = new Date(periodStart);
        prev.setFullYear(prev.getFullYear() - 1);
        calcStartStr = toLocalISODate(prev);
        calcEndStr = toLocalISODate(new Date(periodStart.getTime() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
      }

      const attendanceStats = db.prepare(`
        SELECT status, COUNT(*) as count 
        FROM attendance_records 
        WHERE client_id = ? AND record_date >= ? AND record_date <= ? AND deleted_at IS NULL
        GROUP BY status
      `).all(client.id, calcStartStr, calcEndStr) as any[];

      // 労基法第39条第10項（出勤率の算定）：
      // 業務上傷病による療養休業、産前産後休業、育児・介護休業、年次有給休暇等は出勤したものとみなす
      const deemedPresentStatuses = [
        'present', 'out_facility', 'paid_leave', 'pto', 'special_leave',
        'injury_leave', 'maternity_leave', 'childcare_leave', 'care_leave'
      ];

      let loggedTotalDays = 0;
      let presentDays = 0;
      for (const stat of attendanceStats) {
        if ([...deemedPresentStatuses, 'absent'].includes(stat.status)) {
          loggedTotalDays += stat.count;
          if (stat.status !== 'absent') {
            presentDays += stat.count;
          }
        }
      }

      // 労基法第39条: 「全労働日」とは労働契約上労働義務が課せられている日。
      // 打刻未作成（無断欠勤等）により分母から脱落し出勤率が不当に高くなる欠陥を防ぐため、
      // 算定期間内のシフトおよび所定勤務パターンから契約上の所定労働日数を算出し、分母の基準とする。
      let scheduledWorkingDays = 0;
      try {
        const shiftRows = db.prepare(`
          SELECT target_date, is_holiday, holiday_type, scheduled_in, scheduled_out
          FROM client_shifts
          WHERE client_id = ? AND target_date >= ? AND target_date <= ?
        `).all(client.id, calcStartStr, calcEndStr) as any[];
        const shiftMap = new Map(shiftRows.map((s: any) => [s.target_date, s]));

        const curDate = new Date(calcStartStr);
        const endD = new Date(calcEndStr);
        while (curDate <= endD) {
          const dateIso = toLocalISODate(curDate);
          const shift = shiftMap.get(dateIso);
          const holidayInfo = getClientDayHolidayInfo(dateIso, client, shift);
          if (!holidayInfo.isHoliday) {
            scheduledWorkingDays++;
          }
          curDate.setDate(curDate.getDate() + 1);
        }
      } catch (e) {}

      const totalDays = Math.max(loggedTotalDays, scheduledWorkingDays);
      
      if (totalDays > 0) {
        isAttendanceRate80Percent = (presentDays / totalDays) >= 0.8;
      }
    }

    // 法定付与日数（出勤率8割未満の場合は0日）
    const grantedDays = isAttendanceRate80Percent ? calculateStatutoryGrantDays(weeklyHours, weeklyDays, serviceYears) : 0;
    const isMandatoryTarget = grantedDays >= 10;

    // 当期内の有給取得実績
    const paidLeaveRows = db.prepare(`
      SELECT record_date FROM attendance_records
      WHERE client_id = ? 
        AND status IN ('paid_leave', 'pto')
        AND record_date >= ? 
        AND record_date <= ?
        AND deleted_at IS NULL
      ORDER BY record_date ASC
    `).all(client.id, periodStartStr, periodEndStr) as any[];

    const takenDates = paidLeaveRows.map((r: any) => r.record_date);
    const takenDays = takenDates.length;
    const remainingDaysToTarget = Math.max(0, 5 - takenDays);

    // 残日数の計算
    const daysUntilPeriodEnd = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((periodEnd.getTime() - today.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)));

    let complianceStatus: ClientPaidLeaveStatus['complianceStatus'] = 'exempt';
    let statusLabel = '対象外 (付与10日未満)';

    if (isMandatoryTarget) {
      if (takenDays >= 5) {
        complianceStatus = 'achieved';
        statusLabel = '達成 (5日取得済)';
      } else if (daysUntilPeriodEnd <= 30) {
        complianceStatus = 'critical';
        statusLabel = `警告 (期限まで残り${daysUntilPeriodEnd}日 / 不足${remainingDaysToTarget}日)`;
      } else if (daysUntilPeriodEnd <= 90) {
        complianceStatus = 'warning';
        statusLabel = `注意 (期限まで残り${daysUntilPeriodEnd}日 / 不足${remainingDaysToTarget}日)`;
      } else {
        complianceStatus = 'in_progress';
        statusLabel = `進行中 (取得${takenDays}日 / 目標5日)`;
      }
    }

    results.push({
      clientId: client.id,
      clientName: client.full_name,
      hireDate: hireDateStr,
      currentPeriodStart: periodStartStr,
      currentPeriodEnd: periodEndStr,
      serviceYears,
      weeklyHours,
      weeklyDays,
      grantedDays,
      isMandatoryTarget,
      takenDays,
      takenDates,
      remainingDaysToTarget,
      daysUntilPeriodEnd,
      complianceStatus,
      statusLabel
    });
  }

  return results;
}

export interface StaffPaidLeaveStatus {
  staffId: string;
  staffName: string;
  role: string;
  hireDate: string;
  currentPeriodStart: string;
  currentPeriodEnd: string;
  serviceYears: number;
  weeklyHours: number;
  weeklyDays: number;
  grantedDays: number;
  isMandatoryTarget: boolean; // 年10日以上付与者
  takenDays: number;
  takenDates: string[];
  remainingDaysToTarget: number;
  daysUntilPeriodEnd: number;
  complianceStatus: 'achieved' | 'in_progress' | 'warning' | 'critical' | 'exempt';
  statusLabel: string;
}

export interface MandatoryPtoAlertItem {
  id: string;
  targetId: string;
  name: string;
  personType: 'staff' | 'client';
  personTypeLabel: string;
  hireDate: string;
  periodStart: string;
  periodEnd: string;
  grantedDays: number;
  takenDays: number;
  shortageDays: number; // 5 - takenDays
  daysUntilDeadline: number;
  isCritical: boolean; // 残り30日以内
  alertLevel: 'warning' | 'critical';
  alertMessage: string;
}

/**
 * 全職員の年次有給休暇管理簿（年5日取得義務進捗）を取得
 */
export function getAllStaffPaidLeaveStatus(targetDateStr?: string): StaffPaidLeaveStatus[] {
  const today = targetDateStr ? new Date(targetDateStr) : new Date();
  const todayStr = toLocalISODate(today);
  const staffList = db.prepare(`
    SELECT id, name, role, hire_date, created_at, weekly_hours, regular_holidays
    FROM staff
    WHERE deleted_at IS NULL
    ORDER BY name ASC
  `).all() as any[];

  const results: StaffPaidLeaveStatus[] = [];

  for (const staff of staffList) {
    const hireDateStr = staff.hire_date || (staff.created_at ? staff.created_at.slice(0, 10) : todayStr);
    const hireDate = new Date(hireDateStr);

    const firstPeriodStart = new Date(hireDate);
    const originalDay = hireDate.getDate();
    firstPeriodStart.setMonth(firstPeriodStart.getMonth() + 6);
    if (firstPeriodStart.getDate() !== originalDay) {
      firstPeriodStart.setDate(0);
    }

    let holidaysCount = 2;
    try {
      const holidays = JSON.parse(staff.regular_holidays || '[]');
      if (Array.isArray(holidays)) holidaysCount = holidays.length;
    } catch (e) {}
    const weeklyDays = Math.max(1, 7 - holidaysCount);
    const weeklyHours = Number(staff.weekly_hours) > 0 ? Number(staff.weekly_hours) : 40;

    let periodStart = new Date(firstPeriodStart);
    if (today < firstPeriodStart) {
      periodStart = new Date(hireDate);
    } else {
      while (true) {
        const nextPeriod = new Date(periodStart);
        nextPeriod.setFullYear(nextPeriod.getFullYear() + 1);
        if (today < nextPeriod) break;
        periodStart = nextPeriod;
      }
    }

    const nextPeriodStart = new Date(periodStart);
    nextPeriodStart.setFullYear(nextPeriodStart.getFullYear() + 1);
    const periodEnd = new Date(nextPeriodStart);
    periodEnd.setDate(periodEnd.getDate() - 1);

    const periodStartStr = toLocalISODate(periodStart);
    const periodEndStr = toLocalISODate(periodEnd);

    const diffTime = periodStart.getTime() - hireDate.getTime();
    const serviceYears = Math.max(0, Math.round((diffTime / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24 * 365.25)) * 10) / 10);

    const grantedDays = calculateStatutoryDays(serviceYears, weeklyDays, weeklyHours);
    const isMandatoryTarget = grantedDays >= 10;

    // 当期内の取得実績（staff_shifts から取得）
    const shiftPtoRows = db.prepare(`
      SELECT target_date FROM staff_shifts
      WHERE staff_id = ? 
        AND is_holiday = 1 
        AND holiday_type IN ('有給休暇', '有給', '有休')
        AND target_date >= ? 
        AND target_date <= ?
    `).all(staff.id, periodStartStr, periodEndStr) as any[];

    const takenDates = shiftPtoRows.map((r: any) => r.target_date);
    const takenDays = takenDates.length;
    const remainingDaysToTarget = Math.max(0, 5 - takenDays);
    const daysUntilPeriodEnd = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((periodEnd.getTime() - today.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)));

    let complianceStatus: StaffPaidLeaveStatus['complianceStatus'] = 'exempt';
    let statusLabel = '対象外 (付与10日未満)';

    if (isMandatoryTarget) {
      if (takenDays >= 5) {
        complianceStatus = 'achieved';
        statusLabel = '達成 (5日取得済)';
      } else if (daysUntilPeriodEnd <= 30) {
        complianceStatus = 'critical';
        statusLabel = `警告 (期限まで残り${daysUntilPeriodEnd}日 / 不足${remainingDaysToTarget}日)`;
      } else if (daysUntilPeriodEnd <= 90) {
        complianceStatus = 'warning';
        statusLabel = `注意 (期限まで残り${daysUntilPeriodEnd}日 / 不足${remainingDaysToTarget}日)`;
      } else {
        complianceStatus = 'in_progress';
        statusLabel = `進行中 (取得${takenDays}日 / 目標5日)`;
      }
    }

    results.push({
      staffId: staff.id,
      staffName: staff.name,
      role: staff.role || 'staff',
      hireDate: hireDateStr,
      currentPeriodStart: periodStartStr,
      currentPeriodEnd: periodEndStr,
      serviceYears,
      weeklyHours,
      weeklyDays,
      grantedDays,
      isMandatoryTarget,
      takenDays,
      takenDates,
      remainingDaysToTarget,
      daysUntilPeriodEnd,
      complianceStatus,
      statusLabel
    });
  }

  return results;
}

/**
 * 年5日有給休暇取得義務アラート一覧を取得（職員および利用者統合）
 * 基準日から9ヶ月経過（満了まで残り90日/約3ヶ月以下）で5日未達の者を抽出
 */
export function getMandatoryPtoAlerts(targetDateStr?: string): MandatoryPtoAlertItem[] {
  const clientStatuses = getAllClientsPaidLeaveStatus(targetDateStr);
  const staffStatuses = getAllStaffPaidLeaveStatus(targetDateStr);

  const alerts: MandatoryPtoAlertItem[] = [];

  // 利用者アラート
  for (const c of clientStatuses) {
    if (c.isMandatoryTarget && (c.complianceStatus === 'warning' || c.complianceStatus === 'critical')) {
      alerts.push({
        id: `client_${c.clientId}`,
        targetId: c.clientId,
        name: c.clientName,
        personType: 'client',
        personTypeLabel: '利用者',
        hireDate: c.hireDate,
        periodStart: c.currentPeriodStart,
        periodEnd: c.currentPeriodEnd,
        grantedDays: c.grantedDays,
        takenDays: c.takenDays,
        shortageDays: c.remainingDaysToTarget,
        daysUntilDeadline: c.daysUntilPeriodEnd,
        isCritical: c.complianceStatus === 'critical',
        alertLevel: c.complianceStatus,
        alertMessage: `【年5日有休義務アラート】${c.clientName}様（利用者）: 期間満了（${c.currentPeriodEnd}）まで残り${c.daysUntilPeriodEnd}日ですが、取得日数がまだ${c.takenDays}日（不足${c.remainingDaysToTarget}日）です。`
      });
    }
  }

  // 職員アラート
  for (const s of staffStatuses) {
    if (s.isMandatoryTarget && (s.complianceStatus === 'warning' || s.complianceStatus === 'critical')) {
      alerts.push({
        id: `staff_${s.staffId}`,
        targetId: s.staffId,
        name: s.staffName,
        personType: 'staff',
        personTypeLabel: '職員',
        hireDate: s.hireDate,
        periodStart: s.currentPeriodStart,
        periodEnd: s.currentPeriodEnd,
        grantedDays: s.grantedDays,
        takenDays: s.takenDays,
        shortageDays: s.remainingDaysToTarget,
        daysUntilDeadline: s.daysUntilPeriodEnd,
        isCritical: s.complianceStatus === 'critical',
        alertLevel: s.complianceStatus,
        alertMessage: `【年5日有休義務アラート】${s.staffName}職員: 期間満了（${s.currentPeriodEnd}）まで残り${s.daysUntilPeriodEnd}日ですが、取得日数がまだ${s.takenDays}日（不足${s.remainingDaysToTarget}日）です。`
      });
    }
  }

  // 残日数が少ない順（緊急度順）にソート
  alerts.sort((a, b) => a.daysUntilDeadline - b.daysUntilDeadline);
  return alerts;
}



================================================================================
File: src/lib/compliance/pdfTextExtractor.ts
================================================================================

import zlib from 'zlib';
import iconv from 'iconv-lite';
import { GoogleGenAI } from '@google/genai';
import { getApiKeyForProvider } from '@/lib/ai/modelFactory';

/**
 * テキストファイル（.txt / .md）から文字コード自動判別（UTF-8 / Shift_JIS）でテキストを抽出
 */
export function extractTextFromPlainBuffer(buffer: Buffer): string {
  let text = buffer.toString('utf8');
  // UTF-8デコード時に置換文字（U+FFFD）が含まれる場合はShift_JISとして再デコード
  if (text.includes('\uFFFD')) {
    try {
      const sjisText = iconv.decode(buffer, 'Shift_JIS');
      if (!sjisText.includes('\uFFFD')) {
        text = sjisText;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // ignore
    }
  }
  const trimmed = text.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').trim();
  if (!trimmed.includes('--- [ページ')) {
    return `--- [ページ 1] ---\n${trimmed}`;
  }
  return trimmed;
}

/**
 * PDFのバイナリストリームからローカルでテキスト（FlateDecode + UTF-16BE / リテラル文字列）を抽出するフォールバックパーサー
 */
export function extractPdfTextLocally(buffer: Buffer): string {
  try {
    const rawLatin1 = buffer.toString('latin1');
    const streamRegex = /stream[\r\n]+([\s\S]*?)[\r\n]+endstream/g;
    const pages: string[] = [];
    let currentPageChunks: string[] = [];
    let match: RegExpExecArray | null;

    const decodePdfLiteral = (s: string): string => {
      return s
        .replace(/\\n/g, '\n')
        .replace(/\\r/g, '\r')
        .replace(/\\t/g, '\t')
        .replace(/\\\(/g, '(')
        .replace(/\\\)/g, ')')
        .replace(/\\\\/g, '\\')
        .replace(/\\([0-7]{1,3})/g, (_, oct) => String.fromCharCode(parseInt(oct, 8)));
    };

    const decodeHexString = (hex: string): string => {
      const clean = hex.replace(/\s+/g, '');
      if (clean.length === 0) return '';
      // UTF-16BE BOM (FEFF) または偶数4桁単位の日本語文字列
      if (clean.toUpperCase().startsWith('FEFF') || clean.length % 4 === 0) {
        const start = clean.toUpperCase().startsWith('FEFF') ? 4 : 0;
        let out = '';
        for (let i = start; i + 3 < clean.length; i += 4) {
          const code = parseInt(clean.slice(i, i + 4), 16);
          if (!isNaN(code) && code > 0) {
            out += String.fromCharCode(code);
          }
        }
        if (out.trim().length > 0) return out;
      }
      // 1バイト単位フォールバック
      const bytes: number[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i + 1 < clean.length; i += 2) {
        bytes.push(parseInt(clean.slice(i, i + 2), 16));
      }
      return Buffer.from(bytes).toString('utf8');
    };

    const extractFromDecompressedContent = (content: string): string => {
      const lines: string[] = [];
      // BT ... ET ブロックまたはテキストオペレータを抽出
      const tokenRegex = /\((?:\\.|[^\\()])*\)|<([0-9A-Fa-f\s]+)>|(\bET\b|\bTd\b|\bTD\b|\bT\*\b)/g;
      let tMatch: RegExpExecArray | null;
      let currentLine = '';

      while ((tMatch = tokenRegex.exec(content)) !== null) {
        const full = tMatch[0];
        if (full.startsWith('(') && full.endsWith(')')) {
          currentLine += decodePdfLiteral(full.slice(1, -1));
        } else if (tMatch[1] !== undefined) {
          currentLine += decodeHexString(tMatch[1]);
        } else if (tMatch[2] !== undefined) {
          if (currentLine.trim()) {
            lines.push(currentLine.trim());
            currentLine = '';
          }
        }
      }
      if (currentLine.trim()) {
        lines.push(currentLine.trim());
      }
      return lines.join('\n');
    };

    while ((match = streamRegex.exec(rawLatin1)) !== null) {
      const streamBody = match[1];
      const streamBuf = Buffer.from(streamBody, 'latin1');
      let decompressed = '';

      try {
        decompressed = zlib.inflateSync(streamBuf).toString('latin1');
      } catch {
        try {
          decompressed = zlib.unzipSync(streamBuf).toString('latin1');
        } catch {
          decompressed = streamBody;
        }
      }

      if (decompressed.includes('BT') || decompressed.includes('Tj') || decompressed.includes('TJ')) {
        const extracted = extractFromDecompressedContent(decompressed);
        if (extracted.trim().length > 0) {
          currentPageChunks.push(extracted.trim());
          pages.push(`--- [ページ ${pages.length + 1}] ---\n${extracted.trim()}`);
          currentPageChunks = [];
        }
      }
    }

    if (pages.length > 0) {
      return pages.join('\n\n');
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Local PDF stream extraction warning:', err);
  }
  return '';
}

/**
 * PDFバッファからテキストを抽出する（Gemini Multimodal OCR/テキスト抽出 + ローカルストリーム抽出のハイブリッド）
 */
export async function extractTextFromPdfBuffer(buffer: Buffer, fileName: string): Promise<string> {
  // 1. まずローカルストリームからの即時抽出を試行
  const localExtracted = extractPdfTextLocally(buffer);

  // 2. Google Gemini APIキーが利用可能な場合は、PDFインラインデータから高精度なページ別全文抽出を実施
  const apiKey = getApiKeyForProvider('google');
  if (apiKey && buffer.byteLength <= 18 * 1024 * 1024) {
    try {
      const ai = new GoogleGenAI({ apiKey });
      const response = await ai.models.generateContent({
        model: 'gemini-2.5-flash',
        contents: [
          {
            role: 'user',
            parts: [
              {
                inlineData: {
                  mimeType: 'application/pdf',
                  data: buffer.toString('base64'),
                },
              },
              {
                text: `このPDF資料（${fileName}）に含まれるすべての文章・条文・数値・表の内容を、ページごとに「--- [ページ 1] ---」「--- [ページ 2] ---」の区切り見出しを付けて、一字一句省略・要約・改変せずにそのままテキストとして抽出してください。`,
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
      });

      const geminiText = response.text?.trim();
      if (geminiText && geminiText.length > 20) {
        return geminiText;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Gemini inline PDF extraction skipped/failed, using local stream extraction:', e);
    }
  }

  if (localExtracted && localExtracted.trim().length > 0) {
    return localExtracted;
  }

  return `--- [ページ 1] ---\n[PDFバイナリ保存済: ${fileName} (${Math.round(buffer.byteLength / 1024)} KB)]\n※このPDFはスキャン画像または特殊フォント形式で保存されています。AIチャットおよび法改正照合時にはPDF原本データが直接マルチモーダル解析されます。`;
}


================================================================================
File: src/lib/compliance/recordIntegrityChecker.ts
================================================================================

import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { toLocalISODate } from "@/lib/utils";

export interface IntegrityError {
  clientId: string;
  clientName: string;
  recordDate: string;
  type: 'missing_support_record' | 'missing_attendance' | 'time_mismatch' | 'offsite_mismatch';
  message: string;
}

function parseTime(timeStr: string | null): number | null {
  if (!timeStr) return null;
  const parts = timeStr.split(':');
  if (parts.length !== 2) return null;
  return parseInt(parts[0], 10) * 60 + parseInt(parts[1], 10);
}

export function getIntegrityErrors(targetDatePrefix: string, clientId?: string): IntegrityError[] {
  const errors: IntegrityError[] = [];
  
  // 指定月(または日)における、出勤日または支援記録がある日をすべて抽出
  const datesQuery = db.prepare(`
    SELECT DISTINCT client_id, record_date FROM attendance_records WHERE record_date LIKE ? AND deleted_at IS NULL
    UNION
    SELECT DISTINCT client_id, record_date FROM support_records WHERE record_date LIKE ? AND deleted_at IS NULL
  `).all(`${targetDatePrefix}%`, `${targetDatePrefix}%`) as {client_id: string, record_date: string}[];

  const filteredDates = clientId ? datesQuery.filter(d => d.client_id === clientId) : datesQuery;

  for (const {client_id, record_date} of filteredDates) {
    const client = db.prepare('SELECT full_name FROM clients WHERE id = ?').get(client_id) as any;
    if (!client) continue;

    const att = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM attendance_records WHERE client_id = ? AND record_date = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL').get(client_id, record_date) as any;
    const sup = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM support_records WHERE client_id = ? AND record_date = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL').get(client_id, record_date) as any;

    const attActive = att && ['present', 'out_facility'].includes(att.status);
    
    // 1. 勤怠(出勤)があるのに支援記録がない
    if (attActive && !sup) {
      errors.push({ 
        clientId: client_id, 
        clientName: client.full_name, 
        recordDate: record_date, 
        type: 'missing_support_record', 
        message: '勤怠実績(出勤)がありますが、支援記録が未作成です。' 
      });
    }
    // 2. 支援記録があるのに勤怠(出勤)がないケース
    else if (sup && !att) {
      // 勤怠自体が完全に未登録
      errors.push({ 
        clientId: client_id, 
        clientName: client.full_name, 
        recordDate: record_date, 
        type: 'missing_attendance', 
        message: '支援記録が存在しますが、勤怠実績自体が登録されていません。' 
      });
    }
    else if (sup && att && !attActive && att.status !== 'absent') {
      // 出勤・欠席以外（公休など）なのに支援記録がある場合
      errors.push({ 
        clientId: client_id, 
        clientName: client.full_name, 
        recordDate: record_date, 
        type: 'missing_attendance', 
        message: `支援記録が存在しますが、対応する出勤実績がありません。（勤怠ステータス: ${att.status}）` 
      });
    }
    // 3. 両方ある場合の突合（出勤の場合のみ時間・場所を突合）
    else if (attActive && sup) {
      const attStart = parseTime(att.clock_in);
      const attEnd = parseTime(att.clock_out);
      
      const supStart = parseTime(sup.service_start_time);
      const supEnd = parseTime(sup.service_end_time);

      if (attStart !== null && attEnd !== null && supStart !== null && supEnd !== null) {
        const startDiff = Math.abs(attStart - supStart);
        const endDiff = Math.abs(attEnd - supEnd);
        
        if (startDiff > 30 || endDiff > 30) {
          errors.push({
            clientId: client_id, 
            clientName: client.full_name, 
            recordDate: record_date, 
            type: 'time_mismatch',
            message: `打刻時間(${att.clock_in}〜${att.clock_out})とサービス提供時間(${sup.service_start_time}〜${sup.service_end_time})に30分以上の乖離があります。`
          });
        }
      }
      
      const attIsOffsite = att.status === 'out_facility' || att.is_offsite === 1;
      const loc = (sup.service_location || '').trim();
      
      // 支援記録における場所種別の判定:
      // 事業所内・施設内・在宅・電話連絡・空欄以外（「施設外」「施設外就労先企業」「ハウスプロメイン」などの企業名）を施設外と判定
      const isOnsite = ['施設内', '事業所内', ''].includes(loc);
      const isTelework = ['在宅', 'テレワーク'].includes(loc);
      const isContactOnly = ['電話・連絡', '電話', '連絡'].includes(loc);
      const supIsOffsite = !isOnsite && !isTelework && !isContactOnly;

      if (attIsOffsite !== supIsOffsite) {
        errors.push({
          clientId: client_id, 
          clientName: client.full_name, 
          recordDate: record_date, 
          type: 'offsite_mismatch',
          message: `勤怠と支援記録の「施設外就労」フラグが不一致です。（勤怠: ${attIsOffsite ? '施設外' : '施設内等'}, 支援記録: ${loc || '未入力'}）`
        });
      }
    }
  }

  // 日付順・名前順にソート
  errors.sort((a, b) => {
    if (a.recordDate !== b.recordDate) return a.recordDate.localeCompare(b.recordDate);
    return a.clientName.localeCompare(b.clientName);
  });

  return errors;
}


================================================================================
File: src/lib/compliance/staffingCalculator.ts
