import { db } from "@/lib/db";

export interface DailyStaffingStatus {
  targetDate: string;
  // 全体
  clientPresentCount: number; // 総出勤利用者数（下位互換）
  totalClientCount: number; // 総出勤利用者数
  isEstimated?: boolean; // 実績未登録時の予定ベース判定フラグ
  staffingClass: string; // '7.5:1' or '10:1'
  ratioDenominator: number; // 7.5 or 10
  requiredFte: number; // 全体必要常勤換算数
  requiredMinutes: number; // 全体必要勤務時間(分)
  actualStaffMinutes: number; // 全体実勤務シフト合計(分)
  actualFte: number; // 全体実常勤換算数
  hasFullTimeStaff: boolean; // 直接処遇の常勤職員が存在するか
  isCompliant: boolean; // 総合基準充足フラグ（内勤・施設外・全体のすべて充足）
  shortageMinutes: number; // 不足時間(分)
  complianceReason?: string; // 不足・違反理由

  // 内勤（事業所本体）専用
  inFacilityClientCount: number; // 内勤の出勤利用者数
  inFacilityStaffCount: number; // 内勤の直接処遇職員数
  inFacilityStaffMinutes: number; // 内勤の直接処遇職員時間(分)
  inFacilityRequiredMinutes: number; // 内勤利用者に必要な勤務時間(分)
  inFacilityRequiredFte: number; // 内勤必要FTE
  inFacilityActualFte: number; // 内勤実FTE
  inFacilityShortageMinutes: number; // 内勤不足時間(分)
  hasInFacilityFullTimeStaff: boolean; // 内勤に直接処遇常勤専従職員がいるか
  isInFacilityCompliant: boolean; // 内勤配置基準充足フラグ

  // 施設外就労専用
  offsiteClientCount: number; // 施設外就労の利用者数
  offsiteStaffCount: number; // 施設外就労の引率職員数
  offsiteStaffMinutes: number; // 施設外就労の引率時間(分)
  isOffsiteCompliant: boolean; // 施設外就労配置基準充足フラグ（引率専任配置要件等）
  offsiteWorkplaceDetails?: Array<{
    workplaceId: string;
    workplaceName: string;
    clientCount: number;
    staffCount: number;
    isCompliant: boolean;
  }>;
  // 引率職員未配置の対象利用者詳細
  unassignedOffsiteClients?: Array<{
    attendanceId?: string;
    clientId: string;
    clientName: string;
    workplaceId?: string | null;
    workplaceName: string;
    workType: string;
  }>;
  // 現場ごとの引率体制サマリー
  offsiteAssignments?: Array<{
    workplaceId: string;
    workplaceName: string;
    staffId: string | null;
    staffName: string | null;
    clientCount: number;
    clients: Array<{ clientId: string; clientName: string; attendanceId?: string }>;
    isCompliant: boolean;
  }>;
}

/**
 * 指定日の人員配置基準充足状況を計算する。
 * 厚労省令・告示に基づき、以下の3段階で独立判定を行う:
 * 1. 【内勤（本体事業所）判定】: 内勤利用者のみを対象に、内勤職員の勤務時間（7.5:1 / 10:1）＆ 常勤専従 ＆ 本体常時配置（1名以上）を判定。
 * 2. 【施設外就労判定】: 施設外就労利用者に対し、作業場所（現場ユニット）ごとに専任引率職員（1名以上）が配置されているかを判定。
 * 3. 【事業所全体判定】: 事業所全体（本体＋施設外）の総利用者数に対し、総直接処遇職員が法定比率を満たしているかを判定。
 */
export function calculateDailyStaffingStatus(targetDate: string): DailyStaffingStatus {
  // 1. 事業所設定から配置区分および週所定労働時間を取得
  const facility = db.prepare(`SELECT staffing_class, regular_hours_per_week FROM facility_settings WHERE id = 'default'`).get() as any;
  const staffingClass = facility?.staffing_class || '10:1';
  const ratioDenominator = staffingClass === '6:1' ? 6 : staffingClass === '7.5:1' ? 7.5 : 10;
  // 週所定労働時間（未設定時は法定の週40時間、週5日勤務換算で1日8時間=480分）
  const regularHoursPerWeek = facility?.regular_hours_per_week ? Number(facility.regular_hours_per_week) : 40;
  const dailyStandardMinutes = Math.max(240, Math.round((regularHoursPerWeek / 5) * 60)); // 最低4時間

  // 2. 当日の出勤利用者数を「内勤」と「施設外就労」に分離して集計（実績優先、なければシフト予定を参照）
  const clientStats = db.prepare(`
    SELECT 
      COUNT(*) as total_cnt,
      COUNT(CASE WHEN work_type = 'out_facility' OR (is_offsite = 1 AND work_type != 'both') THEN 1 END) as offsite_cnt,
      COUNT(CASE WHEN work_type = 'both' THEN 1 END) as both_cnt,
      COUNT(CASE WHEN (work_type = 'in_facility' OR work_type IS NULL) AND (is_offsite = 0 OR is_offsite IS NULL) THEN 1 END) as infacility_cnt
    FROM attendance_records 
    WHERE record_date = ? AND status = 'present' AND deleted_at IS NULL
  `).get(targetDate) as any;

  let totalClientCount = clientStats?.total_cnt || 0;
  let bothCount = clientStats?.both_cnt || 0;
  let inFacilityClientCount = (clientStats?.infacility_cnt || 0) + bothCount;
  let offsiteClientCount = (clientStats?.offsite_cnt || 0) + bothCount;
  let isEstimated = false;

  // 施設外就労先現場ごとの利用者および引率職員情報
  const offsiteWorkplaceMap: Record<string, { 
    workplaceId: string; 
    workplaceName: string; 
    clientCount: number; 
    staffIds: Set<string>;
    staffNames: Set<string>;
    clients: Array<{ clientId: string; clientName: string; attendanceId?: string }>;
  }> = {};
  const unassignedOffsiteClients: Array<{
    attendanceId?: string;
    clientId: string;
    clientName: string;
    workplaceId?: string | null;
    workplaceName: string;
    workType: string;
  }> = [];

  if (totalClientCount > 0) {
    const offsiteRecords = db.prepare(`
      SELECT 
        a.id as attendance_id,
        a.client_id,
        c.full_name as client_name,
        a.work_type,
        a.offsite_workplace_id,
        COALESCE(w.name, '施設外就労先') as workplace_name,
        a.offsite_staff_id,
        s.name as staff_name
      FROM attendance_records a
      JOIN clients c ON a.client_id = c.id
      LEFT JOIN offsite_workplaces w ON a.offsite_workplace_id = w.id
      LEFT JOIN staff s ON a.offsite_staff_id = s.id
      WHERE a.record_date = ? AND a.status = 'present' AND (a.work_type IN ('out_facility', 'both') OR a.is_offsite = 1) AND a.deleted_at IS NULL
    `).all(targetDate) as any[];

    for (const r of offsiteRecords) {
      const wpId = r.offsite_workplace_id || 'default_offsite';
      if (!offsiteWorkplaceMap[wpId]) {
        offsiteWorkplaceMap[wpId] = {
          workplaceId: wpId,
          workplaceName: r.workplace_name,
          clientCount: 0,
          staffIds: new Set<string>(),
          staffNames: new Set<string>(),
          clients: []
        };
      }
      offsiteWorkplaceMap[wpId].clientCount++;
      offsiteWorkplaceMap[wpId].clients.push({
        clientId: r.client_id,
        clientName: r.client_name,
        attendanceId: r.attendance_id
      });
      if (r.offsite_staff_id) {
        offsiteWorkplaceMap[wpId].staffIds.add(r.offsite_staff_id);
        if (r.staff_name) offsiteWorkplaceMap[wpId].staffNames.add(r.staff_name);
      } else {
        unassignedOffsiteClients.push({
          attendanceId: r.attendance_id,
          clientId: r.client_id,
          clientName: r.client_name,
          workplaceId: r.offsite_workplace_id,
          workplaceName: r.workplace_name,
          workType: r.work_type || 'out_facility'
        });
      }
    }
  }

  // 実績がない場合はシフト予定 (client_shifts) を集計
  if (totalClientCount === 0) {
    const scheduledStats = db.prepare(`
      SELECT 
        COUNT(*) as total_cnt,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN cs.work_type = 'out_facility' OR (cs.is_offsite = 1 AND cs.work_type != 'both') THEN 1 END) as offsite_cnt,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN cs.work_type = 'both' THEN 1 END) as both_cnt,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN (cs.work_type = 'in_facility' OR cs.work_type IS NULL) AND (cs.is_offsite = 0 OR cs.is_offsite IS NULL) THEN 1 END) as infacility_cnt
      FROM client_shifts cs
      JOIN clients c ON cs.client_id = c.id
      WHERE cs.target_date = ? 
        AND (cs.is_holiday = 0 OR cs.is_holiday IS NULL)
        AND cs.scheduled_in IS NOT NULL AND cs.scheduled_in != ''
        AND c.deleted_at IS NULL
    `).get(targetDate) as any;

    if (scheduledStats && scheduledStats.total_cnt > 0) {
      totalClientCount = scheduledStats.total_cnt;
      const scheduledBoth = scheduledStats.both_cnt || 0;
      inFacilityClientCount = (scheduledStats.infacility_cnt || 0) + scheduledBoth;
      offsiteClientCount = (scheduledStats.offsite_cnt || 0) + scheduledBoth;
      isEstimated = true;

      // 予定の現場集計
      const scheduledOffsite = db.prepare(`
        SELECT 
          cs.id as shift_id,
          cs.client_id,
          c.full_name as client_name,
          cs.work_type,
          cs.offsite_workplace_id,
          COALESCE(w.name, '施設外就労先') as workplace_name
        FROM client_shifts cs
        JOIN clients c ON cs.client_id = c.id
        LEFT JOIN offsite_workplaces w ON cs.offsite_workplace_id = w.id
        WHERE cs.target_date = ? 
          AND (cs.is_holiday = 0 OR cs.is_holiday IS NULL)
          AND (cs.work_type IN ('out_facility', 'both') OR cs.is_offsite = 1)
          AND c.deleted_at IS NULL
      `).all(targetDate) as any[];

      for (const r of scheduledOffsite) {
        const wpId = r.offsite_workplace_id || 'default_offsite';
        if (!offsiteWorkplaceMap[wpId]) {
          offsiteWorkplaceMap[wpId] = {
            workplaceId: wpId,
            workplaceName: r.workplace_name,
            clientCount: 0,
            staffIds: new Set<string>(),
            staffNames: new Set<string>(),
            clients: []
          };
        }
        offsiteWorkplaceMap[wpId].clientCount++;
        offsiteWorkplaceMap[wpId].clients.push({
          clientId: r.client_id,
          clientName: r.client_name
        });
      }
    } else {
      // client_shifts も空の場合、定休日設定から推計（内勤扱い）
      const dObj = new Date(targetDate);
      const dayOfWeek = dObj.getDay().toString();
      const isFacilityHoliday = db.prepare(`SELECT 1 FROM facility_holidays WHERE target_date = ?`).get(targetDate);
      
      if (!isFacilityHoliday && dayOfWeek !== '0' && dayOfWeek !== '6') {
        const allClients = db.prepare(`
          SELECT regular_holidays 
          FROM clients 
          WHERE deleted_at IS NULL 
            AND (contract_end_date IS NULL OR contract_end_date >= ?)
            AND (service_start_date IS NULL OR service_start_date <= ?)
        `).all(targetDate, targetDate) as any[];
        
        let defaultScheduled = 0;
        for (const c of allClients) {
          try {
            const holidays = JSON.parse(c.regular_holidays || '[]');
            if (!holidays.includes(dayOfWeek)) {
              defaultScheduled++;
            }
          } catch(e) {
            defaultScheduled++;
          }
        }
        if (defaultScheduled > 0) {
          totalClientCount = defaultScheduled;
          inFacilityClientCount = defaultScheduled;
          offsiteClientCount = 0;
          isEstimated = true;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // 3. 当日の直接処遇職員（職業指導員・生活支援員）のシフト集計
  // ※他事業所勤務（GH勤務等）は完全除外
  const shifts = db.prepare(`
    SELECT ss.*, s.employment_type, s.name, s.job_category, s.role, s.fte_ratio, s.is_concurrent
    FROM staff_shifts ss
    JOIN staff s ON ss.staff_id = s.id
    WHERE ss.target_date = ? 
      AND (ss.is_holiday = 0 OR ss.is_holiday IS NULL)
      AND (ss.is_other_facility = 0 OR ss.is_other_facility IS NULL)
      AND (ss.holiday_type NOT IN ('他事業所', '他事業所勤務', 'GH', 'GH勤務', 'グループホーム') OR ss.holiday_type IS NULL)
      AND s.deleted_at IS NULL
      AND (
        s.job_category IN ('職業指導員', '生活支援員')
        OR s.role IN ('vocational_instructor', 'life_support_worker')
      )
      AND (
        s.job_category NOT IN (
          '管理者', 'サービス管理責任者', '管理者 兼 サービス管理責任者', 
          '賃金向上達成指導員', '視覚・聴覚言語障害者支援指導員', '重度者支援専門員', 
          '送迎ドライバー', '調理員', '事務員'
        )
        OR s.job_category IS NULL
      )
  `).all(targetDate) as any[];

  // 実績の引率職員IDセット
  const actualOffsiteStaffIds = new Set<string>();
  Object.values(offsiteWorkplaceMap).forEach(w => {
    w.staffIds.forEach(id => actualOffsiteStaffIds.add(id));
  });

  let inFacilityStaffMinutes = 0;
  let inFacilityStaffCount = 0;
  let hasInFacilityFullTimeStaff = false;

  let offsiteStaffMinutes = 0;
  let offsiteStaffCount = 0;
  const offsiteStaffAssignedWorkplaces: Record<string, number> = {};

  let totalStaffMinutes = 0;
  let hasFullTimeStaff = false;

  for (const s of shifts) {
    if (s.scheduled_in && s.scheduled_out) {
      const [sh, sm] = s.scheduled_in.split(':').map(Number);
      const [eh, em] = s.scheduled_out.split(':').map(Number);
      let inM = sh * 60 + sm;
      let outM = eh * 60 + em;
      if (outM < inM) outM += 24 * 60;
      let duration = outM - inM;
      // 労働基準法第34条: 6時間超で45分、8時間超で60分（6時間ちょうどは休憩不要）
      if (duration > 480) {
        duration -= 60;
      } else if (duration > 360) {
        duration -= 45;
      }
      if (s.is_concurrent && s.fte_ratio && s.fte_ratio < 1.0) {
        duration = Math.round(duration * s.fte_ratio);
      }

      if (duration > 0) {
        totalStaffMinutes += duration;

        // 施設外就労引率職員かどうかの判定:
        // ① シフトで is_offsite = 1 または holiday_type に施設外/引率と記載
        // ② または出勤実績の同行職員（offsite_staff_id）に指定されている
        // ③ または work_type が 'out_facility' または 'both'
        const isOffsiteStaff = (s.is_offsite === 1) || 
          actualOffsiteStaffIds.has(s.staff_id) ||
          (s.work_type === 'out_facility') ||
          (s.holiday_type && ['施設外', '施設外就労', '引率', '施設外引率'].includes(s.holiday_type)) ||
          (s.other_facility_name && s.other_facility_name.includes('施設外'));

        // 時間分割（併用勤務）の判定:
        const hasSplitTimes = (s.work_type === 'both') || (s.offsite_start_time && s.offsite_end_time);

        if (hasSplitTimes && s.offsite_start_time && s.offsite_end_time) {
          // 施設外時間と内勤時間を正確に分割按分
          const [osh, osm] = s.offsite_start_time.split(':').map(Number);
          const [oeh, oem] = s.offsite_end_time.split(':').map(Number);
          let offsiteM = (oeh * 60 + oem) - (osh * 60 + osm);
          if (offsiteM < 0) offsiteM += 24 * 60;
          offsiteM = Math.min(duration, Math.max(0, offsiteM));
          const inFacilityM = Math.max(0, duration - offsiteM);

          if (offsiteM > 0) {
            offsiteStaffMinutes += offsiteM;
            offsiteStaffCount++;
            const wpId = s.offsite_workplace_id || 'default_offsite';
            offsiteStaffAssignedWorkplaces[wpId] = (offsiteStaffAssignedWorkplaces[wpId] || 0) + 1;
          }
          if (inFacilityM > 0) {
            inFacilityStaffMinutes += inFacilityM;
            inFacilityStaffCount++;
            if ((s.employment_type === '常勤' || s.employment_type === 'fulltime') && (!s.is_concurrent || (s.fte_ratio || 1.0) >= 1.0)) {
              hasInFacilityFullTimeStaff = true;
            }
          }
        } else if (isOffsiteStaff) {
          offsiteStaffMinutes += duration;
          offsiteStaffCount++;
          const wpId = s.offsite_workplace_id || 'default_offsite';
          offsiteStaffAssignedWorkplaces[wpId] = (offsiteStaffAssignedWorkplaces[wpId] || 0) + 1;
        } else {
          // 内勤（事業所本体）職員
          inFacilityStaffMinutes += duration;
          inFacilityStaffCount++;
          if ((s.employment_type === '常勤' || s.employment_type === 'fulltime') && (!s.is_concurrent || (s.fte_ratio || 1.0) >= 1.0)) {
            hasInFacilityFullTimeStaff = true;
          }
        }

        if ((s.employment_type === '常勤' || s.employment_type === 'fulltime') && (!s.is_concurrent || (s.fte_ratio || 1.0) >= 1.0)) {
          hasFullTimeStaff = true;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // staff_shifts が未登録の場合、定休日設定から推計（全て内勤扱い）
  if (shifts.length === 0) {
    const dObj = new Date(targetDate);
    const dayOfWeek = dObj.getDay().toString();
    const isFacilityHoliday = db.prepare(`SELECT 1 FROM facility_holidays WHERE target_date = ?`).get(targetDate);

    if (!isFacilityHoliday) {
      const allDirectStaff = db.prepare(`
        SELECT id, name, employment_type, fte_ratio, is_concurrent, regular_holidays, work_start_time, work_end_time, break_time_minutes
        FROM staff
        WHERE deleted_at IS NULL
          AND (
            job_category IN ('職業指導員', '生活支援員')
            OR role IN ('vocational_instructor', 'life_support_worker')
          )
          AND (
            job_category NOT IN (
              '管理者', 'サービス管理責任者', '管理者 兼 サービス管理責任者', 
              '賃金向上達成指導員', '視覚・聴覚言語障害者支援指導員', '重度者支援専門員', 
              '送迎ドライバー', '調理員', '事務員'
            )
            OR job_category IS NULL
          )
      `).all() as any[];

      for (const s of allDirectStaff) {
        let isOff = false;
        try {
          const holidays = JSON.parse(s.regular_holidays || '[]');
          if (holidays.includes(dayOfWeek)) {
            isOff = true;
          }
        } catch(e) {}

        if (!isOff) {
          let duration = 0;
          if (s.work_start_time && s.work_end_time) {
            const [sh, sm] = s.work_start_time.split(':').map(Number);
            const [eh, em] = s.work_end_time.split(':').map(Number);
            let inM = sh * 60 + sm;
            let outM = eh * 60 + em;
            if (outM < inM) outM += 24 * 60;
            let gross = outM - inM;
            const brk = s.break_time_minutes ?? (gross >= 360 ? 60 : 0);
            duration = Math.max(0, gross - brk);
            if (s.is_concurrent && s.fte_ratio && s.fte_ratio < 1.0) {
              duration = Math.round(duration * s.fte_ratio);
            }
          } else {
            duration = Math.round(dailyStandardMinutes * (s.fte_ratio || 1.0));
          }

          // 実績の引率職員指定があれば引率、なければ内勤
          if (actualOffsiteStaffIds.has(s.id)) {
            offsiteStaffMinutes += duration;
            offsiteStaffCount++;
          } else {
            inFacilityStaffMinutes += duration;
            inFacilityStaffCount++;
            if ((s.employment_type === '常勤' || s.employment_type === 'fulltime') && (!s.is_concurrent || (s.fte_ratio || 1.0) >= 1.0)) {
              hasInFacilityFullTimeStaff = true;
            }
          }

          totalStaffMinutes += duration;
          if ((s.employment_type === '常勤' || s.employment_type === 'fulltime') && (!s.is_concurrent || (s.fte_ratio || 1.0) >= 1.0)) {
            hasFullTimeStaff = true;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // --- C. 事業所全体判定 ---
  // 常勤換算の不正かさ上げを防止するため、1人あたりのFTE上限を1.0にキャップ
  // （ここでは全体スタッフ分のみ厳密にキャップしてFTEを計算する）
  // 既存の inFacilityStaffMinutes や offsiteStaffMinutes は時間不足計算用にそのまま利用
  let cappedActualFte = 0;
  // 職員ごとのIDを集計するためのマップ作成
  const dailyStaffMinutesMap = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const s of shifts) {
    if (s.scheduled_in && s.scheduled_out) {
      const [sh, sm] = s.scheduled_in.split(':').map(Number);
      const [eh, em] = s.scheduled_out.split(':').map(Number);
      let inM = sh * 60 + sm;
      let outM = eh * 60 + em;
      if (outM < inM) outM += 24 * 60;
      let duration = outM - inM;
      if (duration > 480) duration -= 60;
      else if (duration > 360) duration -= 45;
      if (s.is_concurrent && s.fte_ratio && s.fte_ratio < 1.0) {
        duration = Math.round(duration * s.fte_ratio);
      }
      if (duration > 0) {
        dailyStaffMinutesMap.set(s.staff_id, (dailyStaffMinutesMap.get(s.staff_id) || 0) + duration);
      }
    }
  }
  // シフト未登録時の推計分も加算
  if (shifts.length === 0) {
    // 既に totalStaffMinutes などで概算済みの場合、FTEキャップ済みの概算とする
    cappedActualFte = Math.round((totalStaffMinutes / dailyStandardMinutes) * 100) / 100;
  } else {
    for (const [_, mins] of dailyStaffMinutesMap) {
      cappedActualFte += Math.min(1.0, mins / dailyStandardMinutes);
    }
    cappedActualFte = Math.round(cappedActualFte * 100) / 100;
  }

  // 4. 三段階の人員配置基準チェック
  // --- A. 内勤（事業所本体）判定 ---
  const inFacilityRequiredFte = inFacilityClientCount > 0 ? Math.round((inFacilityClientCount / ratioDenominator) * 100) / 100 : 0;
  const inFacilityRequiredMinutes = Math.round(inFacilityRequiredFte * dailyStandardMinutes);
  const inFacilityShortageMinutes = Math.max(0, inFacilityRequiredMinutes - inFacilityStaffMinutes);
  const inFacilityActualFte = Math.round((inFacilityStaffMinutes / dailyStandardMinutes) * 100) / 100;

  // 内勤適合条件: 
  // 利用者が0名ならOK。利用者がいる場合:
  // ① 勤務時間が必要時間を満たしていること
  // ② 直接処遇職員が最低1名以上稼働していること（常時配置要件。全員施設外に出て本体0名は違反）
  // ③ 常勤専従職員が稼働していること
  const isInFacilityHoursOk = inFacilityStaffMinutes >= inFacilityRequiredMinutes;
  const isInFacilityStaffPresent = inFacilityStaffCount >= 1;
  const isInFacilityCompliant = inFacilityClientCount === 0 || (isInFacilityHoursOk && isInFacilityStaffPresent && hasInFacilityFullTimeStaff);

  // --- B. 施設外就労判定 ---
  // 施設外就労適合条件:
  // 施設外利用者が0名ならOK。
  // 施設外利用者がいる場合、直接処遇の引率職員が1名以上配置されていること（現場ごとに1名以上）
  const offsiteWorkplaceDetails: Array<{ workplaceId: string; workplaceName: string; clientCount: number; staffCount: number; isCompliant: boolean }> = [];
  const offsiteAssignments: Array<{
    workplaceId: string;
    workplaceName: string;
    staffId: string | null;
    staffName: string | null;
    clientCount: number;
    clients: Array<{ clientId: string; clientName: string; attendanceId?: string }>;
    isCompliant: boolean;
  }> = [];
  let isAllWorkplacesCovered = true;

  if (offsiteClientCount > 0) {
    const workplaceEntries = Object.values(offsiteWorkplaceMap);
    if (workplaceEntries.length === 0) {
      // 現場区分未登録の場合、全体の引率職員数で判定
      isAllWorkplacesCovered = offsiteStaffCount >= 1;
      offsiteWorkplaceDetails.push({
        workplaceId: 'default',
        workplaceName: '施設外就労現場',
        clientCount: offsiteClientCount,
        staffCount: offsiteStaffCount,
        isCompliant: offsiteStaffCount >= 1
      });
      offsiteAssignments.push({
        workplaceId: 'default',
        workplaceName: '施設外就労現場',
        staffId: null,
        staffName: offsiteStaffCount >= 1 ? '引率職員あり' : null,
        clientCount: offsiteClientCount,
        clients: [],
        isCompliant: offsiteStaffCount >= 1
      });
    } else {
      for (const w of workplaceEntries) {
        // 当該現場に割り当てられた職員数（実績またはシフト）
        const staffCnt = w.staffIds.size > 0 ? w.staffIds.size : (offsiteStaffAssignedWorkplaces[w.workplaceId] || offsiteStaffCount);
        const wpCompliant = staffCnt >= 1;
        if (!wpCompliant) isAllWorkplacesCovered = false;
        offsiteWorkplaceDetails.push({
          workplaceId: w.workplaceId,
          workplaceName: w.workplaceName,
          clientCount: w.clientCount,
          staffCount: staffCnt,
          isCompliant: wpCompliant
        });

        const assignedStaffNames = Array.from(w.staffNames);
        const staffNameDisplay = assignedStaffNames.length > 0 ? assignedStaffNames.join('、') : null;
        const firstStaffId = Array.from(w.staffIds)[0] || null;

        offsiteAssignments.push({
          workplaceId: w.workplaceId,
          workplaceName: w.workplaceName,
          staffId: firstStaffId,
          staffName: staffNameDisplay,
          clientCount: w.clientCount,
          clients: w.clients,
          isCompliant: wpCompliant
        });
      }
    }
  }
  const isOffsiteCompliant = offsiteClientCount === 0 || (offsiteStaffCount >= 1 && isAllWorkplacesCovered);

  // --- C. 事業所全体判定 ---
  const requiredFte = totalClientCount > 0 ? Math.round((totalClientCount / ratioDenominator) * 100) / 100 : 0;
  const requiredMinutes = Math.round(requiredFte * dailyStandardMinutes);
  const shortageMinutes = Math.max(0, requiredMinutes - totalStaffMinutes);
  const actualFte = cappedActualFte;
  const isTotalCompliant = totalClientCount === 0 || (actualFte >= requiredFte && hasFullTimeStaff);

  // --- 総合判定 ---
  const isCompliant = totalClientCount === 0 || (isInFacilityCompliant && isOffsiteCompliant && isTotalCompliant);

  // 不適合理由の構築
  let complianceReason: string | undefined;
  if (!isCompliant) {
    const reasons: string[] = [];
    if (inFacilityClientCount > 0 && !isInFacilityStaffPresent) {
      reasons.push(`本体事業所に内勤利用者(${inFacilityClientCount}名)がいますが、内勤直接処遇職員が不在です（全員施設外または不在・常時配置義務違反）`);
    } else if (inFacilityClientCount > 0 && inFacilityShortageMinutes > 0) {
      reasons.push(`内勤直接処遇時間が不足しています(-${Math.round((inFacilityShortageMinutes / 60) * 10) / 10}h)`);
    } else if (inFacilityClientCount > 0 && !hasInFacilityFullTimeStaff) {
      reasons.push(`本体事業所（内勤）に直接処遇の常勤職員が配置されていません（省令第189条違反）`);
    }

    if (offsiteClientCount > 0 && !isOffsiteCompliant) {
      const nonCompliantWps = offsiteWorkplaceDetails.filter(w => !w.isCompliant).map(w => w.workplaceName);
      const unassignedNames = unassignedOffsiteClients.map(u => u.clientName);
      if (unassignedNames.length > 0) {
        const namePreview = unassignedNames.length <= 4 
          ? unassignedNames.join('、') 
          : `${unassignedNames.slice(0, 4).join('、')} ほか${unassignedNames.length - 4}名`;
        reasons.push(`施設外就労（${nonCompliantWps.join('・') || '現場'}）に専任引率職員が未配置です（対象: ${namePreview}）`);
      } else if (nonCompliantWps.length > 0) {
        reasons.push(`施設外就労（${nonCompliantWps.join('・')}）に専任引率職員が未配置です（引率配置基準違反）`);
      } else {
        reasons.push(`施設外就労利用者(${offsiteClientCount}名)に対して引率職員が配置されていません`);
      }
    }

    if (totalClientCount > 0 && shortageMinutes > 0 && !reasons.some(r => r.includes('不足'))) {
      reasons.push(`事業所全体の勤務時間が不足しています(-${Math.round((shortageMinutes / 60) * 10) / 10}h)`);
    }

    complianceReason = reasons.join(' / ') || '人員配置基準を満たしていません';
  }

  return {
    targetDate,
    clientPresentCount: totalClientCount,
    totalClientCount,
    isEstimated,
    staffingClass,
    ratioDenominator,
    requiredFte,
    requiredMinutes,
    actualStaffMinutes: totalStaffMinutes,
    actualFte,
    hasFullTimeStaff,
    isCompliant,
    shortageMinutes,
    complianceReason,

    // 内勤
    inFacilityClientCount,
    inFacilityStaffCount,
    inFacilityStaffMinutes,
    inFacilityRequiredMinutes,
    inFacilityRequiredFte,
    inFacilityActualFte,
    inFacilityShortageMinutes,
    hasInFacilityFullTimeStaff,
    isInFacilityCompliant,

    // 施設外就労
    offsiteClientCount,
    offsiteStaffCount,
    offsiteStaffMinutes,
    isOffsiteCompliant,
    offsiteWorkplaceDetails,
    unassignedOffsiteClients,
    offsiteAssignments,
  };
}

/**
 * 指定月の全日について、日毎の人員配置基準充足状況マップを取得する
 */
export function getMonthlyDailyStaffingMap(targetMonth: string): Record<string, DailyStaffingStatus> {
  const [year, month] = targetMonth.split('-').map(Number);
  const daysInMonth = new Date(year, month, 0).getDate();
  const map: Record<string, DailyStaffingStatus> = {};

  for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) {
    const dStr = `${targetMonth}-${String(d).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    map[dStr] = calculateDailyStaffingStatus(dStr);
  }

  return map;
}

export interface MonthlyStaffingStatus {
  targetMonth: string;
  totalClientPresent: number;
  openDays: number;
  averageClientsPerDay: number;
  staffingClass: string;
  ratioDenominator: number;
  requiredFte: number;
  actualMonthlyStaffHours: number;
  monthlyStandardHours: number;
  actualFte: number;
  hasFullTimeStaff: boolean; // 直接処遇の常勤職員が在籍・稼働しているか
  isCompliant: boolean;
  complianceReason?: string;

  // 内勤詳細
  inFacilityTotalClient: number;
  inFacilityAverageClientsPerDay: number;
  inFacilityRequiredFte: number;
  inFacilityStaffHours: number;
  inFacilityActualFte: number;
  hasInFacilityFullTimeStaff: boolean;
  isInFacilityCompliant: boolean;

  // 施設外就労詳細
  offsiteTotalClient: number;
  offsiteAverageClientsPerDay: number;
  offsiteStaffHours: number;
  offsiteActualFte: number;
  isOffsiteCompliant: boolean;

  // 管理者・サービス管理責任者（指定基準第189条）
  hasAdministrator: boolean;
  hasServiceManager: boolean;
  hasFullTimeServiceManager: boolean;
  requiredServiceManagerCount: number;
  actualServiceManagerCount: number;
  isServiceManagerCompliant: boolean;
}

/**
 * 実地指導対応: 指定月の月間平均常勤換算（FTE）と人員配置基準の充足状況を計算する。
 * ※厚労省令第189条: 月間常勤換算が基準以上 かつ 直接処遇職員に常勤1名以上が配置されていること。
 * ※内勤（本体）と施設外就労の分離統計も算出。
 */
export function calculateMonthlyStaffingStatus(targetMonth: string): MonthlyStaffingStatus {
  const facility = db.prepare(`SELECT staffing_class, regular_hours_per_week FROM facility_settings WHERE id = 'default'`).get() as any;
  const staffingClass = facility?.staffing_class || '10:1';
  const ratioDenominator = staffingClass === '6:1' ? 6 : staffingClass === '7.5:1' ? 7.5 : 10;
  const regularHoursPerWeek = facility?.regular_hours_per_week ? Number(facility.regular_hours_per_week) : 40;
  // 月間の常勤1名あたりの標準労働時間 (週所定 * 52週 / 12ヶ月)
  const monthlyStandardHours = Math.round((regularHoursPerWeek * 52) / 12);

  // 1. 月間の出勤実績と開所日数（全体・内勤・施設外を分離集計）
  const clientStats = db.prepare(`
    SELECT 
      COUNT(DISTINCT record_date) as open_days,
      COUNT(*) as total_present,
      COUNT(CASE WHEN work_type = 'out_facility' OR is_offsite = 1 THEN 1 END) as offsite_present,
      COUNT(CASE WHEN (work_type != 'out_facility' OR work_type IS NULL) AND (is_offsite = 0 OR is_offsite IS NULL) THEN 1 END) as infacility_present
    FROM attendance_records
    WHERE record_date LIKE ? AND status = 'present' AND deleted_at IS NULL
  `).get(`${targetMonth}%`) as any;

  const openDays = clientStats?.open_days || 0;
  const totalClientPresent = clientStats?.total_present || 0;
  const inFacilityTotalClient = clientStats?.infacility_present || 0;
  const offsiteTotalClient = clientStats?.offsite_present || 0;

  const averageClientsPerDay = openDays > 0 ? Math.round((totalClientPresent / openDays) * 10) / 10 : 0;
  const inFacilityAverageClientsPerDay = openDays > 0 ? Math.round((inFacilityTotalClient / openDays) * 10) / 10 : 0;
  const offsiteAverageClientsPerDay = openDays > 0 ? Math.round((offsiteTotalClient / openDays) * 10) / 10 : 0;

  // 実績から施設外引率職員のIDセットを取得
  const offsiteStaffRecords = db.prepare(`
    SELECT DISTINCT offsite_staff_id, record_date
    FROM attendance_records
    WHERE record_date LIKE ? AND status = 'present' AND (work_type = 'out_facility' OR is_offsite = 1) AND offsite_staff_id IS NOT NULL AND deleted_at IS NULL
  `).all(`${targetMonth}%`) as any[];
  const offsiteStaffDateKeySet = new Set(offsiteStaffRecords.map((r: any) => `${r.offsite_staff_id}_${r.record_date}`));

  // 2. 月間の直接処遇職員（職業指導員・生活支援員）の総勤務時間（シフト実績から集計）
  const shifts = db.prepare(`
    SELECT ss.*, s.employment_type, s.fte_ratio, s.is_concurrent
    FROM staff_shifts ss
    JOIN staff s ON ss.staff_id = s.id
    WHERE ss.target_date LIKE ? 
      AND (ss.is_holiday = 0 OR ss.is_holiday IS NULL)
      AND (ss.is_other_facility = 0 OR ss.is_other_facility IS NULL)
      AND (ss.holiday_type NOT IN ('他事業所', '他事業所勤務', 'GH', 'GH勤務', 'グループホーム') OR ss.holiday_type IS NULL)
      AND s.deleted_at IS NULL
      AND (
        s.job_category IN ('職業指導員', '生活支援員')
        OR s.role IN ('vocational_instructor', 'life_support_worker')
      )
      AND (
        s.job_category NOT IN (
          '管理者', 'サービス管理責任者', '管理者 兼 サービス管理責任者', 
          '賃金向上達成指導員', '視覚・聴覚言語障害者支援指導員', '重度者支援専門員', 
          '送迎ドライバー', '調理員', '事務員'
        )
        OR s.job_category IS NULL
      )
  `).all(`${targetMonth}%`) as any[];

  let actualStaffMinutes = 0;
  let inFacilityStaffMinutes = 0;
  let offsiteStaffMinutes = 0;
  let hasInFacilityFullTimeStaff = false;
  let hasFullTimeStaff = false;

  const staffTotalMinutesMap = new Map<string, number>();
  const staffInFacilityMinutesMap = new Map<string, number>();
  const staffOffsiteMinutesMap = new Map<string, number>();

  for (const s of shifts) {
    if (s.scheduled_in && s.scheduled_out) {
      const [sh, sm] = s.scheduled_in.split(':').map(Number);
      const [eh, em] = s.scheduled_out.split(':').map(Number);
      let inM = sh * 60 + sm;
      let outM = eh * 60 + em;
      if (outM < inM) outM += 24 * 60;
      let duration = outM - inM;
      // 労働基準法第34条: 6時間超45分、8時間超60分（6時間ちょうどは休憩不要）
      if (duration > 480) {
        duration -= 60;
      } else if (duration > 360) {
        duration -= 45;
      }
      if (s.is_concurrent && s.fte_ratio && s.fte_ratio < 1.0) {
        duration = Math.round(duration * s.fte_ratio);
      }

      if (duration > 0) {
        actualStaffMinutes += duration;
        staffTotalMinutesMap.set(s.staff_id, (staffTotalMinutesMap.get(s.staff_id) || 0) + duration);

        const isOffsite = (s.is_offsite === 1) || 
          offsiteStaffDateKeySet.has(`${s.staff_id}_${s.target_date}`) ||
          (s.holiday_type && ['施設外', '施設外就労', '引率'].includes(s.holiday_type));

        if (isOffsite) {
          offsiteStaffMinutes += duration;
          staffOffsiteMinutesMap.set(s.staff_id, (staffOffsiteMinutesMap.get(s.staff_id) || 0) + duration);
        } else {
          inFacilityStaffMinutes += duration;
          staffInFacilityMinutesMap.set(s.staff_id, (staffInFacilityMinutesMap.get(s.staff_id) || 0) + duration);
          if ((s.employment_type === '常勤' || s.employment_type === 'fulltime') && (!s.is_concurrent || (s.fte_ratio || 1.0) >= 1.0)) {
            hasInFacilityFullTimeStaff = true;
          }
        }

        if ((s.employment_type === '常勤' || s.employment_type === 'fulltime') && (!s.is_concurrent || (s.fte_ratio || 1.0) >= 1.0)) {
          hasFullTimeStaff = true;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // staff_shifts が未登録の場合、定休日設定と開所日数から月間勤務時間を推計
  if (shifts.length === 0 && openDays > 0) {
    const allDirectStaff = db.prepare(`
      SELECT id, name, employment_type, fte_ratio, is_concurrent, regular_holidays
      FROM staff
      WHERE deleted_at IS NULL
        AND (
          job_category IN ('職業指導員', '生活支援員')
          OR role IN ('vocational_instructor', 'life_support_worker')
        )
        AND (
          job_category NOT IN (
            '管理者', 'サービス管理責任者', '管理者 兼 サービス管理責任者', 
            '賃金向上達成指導員', '視覚・聴覚言語障害者支援指導員', '重度者支援専門員', 
            '送迎ドライバー', '調理員', '事務員'
          )
          OR job_category IS NULL
        )
    `).all() as any[];

    const [year, month] = targetMonth.split('-').map(Number);
    const daysInMonth = new Date(year, month, 0).getDate();
    const facilityHolidays = new Set(
      (db.prepare(`SELECT target_date FROM facility_holidays WHERE target_date LIKE ?`).all(`${targetMonth}%`) as any[]).map(r => r.target_date)
    );

    for (let day = 1; day <= daysInMonth; day++) {
      const dStr = `${targetMonth}-${String(day).padStart(2, '0')}`;
      if (facilityHolidays.has(dStr)) continue;
      const dObj = new Date(dStr);
      const dayOfWeek = dObj.getDay().toString();

      for (const s of allDirectStaff) {
        let isOff = false;
        try {
          const holidays = JSON.parse(s.regular_holidays || '[]');
          if (holidays.includes(dayOfWeek)) {
            isOff = true;
          }
        } catch(e) {}

        if (!isOff) {
          const dur = Math.round((regularHoursPerWeek / 5) * 60 * (s.fte_ratio || 1.0));
          actualStaffMinutes += dur;
          inFacilityStaffMinutes += dur;
          staffTotalMinutesMap.set(s.id, (staffTotalMinutesMap.get(s.id) || 0) + dur);
          staffInFacilityMinutesMap.set(s.id, (staffInFacilityMinutesMap.get(s.id) || 0) + dur);
          if ((s.employment_type === '常勤' || s.employment_type === 'fulltime') && (!s.is_concurrent || (s.fte_ratio || 1.0) >= 1.0)) {
            hasFullTimeStaff = true;
            hasInFacilityFullTimeStaff = true;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const actualMonthlyStaffHours = Math.round((actualStaffMinutes / 60) * 10) / 10;
  const inFacilityStaffHours = Math.round((inFacilityStaffMinutes / 60) * 10) / 10;
  const offsiteStaffHours = Math.round((offsiteStaffMinutes / 60) * 10) / 10;

  // 厚生労働省指定基準準拠: 常勤換算（FTE）は職員ごとに月間標準時間を上限（最大1.0）として積算
  let actualFte = 0;
  let inFacilityActualFte = 0;
  let offsiteActualFte = 0;

  if (monthlyStandardHours > 0) {
    for (const [_, mins] of staffTotalMinutesMap) {
      const individualFte = Math.min(1.0, (mins / 60) / monthlyStandardHours);
      actualFte += individualFte;
    }
    for (const [_, mins] of staffInFacilityMinutesMap) {
      const individualFte = Math.min(1.0, (mins / 60) / monthlyStandardHours);
      inFacilityActualFte += individualFte;
    }
    for (const [_, mins] of staffOffsiteMinutesMap) {
      const individualFte = Math.min(1.0, (mins / 60) / monthlyStandardHours);
      offsiteActualFte += individualFte;
    }
  }

  actualFte = Math.round(actualFte * 100) / 100;
  inFacilityActualFte = Math.round(inFacilityActualFte * 100) / 100;
  offsiteActualFte = Math.round(offsiteActualFte * 100) / 100;

  const requiredFte = Math.round((averageClientsPerDay / ratioDenominator) * 100) / 100;
  const inFacilityRequiredFte = Math.round((inFacilityAverageClientsPerDay / ratioDenominator) * 100) / 100;
  
  // サービス管理責任者および管理者の配置基準チェック（指定基準第189条）
  const serviceManagers = db.prepare(`
    SELECT id, name, employment_type, fte_ratio
    FROM staff
    WHERE deleted_at IS NULL
      AND (
        job_category IN ('サービス管理責任者', '管理者 兼 サービス管理責任者')
        OR role = 'service_manager'
      )
  `).all() as any[];

  const administrators = db.prepare(`
    SELECT id, name, employment_type
    FROM staff
    WHERE deleted_at IS NULL
      AND (
        job_category IN ('管理者', '管理者 兼 サービス管理責任者')
        OR role = 'admin'
      )
  `).all() as any[];

  const hasAdministrator = administrators.length > 0;
  const actualServiceManagerCount = serviceManagers.length;
  const hasServiceManager = actualServiceManagerCount > 0;
  const hasFullTimeServiceManager = serviceManagers.some(s => s.employment_type === '常勤' || (s.fte_ratio && s.fte_ratio >= 1.0));
  // 利用者60人につき1人以上
  const requiredServiceManagerCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(averageClientsPerDay / 60));
  const isServiceManagerCompliant = actualServiceManagerCount >= requiredServiceManagerCount && hasFullTimeServiceManager;

  // 内勤・施設外・全体の各適合フラグ
  const isFteCompliant = actualFte >= requiredFte;
  const isInFacilityCompliant = openDays === 0 || (inFacilityActualFte >= inFacilityRequiredFte && hasInFacilityFullTimeStaff);
  const isOffsiteCompliant = offsiteTotalClient === 0 || offsiteStaffHours > 0;
  const isCompliant = openDays === 0 || (isFteCompliant && hasFullTimeStaff && isInFacilityCompliant && isOffsiteCompliant && isServiceManagerCompliant && hasAdministrator);

  let complianceReason: string | undefined;
  if (!isCompliant) {
    const reasons: string[] = [];
    if (!hasAdministrator) {
      reasons.push('管理者が配置されていません（指定基準違反）');
    }
    if (!hasServiceManager) {
      reasons.push('サービス管理責任者が配置されていません（サビ管欠如減算30%対象）');
    } else if (!hasFullTimeServiceManager) {
      reasons.push('サービス管理責任者に常勤職員が配置されていません');
    } else if (actualServiceManagerCount < requiredServiceManagerCount) {
      reasons.push(`サービス管理責任者人数不足(必要${requiredServiceManagerCount}名に対し在籍${actualServiceManagerCount}名)`);
    }

    if (!isInFacilityCompliant) {
      if (!hasInFacilityFullTimeStaff) {
        reasons.push('内勤直接処遇の常勤職員が稼働していません');
      } else {
        reasons.push(`内勤常勤換算不足(実績${inFacilityActualFte} < 必要${inFacilityRequiredFte})`);
      }
    }
    if (!isOffsiteCompliant) {
      reasons.push('施設外就労の引率職員が不足しています');
    }
    if (!isFteCompliant) {
      reasons.push(`月間常勤換算不足(全体${actualFte} < 必要${requiredFte})`);
    }
    complianceReason = reasons.join(' / ') || '月間人員基準を満たしていません';
  }

  return {
    targetMonth,
    totalClientPresent,
    openDays,
    averageClientsPerDay,
    staffingClass,
    ratioDenominator,
    requiredFte,
    actualMonthlyStaffHours,
    monthlyStandardHours,
    actualFte,
    hasFullTimeStaff,
    isCompliant,
    complianceReason,

    // 内勤詳細
    inFacilityTotalClient,
    inFacilityAverageClientsPerDay,
    inFacilityRequiredFte,
    inFacilityStaffHours,
    inFacilityActualFte,
    hasInFacilityFullTimeStaff,
    isInFacilityCompliant,

    // 施設外就労詳細
    offsiteTotalClient,
    offsiteAverageClientsPerDay,
    offsiteStaffHours,
    offsiteActualFte,
    isOffsiteCompliant,

    // 管理者・サービス管理責任者
    hasAdministrator,
    hasServiceManager,
    hasFullTimeServiceManager,
    requiredServiceManagerCount,
    actualServiceManagerCount,
    isServiceManagerCompliant,
  };
}

export interface GlobalAlertItem {
  id: string;
  type: 'staffing' | 'plan' | 'recipient' | 'committee' | 'billing';
  severity: 'critical' | 'warning' | 'info';
  title: string;
  message: string;
  href: string;
}

/**
 * 全画面のヘッダーおよびダッシュボード共通のアラート要約を取得
 */
export function getGlobalAlertSummary(): GlobalAlertItem[] {
  const alerts: GlobalAlertItem[] = [];
  const today = new Date();
  const todayStr = `${today.getFullYear()}-${String(today.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(today.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  const currentMonth = todayStr.substring(0, 7);

  try {
    // 1. 本日の人員配置基準チェック
    const daily = calculateDailyStaffingStatus(todayStr);
    if (!daily.isCompliant && daily.clientPresentCount > 0) {
      let title = '本日の人員配置基準未充足';
      let message = daily.complianceReason || '本日の人員配置基準を満たしていません。';

      if (daily.shortageMinutes > 0) {
        title = '本日の職員勤務時間不足';
        message = `本日利用者${daily.clientPresentCount}名に対し、職員勤務時間が${Math.floor(daily.shortageMinutes / 60)}時間${daily.shortageMinutes % 60}分不足しています。`;
      } else if (!daily.isOffsiteCompliant) {
        title = '本日の施設外就労引率職員不足';
        message = `本日施設外就労の利用者(${daily.offsiteClientCount}名)に対し、専任引率職員が未指定・不足しています。出欠管理または職員シフトで引率職員を指定してください。`;
      } else if (!daily.hasInFacilityFullTimeStaff) {
        title = '本日の常勤職員配置未充足';
        message = '本体事業所に直接処遇の常勤職員が配置・稼働していません。';
      }

      alerts.push({
        id: 'staffing-daily',
        type: 'staffing',
        severity: 'critical',
        title,
        message,
        href: daily.isOffsiteCompliant ? '/staff/shifts' : '/attendance'
      });
    }

    // 2. 当月の月間常勤換算（FTE）チェック
    const monthly = calculateMonthlyStaffingStatus(currentMonth);
    if (!monthly.isCompliant) {
      let title = '当月の人員配置基準未充足';
      let message = monthly.complianceReason || '月間の人員配置基準を満たしていません。';

      if (monthly.actualFte < monthly.requiredFte) {
        title = '当月の常勤換算(FTE)不足';
        message = `当月の必要FTE ${monthly.requiredFte}人 に対し、実績は ${monthly.actualFte}人 です。人員欠如減算対象リスクがあります。`;
      } else if (!monthly.isOffsiteCompliant) {
        title = '当月の施設外就労引率職員未登録';
        message = `当月、施設外就労利用者(${monthly.offsiteTotalClient}名)に対し引率職員が未指定です（常勤換算FTEは${monthly.actualFte}人で充足）。出欠管理等で引率職員を指定してください。`;
      } else if (!monthly.hasFullTimeStaff) {
        title = '当月の直接処遇常勤職員不足';
        message = '直接処遇の常勤職員が稼働していません。人員基準（省令第189条）違反のリスクがあります。';
      }

      alerts.push({
        id: 'staffing-monthly',
        type: 'staffing',
        severity: 'critical',
        title,
        message,
        href: monthly.actualFte < monthly.requiredFte ? '/staff/shifts' : '/attendance'
      });
    }

    // 3. 個別支援計画の期限切れ・未作成チェック（30日以内）
    const thirtyDaysLater = new Date(today.getTime() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString().split('T')[0];
    const expiringPlans = db.prepare(`
      SELECT p.id, c.full_name, p.end_date
      FROM support_plans p
      JOIN clients c ON p.client_id = c.id
      WHERE p.status = 'active' AND p.end_date IS NOT NULL AND p.end_date <= ?
        AND c.deleted_at IS NULL AND (c.status IS NULL OR c.status = 'active')
      ORDER BY p.end_date ASC
    `).all(thirtyDaysLater) as any[];

    for (const p of expiringPlans) {
      const isOverdue = p.end_date < todayStr;
      alerts.push({
        id: `plan-${p.id}`,
        type: 'plan',
        severity: isOverdue ? 'critical' : 'warning',
        title: isOverdue ? `計画期限切れ: ${p.full_name}さん` : `計画期限間近: ${p.full_name}さん`,
        message: `${p.end_date} が終了日です。未作成減算（30%減算）防止のため計画更新が必要です。`,
        href: `/plans/${p.id}`
      });
    }

    // 4. 受給者証の有効期限チェック（30日以内）
    const expiringClients = db.prepare(`
      SELECT id, full_name, valid_to
      FROM clients
      WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND (status IS NULL OR status = 'active') AND valid_to IS NOT NULL AND valid_to <= ?
      ORDER BY valid_to ASC
    `).all(thirtyDaysLater) as any[];

    for (const c of expiringClients) {
      const isExpired = c.valid_to < todayStr;
      alerts.push({
        id: `recipient-${c.id}`,
        type: 'recipient',
        severity: isExpired ? 'critical' : 'warning',
        title: isExpired ? `受給者証失効: ${c.full_name}さん` : `受給者証更新間近: ${c.full_name}さん`,
        message: `${c.valid_to} に有効期限が切れます。更新手続きを確認してください。`,
        href: `/clients/${c.id}/edit`
      });
    }

    // 5. 賃金向上達成指導員配置加算の整合性チェック（令和6年度報酬改定により包括化廃止）
    const facilitySettings = db.prepare("SELECT wage_improvement_addition FROM facility_settings WHERE id = 'default'").get() as any;
    if (facilitySettings?.wage_improvement_addition === 1) {
      alerts.push({
        id: 'obsolete-wage-instructor-addition',
        type: 'billing',
        severity: 'warning',
        title: '【制度改正】賃金向上達成指導員配置加算の廃止',
        message: '賃金向上達成指導員配置加算は令和6年度報酬改定により基本報酬（スコア方式）へ包括化され廃止されました。加算の二重請求や返還を防ぐため、事業所設定の加算を解除してください。',
        href: '/settings'
      });
    }

    // 6. サービス管理責任者（サビ管）配置チェック（基準: 60名以下: 1名, 61〜120名: 2名, 以後60名ごとに+1名）
    const smList = db.prepare(`
      SELECT id, name FROM staff 
      WHERE deleted_at IS NULL 
        AND (
          job_category IN ('サービス管理責任者', '管理者 兼 サービス管理責任者')
          OR role IN ('service_manager', 'admin_and_service_manager')
        )
    `).all() as any[];

    const activeClientCount = (db.prepare(`
      SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM clients WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND (status IS NULL OR status = 'active')
    `).get() as any)?.cnt || 0;

    const requiredSmCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(activeClientCount / 60));

    if (smList.length === 0) {
      alerts.push({
        id: 'missing-service-manager',
        type: 'staffing',
        severity: 'critical',
        title: 'サービス管理責任者が未配置',
        message: '指定基準上必須の「サービス管理責任者」が職員マスタに登録されていません。速やかに配置・登録を行ってください。',
        href: '/staff'
      });
    } else if (smList.length < requiredSmCount) {
      alerts.push({
        id: 'insufficient-service-manager',
        type: 'staffing',
        severity: 'warning',
        title: 'サービス管理責任者の配置不足',
        message: `現在の利用者数（${activeClientCount}名）に対して必要なサービス管理責任者は${requiredSmCount}名ですが、現在${smList.length}名しか配置されていません（指定基準: 60名ごとに1名配置要件）。`,
        href: '/staff'
      });
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("Failed to generate global alert summary:", e);
  }

  return alerts;
}

================================================================================
File: src/lib/constants/areaUnitCostMaster.ts
================================================================================

import { getBillingRules } from '@/lib/db';

/**
 * 障害福祉サービス（就労系等）における地域区分別1単位の単価マスタ（令和6年4月報酬改定対応）
 * 
 * 級地に応じた1単位あたりの単価（円）
 */
export const AREA_UNIT_COSTS: Record<string, number> = {
  '1級地': 11.20,
  '2級地': 11.12,
  '3級地': 10.84,
  '4級地': 10.70,
  '5級地': 10.42,
  '6級地': 10.21,
  '7級地': 10.14,
  'その他': 10.00,
};

/**
 * 事業所設定の地域区分名から1単位あたりの単価を取得する（DB動的取得）
 * @param regionClass 例: '2級地', '1級地', 'その他' など
 * @returns 1単位あたりの単価（円）
 */
export function getAreaUnitCost(regionClass?: string | null): number {
  const rules = getBillingRules();

  const rankKeyMap: Record<string, string> = {
    '1級地': 'area_unit_cost_1',
    '2級地': 'area_unit_cost_2',
    '3級地': 'area_unit_cost_3',
    '4級地': 'area_unit_cost_4',
    '5級地': 'area_unit_cost_5',
    '6級地': 'area_unit_cost_6',
    '7級地': 'area_unit_cost_7',
    'その他': 'area_unit_cost_other',
  };

  if (regionClass) {
    for (const [rank, ruleKey] of Object.entries(rankKeyMap)) {
      if (regionClass.includes(rank)) {
        if (rules[ruleKey] && rules[ruleKey] > 0) {
          return rules[ruleKey];
        }
        return AREA_UNIT_COSTS[rank] ?? 10.00;
      }
    }
  }

  // 未設定時のフォールバック（DBに2級地単価があればそれを採用、なければ11.12円）
  return rules['area_unit_cost_2'] ?? 11.12;
}



================================================================================
File: src/lib/constants/minimumWageMaster.ts
================================================================================

import { getRegionalMinimumWage, DEFAULT_REGIONAL_MINIMUM_WAGES } from '@/lib/db';

/**
 * 厚生労働省公表 地域別最低賃金マスター（DB動的取得）
 * 静的固定値ではなく、常にデータベースの regional_minimum_wages テーブルから最新値を取得
 */
export function getApplicableMinimumWage(prefCode?: string | null, customWage?: number | null): number {
  if (customWage && customWage > 0) {
    return customWage;
  }
  
  if (prefCode) {
    const dbWage = getRegionalMinimumWage(prefCode);
    if (dbWage && dbWage.wage > 0) {
      return dbWage.wage;
    }
  }

  // フォールバック: 最新デフォルトマスター
  if (prefCode) {
    const padded = prefCode.padStart(2, '0');
    const fallback = DEFAULT_REGIONAL_MINIMUM_WAGES.find(w => w.pref_code === padded);
    if (fallback) return fallback.minimum_wage;
  }

  // 最終フォールバック: デフォルトマスター中の全国最低賃金下限
  const nationalFloor = Math.min(...DEFAULT_REGIONAL_MINIMUM_WAGES.map(w => w.minimum_wage));
  return nationalFloor > 0 ? nationalFloor : 1073;
}

================================================================================
File: src/lib/db-client.ts
================================================================================

import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import path from 'path';
import fs from 'fs';

export type ServiceDbKey = 'type_a' | 'type_b' | 'gh' | 'houday' | 'corporate';

const dbPathMap: Record<ServiceDbKey, string> = {
  type_a: path.join(process.cwd(), 'local_data.sqlite'),
  type_b: path.join(process.cwd(), 'local_data_b.sqlite'),
  gh: path.join(process.cwd(), 'local_data_gh.sqlite'),
  houday: path.join(process.cwd(), 'local_data_houday.sqlite'),
  corporate: path.join(process.cwd(), 'local_data_corporate.sqlite'),
};

declare global {
  var __db_connections: Map<string, Database.Database> | undefined;
}

if (!global.__db_connections) {
  global.__db_connections = new Map<string, Database.Database>();
}

/**
 * 低レイヤーDBコネクション取得ヘルパー
 * 指定されたサービスキー（または任意のDBファイルパス）に対して
 * WALモードと busy_timeout = 5000ms を設定した Database インスタンスを返します。
 */
export function getDbConnection(target: ServiceDbKey | string): Database.Database {
  const filePath = (target in dbPathMap) ? dbPathMap[target as ServiceDbKey] : target;

  let conn = global.__db_connections!.get(filePath);
  if (!conn) {
    const dir = path.dirname(filePath);
    if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    }

    conn = new Database(filePath, { timeout: 5000 });
    conn.pragma('journal_mode = WAL');
    conn.pragma('busy_timeout = 5000');
    conn.pragma('foreign_keys = ON');

    global.__db_connections!.set(filePath, conn);
  }

  return conn;
}

/**
 * 指定されたサービスのDBファイルパスを取得
 */
export function getServiceDbPath(key: ServiceDbKey): string {
  return dbPathMap[key];
}


================================================================================
File: src/lib/db.ts
================================================================================

import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import path from 'path';
import fs from 'fs';
import { AsyncLocalStorage } from 'async_hooks';
import type { ServiceMode } from './serviceMode';
import { ensureKnowledgeDocumentsSeeded } from './compliance/knowledgeSeeds';

export const SERVICE_MODE_COOKIE = 'service_mode';

// 明示的なサービスモード実行コンテキスト
export const serviceModeContext = new AsyncLocalStorage<ServiceMode>();

// Next.js 内部の workUnitAsyncStorage
let workUnitAsyncStorage: any;
try {
  // @ts-ignore
  const nextWorkUnit = require('next/dist/server/app-render/work-unit-async-storage.external');
  workUnitAsyncStorage = nextWorkUnit?.workUnitAsyncStorage;
} catch (e) {
  // ignore
}

/**
 * 現在のリクエストスコープ（workUnitAsyncStorage）でオーバーライドされたサービスモードを取得する
 */
export function getRequestServiceModeOverride(): ServiceMode | undefined {
  try {
    if (workUnitAsyncStorage) {
      const store = workUnitAsyncStorage.getStore();
      if (store && (store.__overrideServiceMode === 'type_a' || store.__overrideServiceMode === 'type_b')) {
        return store.__overrideServiceMode;
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return undefined;
}

/**
 * 現在のリクエストスコープ（workUnitAsyncStorage）に対してサービスモードを明示上書きする
 * （複数タブ間でA型/B型を切り替えた状態で別タブから保存操作が行われた際のDB不整合・外部キー違反を防止）
 */
export function setRequestServiceModeOverride(mode: ServiceMode): void {
  try {
    if (workUnitAsyncStorage) {
      const store = workUnitAsyncStorage.getStore();
      if (store && typeof store === 'object') {
        store.__overrideServiceMode = mode;
        if (store.mutableCookies && typeof store.mutableCookies.set === 'function') {
          try {
            store.mutableCookies.set(SERVICE_MODE_COOKIE, mode, {
              path: '/',
              maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 365,
              sameSite: 'lax',
            });
          } catch {}
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}

/**
 * リクエストコンテキストまたはストレージから同期的にサービスモードを取得する
 * サーバーアクションや Server Component 内の同期呼び出しでも透過的に動作
 */
export function getServiceModeSync(): ServiceMode {
  // 1. レコードID逆引き等で確定したリクエスト内オーバーライドを最優先
  const override = getRequestServiceModeOverride();
  if (override) return override;

  // 2. withServiceModeFromRequest 等による明示コンテキスト
  const explicit = serviceModeContext.getStore();
  if (explicit) return explicit;

  // 3. Next.js リクエストの Cookie
  try {
    if (workUnitAsyncStorage) {
      const store = workUnitAsyncStorage.getStore();
      if (store && store.cookies) {
        const val = typeof store.cookies.get === 'function' ? store.cookies.get(SERVICE_MODE_COOKIE)?.value : undefined;
        if (val === 'type_b' || val === 'type_a' || val === 'gh' || val === 'houday' || val === 'corporate') {
          return val as ServiceMode;
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  return 'type_a';
}

/**
 * RequestオブジェクトまたはCookiesホルダーから明示的にサービスモードを判定
 */
export function getServiceModeFromRequest(req: any): ServiceMode {
  try {
    if (req) {
      // NextRequest.cookies
      if (req.cookies && typeof req.cookies.get === 'function') {
        const val = req.cookies.get(SERVICE_MODE_COOKIE)?.value;
        if (val === 'type_b' || val === 'type_a' || val === 'gh' || val === 'houday' || val === 'corporate') return val as ServiceMode;
      }
      // headers.get('cookie')
      if (req.headers && typeof req.headers.get === 'function') {
        const cookieHeader = req.headers.get('cookie') || '';
        const match = cookieHeader.match(new RegExp(`(?:^|;\\s*)${SERVICE_MODE_COOKIE}=([^;]+)`));
        if (match) {
          const val = decodeURIComponent(match[1]);
          if (val === 'type_b' || val === 'type_a' || val === 'gh' || val === 'houday' || val === 'corporate') return val as ServiceMode;
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return 'type_a';
}

/**
 * API Route Handler 用: リクエストのCookieからサービスモードを解決し、
 * コンテキストを分離した状態でハンドラ関数を実行する
 */
export async function withServiceModeFromRequest<T>(
  req: any,
  fn: () => Promise<T>
): Promise<T> {
  const mode = getServiceModeFromRequest(req);
  return serviceModeContext.run(mode, fn);
}

declare global {
  var __db_a: Database.Database | undefined;
  var __db_b: Database.Database | undefined;
}

const dbPathA = path.join(process.cwd(), 'local_data.sqlite');
const dbPathB = path.join(process.cwd(), 'local_data_b.sqlite');

/**
 * 現在アクティブなDBファイルの絶対パスを取得
 */
export function getActiveDbPath(mode: ServiceMode = getServiceModeSync()): string {
  return mode === 'type_b' ? dbPathB : dbPathA;
}

/**
 * 指定モード（または現在の同期コンテキスト）の Database インスタンスを取得
 */
export function getDatabase(mode: ServiceMode = getServiceModeSync()): Database.Database {
  if (mode === 'type_b') {
    if (!global.__db_b) {
      const isNew = !fs.existsSync(dbPathB);
      const dbInstance = new Database(dbPathB, { timeout: 5000 });
      dbInstance.pragma('journal_mode = WAL');
      dbInstance.pragma('busy_timeout = 5000');
      dbInstance.pragma('foreign_keys = ON');
      initDatabase(dbInstance, 'type_b', isNew);
      global.__db_b = dbInstance;
    }
    return global.__db_b;
  } else if (mode === 'gh') {
    const { getGhDb } = require('@/app/(gh)/gh/lib/db');
    return getGhDb();
  } else if (mode === 'houday') {
    const { getHoudayDb } = require('@/app/(houday)/houday/lib/db');
    return getHoudayDb();
  } else {
    if (!global.__db_a) {
      const isNew = !fs.existsSync(dbPathA);
      const dbInstance = new Database(dbPathA, { timeout: 5000 });
      dbInstance.pragma('journal_mode = WAL');
      dbInstance.pragma('busy_timeout = 5000');
      dbInstance.pragma('foreign_keys = ON');
      initDatabase(dbInstance, 'type_a', isNew);
      global.__db_a = dbInstance;
    }
    return global.__db_a;
  }
}

export type ModeResolvableTable =
  | 'clients'
  | 'support_plans'
  | 'contractor_agreements'
  | 'staff'
  | 'departments'
  | 'interview_records'
  | 'offsite_evaluations'
  | 'assessments'
  | 'service_conferences'
  | 'plan_monitorings'
  | 'monitorings'
  | 'attendance_records'
  | 'client_pto_records'
  | 'staff_pto_records'
  | 'staff_shifts'
  | 'offsite_workplaces'
  | 'committee_records'
  | 'incident_reports'
  | 'labor_agreements'
  | 'client_documents'
  | 'support_records'
  | 'daily_logs'
  | 'complaint_records'
  | 'restraint_records'
  | 'telework_support_records'
  | 'training_records'
  | 'self_plans'
  | 'knowledge_documents';

const ALLOWED_RESOLVE_TABLES: ReadonlySet<ModeResolvableTable> = new Set([
  'clients',
  'support_plans',
  'contractor_agreements',
  'staff',
  'departments',
  'interview_records',
  'offsite_evaluations',
  'assessments',
  'service_conferences',
  'plan_monitorings',
  'monitorings',
  'attendance_records',
  'client_pto_records',
  'staff_pto_records',
  'staff_shifts',
  'offsite_workplaces',
  'committee_records',
  'incident_reports',
  'labor_agreements',
  'client_documents',
  'support_records',
  'daily_logs',
  'complaint_records',
  'restraint_records',
  'telework_support_records',
  'training_records',
  'self_plans',
  'knowledge_documents',
]);

const ALLOWED_ID_COLUMNS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(['id', 'client_id', 'staff_id', 'support_plan_id']);

/**
 * 汎用クロスモード解決ヘルパー:
 * 指定されたテーブルとレコードIDが現在のモードのDBに存在するか確認し、
 * 存在しない場合はもう一方のモード（Type A ⇔ Type B）のDBを検索して、
 * 発見された側のモードへリクエストコンテキストおよびCookieを自動同期する
 */
export function resolveServiceModeForTableRecord(
  tableName: ModeResolvableTable,
  recordId: string | null | undefined,
  idColumn: 'id' | 'client_id' | 'staff_id' | 'support_plan_id' = 'id'
): ServiceMode {
  const currentMode = getServiceModeSync();
  if (!recordId || recordId === 'new') return currentMode;
  if (!ALLOWED_RESOLVE_TABLES.has(tableName) || !ALLOWED_ID_COLUMNS.has(idColumn)) {
    return currentMode;
  }
  try {
    const sql = `SELECT ${idColumn} FROM ${tableName} WHERE ${idColumn} = ? LIMIT 1`;
    const currentDb = getDatabase(currentMode);
    const existsInCurrent = currentDb.prepare(sql).get(recordId);
    if (existsInCurrent) {
      return currentMode;
    }
    const otherMode: ServiceMode = currentMode === 'type_a' ? 'type_b' : 'type_a';
    const otherDb = getDatabase(otherMode);
    const existsInOther = otherDb.prepare(sql).get(recordId);
    if (existsInOther) {
      setRequestServiceModeOverride(otherMode);
      return otherMode;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return currentMode;
}

export function resolveServiceModeForClient(clientId: string): ServiceMode {
  return resolveServiceModeForTableRecord('clients', clientId, 'id');
}

export function resolveServiceModeForPlan(planId: string): ServiceMode {
  return resolveServiceModeForTableRecord('support_plans', planId, 'id');
}

export function resolveServiceModeForContractor(contractorId: string): ServiceMode {
  return resolveServiceModeForTableRecord('contractor_agreements', contractorId, 'id');
}

export function resolveServiceModeForStaff(staffId: string): ServiceMode {
  return resolveServiceModeForTableRecord('staff', staffId, 'id');
}

export function resolveServiceModeForDepartment(departmentId: string): ServiceMode {
  return resolveServiceModeForTableRecord('departments', departmentId, 'id');
}

export function resolveServiceModeForInterview(interviewId: string): ServiceMode {
  return resolveServiceModeForTableRecord('interview_records', interviewId, 'id');
}

export function resolveServiceModeForOffsiteEval(evalId: string): ServiceMode {
  return resolveServiceModeForTableRecord('offsite_evaluations', evalId, 'id');
}

export function resolveServiceModeForAssessment(assessmentId: string): ServiceMode {
  return resolveServiceModeForTableRecord('assessments', assessmentId, 'id');
}

export function resolveServiceModeForConference(conferenceId: string): ServiceMode {
  return resolveServiceModeForTableRecord('service_conferences', conferenceId, 'id');
}

export function resolveServiceModeForPlanMonitoring(monitoringId: string): ServiceMode {
  return resolveServiceModeForTableRecord('plan_monitorings', monitoringId, 'id');
}

export function resolveServiceModeForMonitoring(monitoringId: string): ServiceMode {
  return resolveServiceModeForTableRecord('monitorings', monitoringId, 'id');
}

export function resolveServiceModeForAttendance(attendanceId: string): ServiceMode {
  return resolveServiceModeForTableRecord('attendance_records', attendanceId, 'id');
}

/**
 * 外部キー（REFERENCES）カラムへ保存するIDの安全なサニタイズ＆存在確認ヘルパー:
 * - 空文字 "" や空白のみの文字列を null に正規化する
 * - 参照先テーブルに該当IDが存在しない場合は null を返す
 * - offsite_workplaces に contractor_agreements のIDが渡された場合は offsite_workplaces に自動同期して外部キーエラーを防ぐ
 */
export function sanitizeForeignKeyId(
  targetTable: 'staff' | 'offsite_workplaces' | 'contractor_agreements' | 'support_plans' | 'departments' | 'clients',
  rawId: string | null | undefined
): string | null {
  const trimmed = typeof rawId === 'string' ? rawId.trim() : '';
  if (!trimmed || trimmed === 'new') return null;

  try {
    const currentDb = getDatabase();
    const exists = currentDb.prepare(`SELECT id FROM ${targetTable} WHERE id = ? LIMIT 1`).get(trimmed);
    if (exists) return trimmed;

    if (targetTable === 'offsite_workplaces') {
      const contractor = currentDb
        .prepare('SELECT id, company_name FROM contractor_agreements WHERE id = ? LIMIT 1')
        .get(trimmed) as any;
      if (contractor) {
        currentDb
          .prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO offsite_workplaces (id, name, company_name) VALUES (?, ?, ?)')
          .run(contractor.id, contractor.company_name, contractor.company_name);
        return trimmed;
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  return null;
}

  { rule_key: 'deduction_capacity', category: 'deduction', rule_name: '定員超過利用減算', rule_value: 0.30, unit_label: '率', description: '延べ利用者が定員125%超過等の場合 30%減算', effective_from: '2024-04-01' },
  { rule_key: 'deduction_staffing', category: 'deduction', rule_name: '人員基準欠如減算', rule_value: 0.30, unit_label: '率', description: '月間常勤換算配置基準不足等の場合 30%減算', effective_from: '2024-04-01' }
];

export function getBillingRules(): Record<string, number> {
  try {
    const rows = db.prepare('SELECT rule_key, rule_value FROM billing_rules').all() as any[];
    const map: Record<string, number> = {};
    for (const r of rows) {
      map[r.rule_key] = Number(r.rule_value);
    }
    return map;
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to load billing_rules, using defaults:', e);
    const map: Record<string, number> = {};
    for (const r of DEFAULT_BILLING_RULES) {
      map[r.rule_key] = r.rule_value;
    }
    return map;
  }
}

/**
 * 都道府県コードまたは都道府県名から最新の最低賃金データを取得（DB駆動）
 */
export function getRegionalMinimumWage(prefCodeOrName?: string | null): { wage: number; effectiveDate: string; prefName: string; prefCode: string } | null {
  if (!prefCodeOrName) return null;
  try {
    const trimmed = prefCodeOrName.trim();
    const isCode = /^\d{1,2}$/.test(trimmed);
    let row: any;
    if (isCode) {
      const code = trimmed.padStart(2, '0');
      row = db.prepare('SELECT pref_code, pref_name, minimum_wage, effective_date FROM regional_minimum_wages WHERE pref_code = ?').get(code);
    } else {
      const cleanName = trimmed.replace(/(都|府|県)$/, '');
      row = db.prepare('SELECT pref_code, pref_name, minimum_wage, effective_date FROM regional_minimum_wages WHERE pref_name LIKE ?').get(`%${cleanName}%`);
    }
    if (row) {
      return {
        wage: Number(row.minimum_wage),
        effectiveDate: row.effective_date,
        prefName: row.pref_name,
        prefCode: row.pref_code
      };
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to get regional minimum wage from DB:', e);
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * 就労継続支援A型用の初期シードデータ（未登録時のフェイルセーフ）
 */
function seedTypeAInitialData(targetDb: Database.Database) {
  try {
    const existing = targetDb.prepare('SELECT id FROM facility_settings LIMIT 1').get();
    if (!existing) {
      targetDb.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO facility_settings (
          id, facility_number, corporation_name, facility_name,
          postal_code, address, phone_number, pref_code, city_code,
          capacity_class, staffing_class, region_class, treatment_addition_class,
          active_ai_model,
          has_bcp, has_abuse_prevention, has_restraint_reduction, has_information_disclosure,
          business_start_time, business_end_time,
          service_start_time, service_end_time, regular_hours_per_week,
          regional_minimum_wage,
          use_variable_working_hours
        ) VALUES (
          'default', '2810700001', '特定非営利活動法人須磨福祉会', '就労継続支援A型事業所 須磨ワークス',
          '654-0011', '兵庫県神戸市須磨区前池町1-1-1', '078-123-4567', '28', '281076',
          '20人以下', '7.5:1', '5級地', 'treatment_addition_1',
          'google:gemini-3.8-flash',
          1, 1, 1, 1,
          '09:00', '18:00',
          '09:00', '14:00', 40,
          1173,
          1
        )
      `).run();
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Error seeding A facility_settings:', e);
  }

  try {
    const deptCount = (targetDb.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM departments WHERE deleted_at IS NULL').get() as any)?.count || 0;
    if (deptCount === 0) {
      targetDb.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO departments (id, name, code, description, sort_order, capacity, facility_type)
        VALUES ('dept-a-main', 'A型作業部（本部）', 'MAIN', 'PC入力・軽作業・受託作業', 1, 20, 'main')
      `).run();
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Error seeding A departments:', e);
  }

  try {
    const staffCount = (targetDb.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM staff').get() as any)?.count || 0;
    if (staffCount === 0) {
      const mainDept = (targetDb.prepare('SELECT id FROM departments LIMIT 1').get() as any)?.id || 'dept-a-main';
      targetDb.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO staff (id, name, role, employment_type, job_category, department_id, fte_ratio)
        VALUES 
          ('staff-a-admin', '管理者（A型）', 'admin', 'fulltime', 'manager', ?, 1.0),
          ('staff-a-sm', 'サビ管（A型）', 'service_manager', 'fulltime', 'service_manager', ?, 1.0),
          ('staff-a-voc1', '職業指導員（常勤）', 'staff', 'fulltime', 'vocational_advisor', ?, 1.0),
          ('staff-a-life1', '生活支援員（常勤）', 'staff', 'fulltime', 'life_support', ?, 1.0)
      `).run(mainDept, mainDept, mainDept, mainDept);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Error seeding A staff:', e);
  }
}

/**
 * 就労継続支援B型用の初期シードデータ（令和6年度改定・自治体ガイドライン準拠）
 */
function seedTypeBInitialData(targetDb: Database.Database) {
  try {
    const existing = targetDb.prepare('SELECT id FROM facility_settings LIMIT 1').get();
    if (!existing) {
      targetDb.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO facility_settings (
          id, facility_number, corporation_name, facility_name,
          postal_code, address, phone_number, pref_code, city_code,
          capacity_class, staffing_class, region_class, treatment_addition_class,
          active_ai_model,
          has_bcp, has_abuse_prevention, has_restraint_reduction, has_information_disclosure,
          business_start_time, business_end_time,
          service_start_time, service_end_time, regular_hours_per_week,
          regional_minimum_wage,
          use_variable_working_hours,
          overtime_rate, overtime_over60_rate, night_rate, holiday_rate, employment_insurance_rate,
          target_wage_monthly_target, target_wage_instructor_addition, target_wage_achievement_addition, community_cooperation_addition
        ) VALUES (
          'default', '2810700002', '特定非営利活動法人須磨福祉会', '就労継続支援B型事業所 須磨オアシス',
          '654-0000', '兵庫県神戸市須磨区須磨浦通1-1', '078-731-0002', '28', '281076',
          '20人以下', '6:1', '5級地', 'treatment_addition_1',
          'google:gemini-3.8-flash',
          1, 1, 1, 1,
          '09:00', '18:00',
          '10:00', '15:30', 40,
          0,
          0,
          0, 0, 0, 0, 0,
          20000, 1, 0, 0
        )
      `).run();
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Error seeding B facility_settings:', e);
  }

  try {
    const deptCount = (targetDb.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM departments WHERE deleted_at IS NULL').get() as any)?.count || 0;
    if (deptCount === 0) {
      targetDb.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO departments (id, name, code, description, sort_order, capacity, facility_type)
        VALUES ('dept-b-main', 'B型作業工房（本部）', 'MAIN', '菓子製造・軽作業・受託作業', 1, 20, 'main')

/**
 * データベースの初期化と全テーブル・インデックス作成
 */
function initDatabase(db: Database.Database, mode: ServiceMode = 'type_a', isNew: boolean = false) {
  // 1. 全52テーブル作成
  db.exec(`
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS absence_logs (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    client_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    absence_date TEXT NOT NULL,
    contact_person TEXT,
    response_details TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
  );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS assessments (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    client_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    support_plan_id TEXT REFERENCES support_plans(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    assessment_date TEXT NOT NULL,
    assessor_name TEXT,
    living_status TEXT,
    user_wishes TEXT,
    family_wishes TEXT,
    strengths TEXT,
    challenges TEXT,
    notes TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
  , client_hopes TEXT, health_and_life TEXT, working_capabilities TEXT, communication_skills TEXT, current_challenges TEXT, support_policy_instructions TEXT, support_policy_consultation TEXT, support_policy_environment TEXT, operational_notes TEXT, consent_date TEXT, deleted_at TEXT DEFAULT NULL);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS attendance_records (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    client_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    record_date TEXT NOT NULL,
    status TEXT DEFAULT 'present',
    clock_in TEXT,
    clock_out TEXT,
    break_time_minutes INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    work_type TEXT,
    is_offsite INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    is_telework INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    is_absence_addition INTEGER DEFAULT 0, -- boolean 0 or 1
    absence_reason TEXT,
        contact_time TEXT,
        contact_method TEXT,
        contact_person TEXT,
        handled_by TEXT,
        absence_condition TEXT,
        consultation_details TEXT,
        next_scheduled_date TEXT,
    meal_provided INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    meal_intake TEXT,
    transportation_used INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    pickup_driver TEXT,
    dropoff_driver TEXT,
    visit_support_used INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    visit_location TEXT,
    visit_staff_name TEXT,
    visit_start_time TEXT,
    visit_end_time TEXT,
    visit_content TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    deleted_at TEXT DEFAULT NULL, pickup_used INTEGER DEFAULT 0, dropoff_used INTEGER DEFAULT 0, offsite_workplace_id TEXT REFERENCES offsite_workplaces(id), offsite_staff_id TEXT REFERENCES staff(id), offsite_start_time TEXT, offsite_end_time TEXT, in_facility_start_time TEXT, in_facility_end_time TEXT, pickup_service INTEGER DEFAULT 0, dropoff_service INTEGER DEFAULT 0, transportation_service_used INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    FOREIGN KEY (client_id) REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    UNIQUE(client_id, record_date)
  );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit_logs (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id TEXT,
    user_name TEXT,
    action TEXT NOT NULL,
    target_table TEXT NOT NULL,
    target_id TEXT,
    details TEXT,
    ip_address TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
  );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS backup_logs (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    backup_type TEXT NOT NULL,          -- 'manual_download' | 'manual_cloud' | 'auto_cloud' | 'restore'
    destination TEXT,                   -- 'browser' | 'google_drive' | 's3' | 'local_restore'
    file_name TEXT,
    file_size_bytes INTEGER,
    status TEXT NOT NULL,               -- 'success' | 'failed'
    message TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
  );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS backup_settings (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    cloud_provider TEXT DEFAULT 'none', -- 'none' | 'google_drive_folder' | 's3'
    local_sync_path TEXT,               -- 例: G:\マイドライブ\福祉バックアップ
    s3_endpoint TEXT,                   -- 例: https://<account>.r2.cloudflarestorage.com
    s3_bucket TEXT,                     -- 例: welfare-backup-bucket
    s3_region TEXT DEFAULT 'auto',
    s3_access_key_id TEXT,
    s3_secret_access_key TEXT,
    auto_sync_enabled INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    auto_sync_interval_hours INTEGER DEFAULT 24,
    default_passphrase_hash TEXT,
    last_sync_at TEXT,
    last_sync_status TEXT,              -- 'success' | 'failed'
    last_sync_message TEXT,
    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
  );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS billing_monthly_locks (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    target_month TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    is_locked INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    locked_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    locked_by TEXT,
    unlock_reason TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
  );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS billing_rules (
      rule_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      category TEXT NOT NULL,
      rule_name TEXT NOT NULL,
      rule_value REAL NOT NULL,
      unit_label TEXT DEFAULT '単位',
      description TEXT,
      effective_from TEXT,
      updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
  );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS client_documents (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    client_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    uploaded_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (client_id) REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
  );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS client_facesheets (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    client_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES clients(id),
    diagnosis TEXT,
    medication TEXT,
    allergy TEXT,
    life_history TEXT,
    family_structure TEXT,
    hobbies_interests TEXT,
    communication_style TEXT,
    notes TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
  , medical_history TEXT, disability_details TEXT, emergency_contact TEXT);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS client_pto_records (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    client_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    grant_date TEXT NOT NULL,
    granted_days REAL NOT NULL,
    used_days REAL DEFAULT 0,
    remaining_days REAL NOT NULL,
    expiration_date TEXT NOT NULL,
    note TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, expired_days REAL DEFAULT 0,
    FOREIGN KEY (client_id) REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
  );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS client_shifts (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    client_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    target_date TEXT NOT NULL,
    scheduled_in TEXT,
    scheduled_out TEXT,
    is_holiday INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    holiday_type TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, work_type TEXT DEFAULT 'in_facility', is_offsite INTEGER DEFAULT 0, offsite_workplace_id TEXT, offsite_workplace_name TEXT, offsite_start_time TEXT, offsite_end_time TEXT, in_facility_start_time TEXT, in_facility_end_time TEXT, deleted_at TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
    FOREIGN KEY (client_id) REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    UNIQUE(client_id, target_date)
  );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clients (

      client_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
      record_date TEXT NOT NULL,

      end_time TEXT,
      urgency_reason TEXT NOT NULL,          -- 切迫性: 本人または他者の生命・身体の危険性

      temporariness_reason TEXT NOT NULL,    -- 一時性: 一時的な制限である理由と解除条件
      restraint_type TEXT NOT NULL,          -- 拘束の態様・方法
      client_condition TEXT,                 -- 拘束中の心身状況・観察記録
      review_process TEXT,                   -- 解除に向けた検討経過
      family_notified INTEGER DEFAULT 0,     -- 本人・家族への説明・同意状況 (0:未, 1:済)
      family_consent_date TEXT,              -- 説明・同意日
      staff_name TEXT NOT NULL,              -- 記録者・実施者
      manager_name TEXT,                     -- 管理者承認
      created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
      updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
  , deleted_at TEXT DEFAULT NULL, service_conference_id TEXT, committee_record_id TEXT);

  );

    FOREIGN KEY (staff_id) REFERENCES staff(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,

    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,

  );

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS telework_support_records (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    client_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    record_date TEXT NOT NULL,
    morning_contact_time TEXT,

    morning_health_condition TEXT,
    morning_task_instructions TEXT,

    evening_work_progress TEXT,

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS training_records (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      training_type TEXT NOT NULL,
      training_date TEXT NOT NULL,
      title TEXT NOT NULL,
      instructor TEXT,
