      materials TEXT,
      next_scheduled_date TEXT,
      created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
      updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
  , deleted_at TEXT DEFAULT NULL);
  `);

  // 2. 全インデックス作成
  db.exec(`
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_assessments_client_id ON assessments(client_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_attendance_client_date ON attendance_records(client_id, record_date);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_attendance_client_id ON attendance_records(client_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_attendance_record_date ON attendance_records(record_date);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_logs_created_at ON audit_logs(created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_logs_target ON audit_logs(target_table, target_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_burden_limits_client_month ON monthly_burden_limits(client_id, target_month);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_client_pto_expiration ON client_pto_records(expiration_date);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_client_shifts_client_id ON client_shifts(client_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_client_shifts_target_date ON client_shifts(target_date);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clients_deleted_at ON clients(deleted_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_conferences_client_id ON service_conferences(client_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_departments_sort ON departments(sort_order, created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_jurisdictions_code ON jurisdictions(jurisdiction_code);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_knowledge_docs_category ON knowledge_documents(category, deleted_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_monitorings_client_id ON plan_monitorings(client_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_offsite_eval_client ON offsite_evaluations(client_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_offsite_eval_date ON offsite_evaluations(evaluation_date);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payslips_client_month ON monthly_payslips(client_id, target_month);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_staff_pto_expiration ON staff_pto_records(expiration_date);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_staff_pto_staff_id ON staff_pto_records(staff_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_staff_shifts_staff_id ON staff_shifts(staff_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_staff_shifts_target_date ON staff_shifts(target_date);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_support_plans_client_id ON support_plans(client_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_support_plans_client_status ON support_plans(client_id, status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_support_plans_client_date ON support_plans(client_id, plan_date);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_support_records_client_id ON support_records(client_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_support_records_record_date ON support_records(record_date);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_support_records_client_date ON support_records(client_id, record_date);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_daily_logs_date ON daily_logs(record_date);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_daily_logs_dept_date ON daily_logs(department_id, record_date);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payslips_target_month ON monthly_payslips(target_month);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_telework_records_client_id ON telework_support_records(client_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_telework_records_record_date ON telework_support_records(record_date);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_monthly_provision_records_client_month ON monthly_provision_records(client_id, target_month);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_daily_provision_details_client_month ON daily_provision_details(client_id, target_month);
  `);

  // 3. billing_rules の初期シード
  try {
    const insertRuleStmt = db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO billing_rules (rule_key, category, rule_name, rule_value, unit_label, description, effective_from)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      ON CONFLICT(rule_key) DO NOTHING
    `);
    db.transaction(() => {
      for (const r of DEFAULT_BILLING_RULES) {
        insertRuleStmt.run(r.rule_key, r.category, r.rule_name, r.rule_value, r.unit_label, r.description, r.effective_from);
      }
    })();
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to seed billing_rules:', e);
  }

  // 4. regional_minimum_wages の初期シード
  try {
    const insertWageStmt = db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO regional_minimum_wages (pref_code, pref_name, minimum_wage, effective_date)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
      ON CONFLICT(pref_code) DO UPDATE SET
        pref_name = excluded.pref_name,
        minimum_wage = excluded.minimum_wage,
        effective_date = excluded.effective_date,
        updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    `);
    db.transaction(() => {
      for (const w of DEFAULT_REGIONAL_MINIMUM_WAGES) {
        insertWageStmt.run(w.pref_code, w.pref_name, w.minimum_wage, w.effective_date);
      }
    })();
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to seed regional_minimum_wages:', e);
  }

  // 5. service_code_master の初期シード
  try {
    const insertCodeStmt = db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO service_code_master (service_type_code, service_item_code, service_name, unit_score)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
      ON CONFLICT(service_type_code, service_item_code) DO NOTHING
    `);
    db.transaction(() => {
      for (const c of DEFAULT_SERVICE_CODES) {
        insertCodeStmt.run(c.service_type_code, c.service_item_code, c.service_name, c.unit_score);
      }
      db.prepare(`
        UPDATE service_code_master 
        SET unit_score = 0, service_name = '就労Ａ型賃金向上達成指導員配置加算（包括化廃止）' 
        WHERE service_type_code = '43' AND service_item_code = '6300'
      `).run();
    })();
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to seed service_code_master:', e);
  }

  // 6. jurisdictions の初期シード
  try {
    const insertJurisdictionStmt = db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO jurisdictions (id, jurisdiction_type, jurisdiction_code, name, local_rules_json)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      ON CONFLICT(jurisdiction_code) DO NOTHING
    `);
    db.transaction(() => {
      for (const j of DEFAULT_JURISDICTIONS) {
        insertJurisdictionStmt.run(j.id, j.jurisdiction_type, j.jurisdiction_code, j.name, j.local_rules_json);
      }
    })();
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to seed jurisdictions:', e);
  }

  // 7. backup_settings 初期レコード
  try {
    db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO backup_settings (id, cloud_provider, auto_sync_enabled, auto_sync_interval_hours)
      VALUES ('default', 'none', 0, 24)
      ON CONFLICT(id) DO NOTHING
    `).run();
  } catch (e) {}

  // 7.5 システムメタデータテーブル（DBファイルの事業種別を不変保持・復元時クロスチェック用）
  try {
    db.exec(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS app_system_meta (
        meta_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        meta_value TEXT NOT NULL,
        updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
      );
    `);
    db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO app_system_meta (meta_key, meta_value)
      VALUES ('service_mode', ?)
      ON CONFLICT(meta_key) DO UPDATE SET meta_value = excluded.meta_value, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    `).run(mode);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to initialize app_system_meta:', e);
  }

  // 8. 事業種別別初期シードの投入
  if (mode === 'type_b') {
    seedTypeBInitialData(db);
  } else {
    seedTypeAInitialData(db);
  }

  // 8.2 内蔵NotebookLM 公式資料ナレッジベースの初期シード（A型/B型完全分離）
  ensureKnowledgeDocumentsSeeded(db, mode);

  // 8.5 facility_settings に service_type および B型目標工賃・加算カラムを保持
  try {
    const facilityColumns = db.prepare(`PRAGMA table_info(facility_settings)`).all().map((c: any) => c.name);
    if (!facilityColumns.includes('service_type')) {
      db.prepare(`ALTER TABLE facility_settings ADD COLUMN service_type TEXT DEFAULT '${mode}'`).run();
    }
    if (!facilityColumns.includes('target_wage_monthly_target')) {
      db.prepare(`ALTER TABLE facility_settings ADD COLUMN target_wage_monthly_target INTEGER DEFAULT 20000`).run();
    }
    if (!facilityColumns.includes('target_wage_instructor_addition')) {
      db.prepare(`ALTER TABLE facility_settings ADD COLUMN target_wage_instructor_addition INTEGER DEFAULT 0`).run();
    }
    if (!facilityColumns.includes('target_wage_achievement_addition')) {
      db.prepare(`ALTER TABLE facility_settings ADD COLUMN target_wage_achievement_addition INTEGER DEFAULT 0`).run();
    }
    if (!facilityColumns.includes('community_cooperation_addition')) {
      db.prepare(`ALTER TABLE facility_settings ADD COLUMN community_cooperation_addition INTEGER DEFAULT 0`).run();
    }
    db.prepare(`UPDATE facility_settings SET service_type = ? WHERE id = 'default'`).run(mode);
  } catch (e) {}

  // 9. B型工賃・多様な給与形態に対応するためのカラム拡張（安全なALTER TABLE）
  try {
    const clientsColumns = db.prepare(`PRAGMA table_info(clients)`).all().map((c: any) => c.name);
    if (!clientsColumns.includes('wage_type')) {
      db.prepare(`ALTER TABLE clients ADD COLUMN wage_type TEXT DEFAULT 'hourly'`).run();
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  try {
    const contractsColumns = db.prepare(`PRAGMA table_info(employment_contracts)`).all().map((c: any) => c.name);
    if (!contractsColumns.includes('daily_wage')) {
      db.prepare(`ALTER TABLE employment_contracts ADD COLUMN daily_wage REAL DEFAULT 0`).run();
    }
    if (!contractsColumns.includes('piecework_unit_price')) {
      db.prepare(`ALTER TABLE employment_contracts ADD COLUMN piecework_unit_price REAL DEFAULT 0`).run();
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  try {
    const payrollSettingsColumns = db.prepare(`PRAGMA table_info(payroll_settings)`).all().map((c: any) => c.name);
    if (!payrollSettingsColumns.includes('wage_type')) {
      db.prepare(`ALTER TABLE payroll_settings ADD COLUMN wage_type TEXT DEFAULT 'hourly'`).run();
    }
    if (!payrollSettingsColumns.includes('daily_wage')) {
      db.prepare(`ALTER TABLE payroll_settings ADD COLUMN daily_wage REAL DEFAULT 0`).run();
    }
    if (!payrollSettingsColumns.includes('piecework_unit_price')) {
      db.prepare(`ALTER TABLE payroll_settings ADD COLUMN piecework_unit_price REAL DEFAULT 0`).run();
    }
    if (!payrollSettingsColumns.includes('piecework_quantity')) {
      db.prepare(`ALTER TABLE payroll_settings ADD COLUMN piecework_quantity REAL DEFAULT 0`).run();
    }
    if (!payrollSettingsColumns.includes('ability_allowance')) {
      db.prepare(`ALTER TABLE payroll_settings ADD COLUMN ability_allowance REAL DEFAULT 0`).run();
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  try {
    const monthlyPayslipsColumns = db.prepare(`PRAGMA table_info(monthly_payslips)`).all().map((c: any) => c.name);
    if (!monthlyPayslipsColumns.includes('wage_type')) {
      db.prepare(`ALTER TABLE monthly_payslips ADD COLUMN wage_type TEXT DEFAULT 'hourly'`).run();
    }
    if (!monthlyPayslipsColumns.includes('daily_wage_payment')) {
      db.prepare(`ALTER TABLE monthly_payslips ADD COLUMN daily_wage_payment REAL DEFAULT 0`).run();
    }
    if (!monthlyPayslipsColumns.includes('piecework_payment')) {
      db.prepare(`ALTER TABLE monthly_payslips ADD COLUMN piecework_payment REAL DEFAULT 0`).run();
    }
    if (!monthlyPayslipsColumns.includes('ability_allowance')) {
      db.prepare(`ALTER TABLE monthly_payslips ADD COLUMN ability_allowance REAL DEFAULT 0`).run();
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}

// 起動時にデフォルトDB（A型）を安全に初期化
try {
  getDatabase('type_a');
} catch (e) {
  console.error('Initial DB init error:', e);
}

/**
 * 透過的Proxy: 呼び出しコンテキストのサービスモード（type_a / type_b）に応じて
 * 自動的に適切な SQLite データベースインスタンスへディスパッチする。
 * 既存の全ページ・全アクション（数百箇所）を1行も書き換えることなく完全分離を実現。
 */
export const db = new Proxy({} as Database.Database, {
  get(target, prop, receiver) {
    const activeDb = getDatabase();
    const val = (activeDb as any)[prop];
    if (typeof val === 'function') {
      return function(...args: any[]) {
        return val.apply(activeDb, args);
      };
    }
    return val;
  },
  set(target, prop, value, receiver) {
    const activeDb = getDatabase();
    (activeDb as any)[prop] = value;
    return true;
  }
});
